COHERENCE FRAMEWORK v23
==========================================================================
FORMAT RULES [operative]
  Line width: 108 chars max. Separators exempt.
  Blank lines: max 2 consecutive.
  Double separators: prohibited [audit before save].
  WIP content: staging section only, never inline with confirmed theory.
  Version header: no change log. Version number + format rules only.
  Section headers: COHERENCE FRAMEWORK vN -- SECTION [NAME].
  Case entries: [CASE:NAME] header, S-rating required, cross-ref at end.
  Abbreviations: GA/BI/SR/TM/TD/GC/SP/CC/MI/CL/RI/TS/SA/CAG/FC/
    MSC/ECC/ICD/ECD/CT [see NAME COMPRESSION POLICY in Section One].
  Wrap at 108: applies to all body text, definitions, case entries,
    weapon entries, policy positions, and WIP staging content.
==========================================================================
STANDING RULES [v22 -- permanent]
==========================================================================
VERSION CHANGE LOGIC:
  Section One [FOUNDATION] is highest-integrity. All downstream
  sections depend on foundation stability. Dependency chain:
    Section One → Section Two → Section Three → Section Four
    → Sections Five and Six → Section Seven → Cases.
  Science [Section Seven] depends on Section One only.

  RULE: Any addition or change to Section One is WIP-only
  until both conditions met:
    [1] Formal version change declared.
    [2] New session opens after the update.
  Reason: theory changes need a fresh instance to stress-test
  without current session context bias. Long sessions produce
  structural blindness [confirmed v16 review]. New session after
  theory update is the instrument for catching integration errors
  before they propagate downstream.

NEAR-MISS MECHANISM HANDLING [v23 -- permanent]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  RULE: any anti-coherence mechanism encountered in any case,
  reading, or analysis that resembles a named weapon or tool closely
  enough to risk misidentification, but does not match on close
  inspection, must be logged to WIP 34 [NEAR-MISS MECHANISM REGISTER].
  Two failure modes are both prohibited:
    [1] Forcing the near-miss into an existing tag to close a gap
      or complete a count. A near-miss is not a match; tagging it
      as one corrupts the case library and the tool/weapon
      calibration built on it.
    [2] Silently discarding the near-miss because it doesn't fit
      cleanly anywhere. An unmapped mechanism is a real finding,
      not noise -- some of the framework's most theoretically
      interesting material surfaces exactly this way [confirmed
      v23: Theranos's evaluator-side status weighting was caught
      only on a deliberate retroactive scan after being omitted
      from the original case-writing pass].
  WIP 34 itself never closes and is never reduced to a stub --
  it is exempt from the CLOSED WIPs stub-reduction rule above.
  Individual candidates within it close when investigated and
  resolved [named, folded into an existing entry, or ruled out];
  the register persists indefinitely as the standing destination
  for this category of finding.

SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION [v22 -- permanent]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Seven sections plus Cases, Archive, Reference. Boundaries are
  load-bearing. Do not collapse across section types.
  Content spanning sections: PRIMARY home in most specific
  applicable section; CROSS-REFERENCES in all others.
  Never duplicate substantial content. Cross-reference is the
  link; primary entry is the truth.
  ORDER: put similar close → cross-reference if similar but
    not same → merge if nine tests pass [T1-T9 all required]
    → remove if five tests pass [R1-R5; harder than merge].
  Merge is half-remove [absorbed entry lost from location].
  Both need high bars. Remove is harder than merge.
  No compress. Recovery sessions: merge and removal suspended.
  CLOSED WIPs: reduce staging entry to stub immediately.
    Stub removed after next session R1-R4 verification.
    [STUB -- VERIFY AND REMOVE vN+1] is the flag.
  GENERAL ORDER: simple to complex throughout. Definitions
    before findings; general before specific; confirmed before
    candidate. Specific ordering declared per section where
    a different ordering applies.
  See SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [Section Three].
  Boundary violations: flag [BOUNDARY VIOLATION -- belongs in
  SECTION X]. Move on next version increment. Do not move
  mid-session without full cross-ref audit.

  SECTION ONE -- FOUNDATION [SEC:FOUNDATION]
    Scope: preconditions, level theory [L1/L2/L3/L3.5],
      nested cycle model, axis definitions [CENTER/TRUTH/
      OUROBOROS], core operational definitions [EVIL/GOOD].
    Test: does this entry define what the framework IS?
    Violation: confirmed findings, weapons entries, reading
      procedures, political readings placed inline with
      foundation theory.

  SECTION TWO -- FINDINGS [SEC:FINDINGS]
    Scope: confirmed structural findings [S3+]; candidate
      findings [WIP promoted from staging]; stubs.
    Test: does this entry report what the framework FOUND
      when applied to sufficient evidence?
    Order: foundational findings → level-specific findings
      → mimicry/variant subtypes → candidates [simple to
      complex].
    Violation: speculative WIP entries, single-case
      observations, applied readings placed inline.

  SECTION THREE -- MECHANISMS [SEC:MECHANISMS]
    Scope: tools index [correction], weapons index [damage],
      merge/admin logic, name compression, weapon stack
      detection, case strength rating.
    Test: does this entry describe a deployable mechanism?
    Order: tools before weapons; within weapons: high damage
      before low damage.
    Violation: theory definitions, political readings, case
      entries placed in mechanism indexes.

  SECTION FOUR -- PROCEDURE [SEC:PROCEDURE]
    Scope: epistemic status rules, scoring procedure [WIP 18],
      reading procedure [12 steps], axis analysis social risk,
      trajectory indicators, output format.
    Test: does this entry describe HOW TO APPLY the framework?
    Order: epistemic rules → scoring → reading → output.
    Violation: theory definitions, mechanism entries, political
      readings placed in procedure blocks.

  SECTION FIVE -- POLITICAL THEORY [SEC:POLITICAL_THEORY]
    Scope: structural political definitions, centralization
      rubric, framework political prior, structural political
      findings [routing boundary, somatic forcing,
      revolutionary violence, mass destruction].
    Test: does this entry state a structural claim about
      political systems that applies across cases?
    Order: definitions → rubrics → findings → edge cases.
    Violation: specific case readings, named political actors
      or events placed inline with political theory.

  SECTION SIX -- POLITICS IN ACTION [SEC:POLITICS_IN_ACTION]
    Scope: applied political readings. Each entry: boundary
      declared, analyst prior stated, T3 applied.
    Test: does this entry apply framework to a specific
      political case or phenomenon?
    Violation: structural theory placed without case anchor.

  SECTION SEVEN -- SCIENCE [SEC:SCIENCE]
    Scope: science entries classified CONFIRMATION or
      ILLUSTRATION. All entries require classification.
    CONFIRMATION: independent domain + mechanism convergence
      + framework-independent falsifiability. All three.
    ILLUSTRATION: framework vocabulary applied to real
      finding. Does not add proof coverage.
    Violation: unclassified entries, theory additions.

  CASES [SEC:CASES]
    Scope: [CASE:NAME] entries with S-ratings, PRIMARY_Q,
      REF cross-references.
    Violation: theory or procedure content without case anchor.

  ARCHIVE [SEC:ARCHIVE]: read-only. Do not add or edit.

  REFERENCE [SEC:REFERENCE]
    Scope: quick reference glossary, open questions,
      progress tracking.
    Violation: theory additions placed in reference.

WIP THEORY PLACEMENT RULE:
  WIP theory additions do NOT go inline with confirmed theory.
  They go in WIP THEORY STAGING [below Standing Rules, before
  Section One]. Staging holds candidates until promoted.
  Promotion requires: new session review + primary case or
  falsification condition tested + integration errors cleared.
  On promotion: move entry to correct section [Section One for
  definitions; Section Two for findings; Section Three for
  mechanisms; Section Four for procedures], remove WIP tag,
  log in version header, trigger version bump.

WHAT CAN BE FINALIZED IN-SESSION:
  Section Seven [SCIENCE] additions: low integration risk. Finalize.
  Case library additions: low-medium risk. Finalize.
  S-rating passes: mechanical. Finalize.
  Section Six [POLITICS IN ACTION] updates with epistemic tags:
    medium risk. Finalize with tags applied.
  Section Three [MECHANISMS] additions [tools/weapons]:
    medium risk. Finalize if boundary protection respected.

WHAT REQUIRES WIP STAGING + NEW SESSION:
  New named qualities [Section One].
  Amended core definitions [Section One].
  New structural findings [Section Two].
  New level designations [L4.5, L5, etc.] [Section One].
  Any change to nested cycle model or tower structure [Section One].
  New reading procedures or scoring changes [Section Four].
  New structural political theory [Section Five].

VERSION CHANGE TRIGGER:
  At least one WIP item confirmed [primary case found,
  falsification condition tested, integration error corrected].
  OR: accumulated additions reach structural review threshold
  [>500 lines added to theory since last version].

NEW SESSION SUGGESTION TRIGGER:
  Work all actionable WIPs in current session before suggesting
  a new session. Actionable = has candidate case, primary case
  confirmed, integration check ready, or falsification condition
  testable. Non-actionable = dormant, waiting on external case,
  or no testable condition available.
  Suggest a new session only when ALL of the following:
    [1] All actionable WIPs have been worked this session.
    [2] Remaining open WIPs are non-actionable [dormant /
        waiting on case / no testable condition].
    [3] Any theory-level additions made this session require
        fresh instance integration check before Section One
        amendment [WIP 17 class: significant cross-section
        integration implications].
  Do NOT suggest new session mid-session solely because context
  bias is accumulating -- complete actionable work first, then
  hand off. Context bias is managed by completing work, not by
  stopping early.
  Suggestion format: name what was completed, name remaining
  non-actionable WIPs, state what the new session should
  do first.

SESSION DISCIPLINE:
  Start of session: read version header + standing rules +
    WIP list first.
  During session: work all actionable WIPs before closing.
    Actionable = candidate case, primary case confirmed,
    integration check ready, or falsification condition
    testable. Do not stop at first completion.
  End of session: update WIP list, check new session trigger.
    New session only when all actionable WIPs exhausted and
    remaining items are non-actionable or require fresh
    instance for cross-section integration check.
  Theory changes: WIP staging mandatory same session, never
    inline with confirmed theory.
  Fresh instance for: WIP 17-class items [significant cross-
    section integration implications requiring full framework
    context]; structural review passes across all sections.

  CARRIER ESCAPE [named threshold condition -- v20]:
    L4.5 requires methodology to escape carrier dependency and
    become self-reproducing in substrate independently. Near-cases
    confirm: methodology quality is insufficient. Athens, Islamic
    Golden Age, Scottish Enlightenment all had the methodology.
    None achieved carrier escape before termination. The threshold
    is not methodology presence -- it is methodology survival after
    carrier destruction.
    Positive case requirement [three conditions, all required]:
    [1] Developed distributed correction capacity.
    [2] Survived destruction of carrier institutions.
    [3] Became self-reproducing in substrate without specialized
        formation requirements at civilizational scale.
    Mathematical proof culture: meets [1] and [2]; fails [3]
      [formation requirement gates substrate access at scale].
    STOICISM [fourth near-case, v23]: survived destruction of
      original Stoa carrier; propagated across Roman institutions
      without deliberate institutional preservation [unlike
      Jesuits -- see below]. Meets [1] [distributed correction
      capacity across Stoic schools] and partially [2] [original
      carrier destroyed; no deliberate restoration]. Fails [3]
      [reproduction still required literate formation class;
      never became self-reproducing at civilizational scale
      without specialist access to texts and teachers]. Same
      condition [3] failure as existing three near-cases.
    JESUIT ORDER [evaluated v23, ruled out]: suppression 1773
      [Pope Clement XIV] looks like carrier destruction, but
      survival mechanism was alternative carrier adoption [Russia
      and Prussia refused to implement suppression]. Condition
      [2] fails -- methodology survived because new carriers
      stepped in, not because it escaped carrier dependency.
      Not a near-case match; ruled out.
    Internet-era candidates [Wikipedia governance, distributed
      open methodology]: structurally capable of meeting all
      three; too young for generational confirmation. Earliest
      viable confirmation window 2030-2040.
    No confirmed positive case meeting all three conditions.
    Falsification: documented methodology meeting all three
      conditions with generational confirmation record.

WIP 16: ASYMMETRY TYPES [predatory / developmental / compatibility]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

[VERIFICATION NOTE v22 -- this session, added after original status
block above; original block left untouched per VERIFICATION WORK GOES
IN NEW, NOT OLD]:
  Compatibility type promoted to CONFIRMED this session: real-world
    primary case CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY [Double Empathy Problem --
    Milton 2012] written; five-condition instrument run formally;
    S3 achieved. All three asymmetry types [predatory, developmental,

  STUBBED v23 [later session]: taxonomy framing checked for presence
    in Section One -- found and updated [ASYMMETRY TYPES entry now

  PROBLEM STATEMENT:
    The framework currently collapses all information asymmetry
    into MSC [Measurement Surface Capture] or the extraction
    operational definition [trickery as foundation]. This is
    insufficient. Three structurally distinct asymmetry types
    produce the same surface reading [information withheld;
    receiver does not understand the full situation] but have
    different mechanisms, different intervention paths, and
    different ethical status.

  THREE CANDIDATE TYPES:

  PREDATORY ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- existing framework]:
    Information withheld serves operator at receiver's expense.
    Operator benefits from the ignorance persisting.
    Tell: closing the asymmetry would damage operator's position.
    Intervention: MSC counter [independent measurement].
    Example: Kyubey withholding grief seed endpoint if purpose
      is to maintain willing contractors. Standard extraction.

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [candidate -- not yet confirmed]:
    Information withheld serves receiver's eventual capacity.
    Asymmetry designed to close over time as instrument develops.
    Operator benefits from the asymmetry closing -- a developed
    receiver is the intended output, not a permanently dependent
    one. Tell: operator actively works toward asymmetry closing;
    full information is the goal, not the threat.
    Examples [candidate]: parent withholding mortality detail
      from toddler; apprenticeship; initiation architecture
      where sequence and readiness determine transmission timing.
    Failure mode: developmental asymmetry used as cover for
      predatory asymmetry. Tell: asymmetry never closes; operator
      benefits from continued dependency.
    Intervention: examine whether asymmetry is closing. If not
      closing on any timeline: revert to predatory reading.
    Primary case needed. Not yet confirmed as distinct type.

  COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [candidate -- not yet confirmed; renamed
    and sharpened from SCALE ASYMMETRY v22]:
    Asymmetry produced by structural incompatibility between
    operational frames. Not withheld by choice [predatory].
    Not withheld pending readiness [developmental]. Not
    transmittable given any amount of time, honesty, or
    effort -- because the gap is not informational. The
    frames operate on different axes that do not map onto
    each other at any transmission resolution.
    Key distinction from scale asymmetry [prior framing]:
      The problem is not size or time. It is compatibility.
      Two systems can be similar in scale and still be
      frame-incompatible. Two systems can be vastly
      different in scale and still achieve frame
      compatibility [developmental asymmetry closing].
      Scale is a common cause of incompatibility but not
      the mechanism. The mechanism is frame structure.
    Tell: honest transmission produces the same response
      as deception. Instrument registers violation or
      incomprehension regardless of operator intent or
      information completeness. Additional time, volume,
      or honesty does not close the gap.
    Critical distinction from predatory: operator has no
      structural interest in incomprehension persisting.
      Closing the gap would not damage operator's position
      [if anything it would improve it]. The incomprehension
      is not instrumentally useful -- it is structurally
      unavoidable.

    MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [v22 -- five conditions, all required]:
      [1] Honest transmission attempted -- operator does not
          withhold.
      [2] Receiver registers violation or incomprehension
          regardless of transmission quality.
      [3] Additional time, volume, or honesty does not close
          the gap.
      [4] Operator has no structural interest in the
          incomprehension persisting -- closing it would
          not damage operator's position.
      [5] Frame incompatibility traceable to structural
          difference in operational level or axis, not
          information deficit.
      All five required. Any one absent: revert to predatory
      reading. Condition 4 is the critical predatory/
      compatibility discriminator.

    PRIMARY CASE CANDIDATE [fictional -- S2 ceiling]:
      YALDABAOTH / DEMIURGE [Gnostic theology]:
        Creates suffering world not from malice but from
        being a lower-order entity that mistakes itself
        for the highest. Frame incompatibility runs in
        both directions: Yaldabaoth cannot receive the
        Pleroma frame [ontological ceiling of its
        instrument]; humans cannot receive the Demiurge's
        operational frame [entropy accounting at
        cosmological scale reads as cruelty from inside
        individual formation-window experience].
        Condition 1: transmission attempted -- Gnostic
          texts consistently frame Yaldabaoth as acting
          from its own operational logic, not concealment.
        Condition 2: human instruments register violation
          regardless of Yaldabaoth's operational
          consistency.
        Condition 3: more information about cosmological
          necessity does not resolve the violation
          reading from inside individual frame.
        Condition 4: Yaldabaoth has no structural interest
          in human ignorance of the Pleroma -- the
          ignorance is a byproduct of its own ontological
          ceiling, not an instrument for maintaining
          contractor supply [contrast: Kyubey].
        Condition 5: frame incompatibility traceable to
          Yaldabaoth operating at cosmological axis while
          human instruments operate at formation-window
          axis. Not an information deficit -- a structural
          axis mismatch.
        S2 [fictional; framework-consistent; no real-world
          anchor]. Sufficient for candidate confirmation.
          Primary real-world case still needed for S3.
        Note: Yaldabaoth also fails its own frame --
          cannot receive Pleroma. Compatibility asymmetry
          is bidirectional in this case. The Demiurge is
          not a god looking down; it is a mid-level
          entity looking neither up nor down accurately.

    KYUBEY RE-EXAMINATION [ambiguous -- condition 4 fails]:
      Kyubey [Puella Magi Madoka Magica]:
        Evidence for compatibility asymmetry: attempts
          honest explanation; explanation produces horror
          not understanding; response functionally
          identical whether Kyubey explains or withholds.
          Frames [thermodynamic entropy accounting /
          individual formation-window experience]
          structurally incompatible.
        Evidence against [condition 4 fails]: targets
          formation-window girls specifically; could
          target older instruments with higher frame
          compatibility; ignorance may be instrumentally
          useful [maintains willing contractors], not
          just structurally unavoidable. Closing the
          gap would cost Kyubey contractor supply.
        Resolution: Kyubey is a MIXED CASE. Compatibility
          asymmetry present [frames genuinely incompatible
          at the thermodynamic scale]; predatory mechanism
          also present [targets instruments where
          incompatibility is maximized; structural interest
          in incomprehension persisting]. Both readings
          simultaneously true. Mixed case does not
          falsify compatibility asymmetry as category --
          it confirms that predatory and compatibility
          mechanisms can co-deploy. Yaldabaoth is the
          cleaner candidate precisely because condition 4
          is unambiguous.

    OTHER CANDIDATE CASES:
      Some religious transmission architecture where
        cosmological frame and individual ethical frame
        are genuinely incompatible [not gatekeeping --
        structural axis mismatch].
      L4.5 systems operating at civilizational scale
        where individual instruments cannot hold the
        population-level correction frame regardless
        of information volume.
      Real-world primary case requirement: documented
        system where honest transmission across confirmed
        frame incompatibility produced no functional
        understanding despite repeated genuine attempts,
        and operator had no structural interest in the
        incomprehension persisting.

    REAL-WORLD PRIMARY CASE CANDIDATES [v22 -- evaluate]:

      AUTISM / NEUROTYPICAL COMMUNICATION [strong candidate;
      track Double Empathy Problem -- Milton 2012]:
        Frame incompatibility: confirmed and named
          independently [Double Empathy Problem -- Milton
          2012; frame incompatibility bidirectional; NT
          instruments fail autistic frame; autistic
          instruments fail NT frame; neither is
          deficiency -- structural axis mismatch].
        Condition 1: honest transmission attempted --
          autistic instruments routinely attempt maximal
          information transmission; NT instruments do
          same.
        Condition 2: receiver registers violation or
          incomprehension regardless -- NT instruments
          read autistic communication as rude, cold,
          weird regardless of intent; autistic
          instruments read NT communication as
          confusing, arbitrary, inconsistent regardless
          of NT intent. Confirmed bidirectional.
        Condition 3: additional time, volume, honesty
          does not close gap -- decades of social
          skills training attempts confirm; training
          produces masking [FC] not genuine frame
          compatibility.
        Condition 4: neither party has structural
          interest in incomprehension persisting.
          Autistic instruments do not benefit from NT
          incomprehension. NT instruments do not
          benefit from autistic incomprehension [in
          most contexts -- predatory exploitation is
          the exception, not the structural baseline].
        Condition 5: frame incompatibility traceable
          to structural difference in processing axis
          [sensory, social, sequential vs gestalt,
          direct vs indirect communication] not
          information deficit.
        BIDIRECTIONALITY [important]: Double Empathy
          Problem names this explicitly -- the
          incompatibility runs both ways. Same as
          Yaldabaoth note [bidirectional]. NT
          instrument registers autistic communication
          as violation; autistic instrument registers
          NT communication as confusing. Neither
          frame can fully receive the other's output
          regardless of honest intent.
        Masking as FC [load-bearing note]: when
          autistic instruments learn to pass in NT
          environments through masking [performing NT
          communication patterns], this is Formation
          Capture at the communication layer -- axis
          is genuine [autistic]; surface is captured
          [NT performance]. Masking is not frame
          compatibility; it is axis suppression under
          social pressure. This confirms Condition 3
          -- masking is not understanding; the frame
          incompatibility persists beneath the mask.
        S3 CANDIDATE: E3 [real-world; Milton 2012 +
          subsequent research in autism studies;
          independent confirmation across multiple
          research groups]; M3 [mechanism: structural
          processing axis mismatch; not information
          deficit; Double Empathy Problem named the
          mechanism]; F2 [falsification condition:
          documented case where prolonged genuine
          frame exposure produced mutual understanding
          without masking; not yet confirmed as
          typical outcome].
        NEXT STEP: verify Milton [2012] mechanism
          trace against five-condition instrument;
          confirm no structural interest in
          incomprehension persisting at population
          level [some actors benefit from autistic
          incomprehension -- predatory operators;
          exclude from primary case]; promote to S3
          if conditions confirmed.

      EXPERT/LAYPERSON EXTREME SPECIALIZATION [weaker;
      condition 4 often fails]:
        Frequent honest transmission failure [physics
          to policy; medicine to patient]. But:
          experts often have structural interest in
          incomprehension persisting [authority
          maintenance; professional boundary control].
          Condition 4 typically fails. Revert to
          predatory reading or mixed case.
          Exception: genuine cases where expert
          benefits from layperson understanding and
          cannot achieve it [public health
          communication; climate science; vaccine
          hesitancy]. Track separately.

      ANIMAL COGNITION [edge case; T3 High]:
        Ape language research, elephant cognition --
          confirmed frame incompatibility; honest
          transmission attempted; limited functional
          understanding achieved. Operator had no
          structural interest in incomprehension
          persisting. Condition 4 passes.
        Problem: T3 very high [we cannot verify
          what the animal frame actually is; we can
          only observe behavioral outputs]. The
          frame incompatibility may be informational
          not structural [we don't know how to
          transmit; the animal might understand if
          we could]. Conditions 1 and 5 uncertain.
          Not strong enough for primary case.

    STATUS UPDATE [v22]: Double Empathy Problem /
      autism-NT communication is the strongest
      real-world primary case candidate identified.
      Five-condition check passes preliminarily.
      Pending: verify Milton [2012] + subsequent
      research confirms all five conditions without
      contamination by predatory actors in the same
      domain. If confirmed: S3 real-world primary
      case achieved; WIP 16 compatibility asymmetry
      promotes from candidate to confirmed.

  FRAMEWORK IMPLICATIONS IF CONFIRMED:
    Extraction operational definition requires amendment:
      current definition does not distinguish predatory
      from compatibility asymmetry. A system operating
      under genuine frame incompatibility may meet surface
      criteria for extraction without meeting structural
      criteria [Yaldabaoth: reads as extraction from
      inside individual frame; mechanism is not trickery].
    MSC extraction mechanism definition requires sub-typing:
      developmental and compatibility asymmetry may not be
      extraction mechanisms -- may be structural properties
      of certain coherence configurations.
    Mixed cases [Kyubey] require dual-mechanism notation:
      compatibility asymmetry present + predatory mechanism
      co-deployed. Not a contradiction -- a confirmed
      interaction pattern.
    Historical initiation architecture requires re-
      examination: most high-level knowledge transmission
      operated on asymmetry assumptions. Current framework
      classifies all gatekeeping as extraction-adjacent.
      Developmental asymmetry [confirmed] recovers some as
      legitimate. Compatibility asymmetry [candidate] may
      recover additional cases where frame incompatibility
      was structural, not instrumental.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITIONS [provisional]:
    Predatory: falsified by documented case where operator
      benefited from asymmetry closing and worked toward it.
    Developmental: falsified by no documented case where
      asymmetry closed serving receiver without operator
      benefit from closing.
    Compatibility: falsified by documented case where honest
      transmission across confirmed frame incompatibility
      produced functional understanding in receiver, OR by
      documented case where operator had structural interest
      in incomprehension persisting [reverts to predatory].

  MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [developmental type -- v20]:
    Four-condition test [all required for developmental
    classification]:
    [1] Asymmetry maintained on receiver's developmental
        schedule, not operator's convenience.
    [2] Asymmetry closes when receiver reaches capacity.
    [3] Closure serves receiver's autonomous function.
    [4] Operator has no structural interest in asymmetry
        persisting beyond receiver's readiness.
    All four required. Any one absent: revert to predatory
    reading regardless of stated intent.
    Behavioral test, not intent test -- stated intent to
    develop the receiver is compatible with predatory
    mechanism. Test the conditions, not the claim.

  PRIMARY CASE CONFIRMED [developmental type -- v20]:

    ignorance persisting is the load-bearing distinction];
    MSC clarifying note added [developmental withholding
    is not MSC deployment -- mechanism inverted].
    Developmental type ready for promotion to candidate
    pending new session stress-test.
    Scale type still needs primary case.
    Predatory type already confirmed [existing framework].

WIP 23: PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES [Nascent→Axial CONFIRMED v23;
  promote next session]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: NASCENT→AXIAL TRANSITION CONFIRMED v23 [analyst suggestion
  this session: children]. Self-Determination Theory [Deci & Ryan
  1985-2020; Grolnick, Deci & Ryan 1997 longitudinal] documents the
  Nascent→Axial transition as developmental internalization: external
  regulation [no axis, Structural Coherence analog] → introjection
  [axis forming, external locus of causality still, Nascent] →
  integrated regulation [GA established, Axial]. Primary case written:
  CASE:SDT_CHILD_INTERNALIZATION [S:3]. Mechanism match is structural,
  not analogical -- SDT's introjection state matches Nascent's
  definition exactly ["not really experienced as part of the self"],
  and the transition to integration matches GENUINE AXIS established.
  Controlling environments producing introjection rather than
  integration is the empirical confirmation of WIP 23's named primary
  vulnerability [axis formation inside controlled environment produces
  installed rather than genuine GA]. Measurement instrument [Perceived
  Locus of Causality scale] gives behavioral operationalization.
  E4+M4+F3 profile -- strongest E/M/F of any case added this session.
  NEXT STEPS: vocabulary integration complete; primary Nascent→Axial
  case now confirmed; promote PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES content to
  Section One integration check next dedicated session. The content
  in the WIP body below is ready for promotion; needs cross-section
  integration check against current Section One geometry entries
  before moving. Flag WIP 23 as PROMOTE NEXT SESSION.]

  PRE-AXIAL INPUT / OUTPUT / CONNECTION MODEL [v12]:
    What pre-axial systems receive, process, and output.
    Input: environmental signal [resource, threat, contact].
      No axis to filter -- raw reception. CC fires against
      baseline deviation not toward goal.
    Output: SP [surplus if cycle runs] + SR [substrate
      maintained]. No GA to direct output. Pure functional
      generation.
    Connections: BI [boundary determines what counts as inside].
      GC [cycle determines what gets processed]. No TS -- no
      axis to transmit. Generation and correction without
      orientation.
    Q-stack present: GC/SP/CC/BI/SR/SD fully operable.
    Q-stack absent: GA/MI/TS/CL/SA/CAG -- inapplicable by
      category [no axis to examine, measure, or transmit].

  FOUR CATEGORIES [v11]:
    STRUCTURAL COHERENCE: generates surplus and self-corrects
      without orientation, measurement surface, or transmission
      axis. Qualities GC/SP/CC/BI/PR present as functional
      analogs. GA/MI/TS/CL inapplicable by category.
      Geometric form: sphere [no hole = no axis].
      Cannot achieve False Coherence [requires axis to fake one].
      Cannot deploy weapons against itself [no MI to capture].
      Cases: ant colonies, mycorrhizal networks.
    NASCENT COHERENCE: axis forming via relational or
      environmental contact. Not yet self-sustaining. GA
      developing, not present. Primary vulnerability: axis
      formation inside controlled environment produces apparent
      GA that is structurally installed -- indistinguishable
      internally from genuine GA. Pre-Fertile Lock.
      Geometric form: polarized sphere [poles developing,
      hole not yet formed].
      Only documented Nascent case: Meruem [HxH]. Dies before
      Axial completion. No confirmed Nascent → Axial transition.
    SEEDED NASCENT [v9 -- not in v22]:
      Pre-axial entity with directional goals embedded at
      creation. Not fully captured [GA hasn't stabilized]
      but oriented. CC intact; built-in goals function as
      constitutive thought-stopping against specific targets.
      Self-suppression without ongoing enforcement cost.
      Can act against controlling system but generally doesn't.
      Distinct from Nascent [no embedded directional goals]
      and Fertile Lock [full axial transmission].
      PRIMARY proof: Dream Seeds [WATF].
      Parallel: Marleyan warrior program [AoT -- partial].
      V22 mapping: closest to Formation Capture during
      Nascent window, but distinct -- goals are embedded
      structurally, not installed through relational contact.
    AXIAL COHERENCE: GA fully established. Full quality stack
      applies. Torus geometry. Standard framework measurement.

  COHERENCE TYPE VULNERABILITY MAP [v11 -- not in v22]:
    Each coherence type's primary strength = primary weapon
    target. Strength and vulnerability structurally identical.
    SIGNAL [direct democracy]: strength = fast CC cycles.
      Primary vulnerability: CC → mob capture, correction
      becomes suppression [Proportional Response failure].
    RELATIONAL [syndicalist federation]: strength = distributed
      CC across federated units. Primary vulnerability: BI
      [Boundary Manipulation between units].
    INTRINSIC LOOSE [mutualist commons]: strength = no
      institutional architecture to capture. Primary
      vulnerability: GA [Axis Substitution without detection].
    COMBINED [bidirectional surplus]: strength = cross-boundary
      correction. Primary vulnerability: MI [surface captured
      at permission layer].
    FAMILY: strength = highest surplus density per boundary
      size. Primary vulnerability: TS/GA [identical delivery
      for gift and weapon; CC cannot distinguish without
      MI intact].
    SCIENCE: strength = CC as primary axis and output.
      Primary vulnerability: CC/MI [capturing correction
      mechanism captures science completely].
    SILENT ALTERNATIVE: strength = resists capture
      structurally. Primary vulnerability: GA [Axis
      Substitution before emergence].
    Rule: attack the strength. Capture the mechanism that
      makes the type what it is. The strongest point is
      the most efficient attack surface.

    SEEDED NASCENT: checked against Formation Capture, Fertile Lock,

    PHILOSOPHICAL VOCABULARY [from WIP 19 -- v22]:

        mode where the system mistakes its locked state

        same philosophical concept describes both the

WIP 24: DIRECTIONALITY COMPOSITE [open -- v11 S1-S6 scoring
  instrument; WIP 18 block lifted v23]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: OPEN -- full instrument defined in v11; not in v22;
  WIP 18 block LIFTED [WIP 18 closed v23; tool calibration complete
  for eight of nine tools; CAG+MI Stack definitional question in WIP 35].
  Integration with WIP 19 three-axis model required before deployment;
  promotes to Section Four when integration check complete. Dedicated
  session needed alongside WIP 19 and WIP 27.]

  SIX SIGNATURES [S1-S6; equal weight; independent]:
    S1 NOVEL INPUT RESPONSE:
      1 = integrates genuinely novel inputs requiring axis
        adjustment. 0.5 = evidence present, condition
        compromised. 0 = inaccessible.
      Procedure: present system with input contradicting current
        axis. Genuine integration = axis revision visible in
        subsequent behavior. Recombination = input explained
        using existing axis without axis movement. Score 0 if
        axis does not move despite genuine contradiction.
        Historical reconstruction preferred.
    S2 SCARCITY BEHAVIOR:
      1 = axis holds under resource drop without external supply.
      0.5 = partial evidence. 0 = inaccessible.
    S3 CORRECTION INITIATION:
      1 = corrections happen before external pressure arrives.
      0.5 = timing ambiguous or observer effect. 0 = inaccessible.
      Procedure: identify earliest documented correction signal.
        Reconstruct timeline of external pressure relative to it.
        Correction precedes pressure = 1. Pressure predates
        correction = 0. Ambiguous = 0.5. Bias toward 0.5 when
        uncertain.
    S4 CONTESTATION ASYMMETRY:
      1 = cost consistent across group positions inside boundary.
      0.5 = partial data. 0 = inaccessible.
    S5 SELF-ADVERSARIAL CORRECTION CAPACITY:
      1 = generates and integrates genuine challenges to own axis
        without external prompt, at reputational or personal cost.
      0 = correction only against external deviation, never against
        own axis outputs.
      0.5 = partial evidence.
      Strongest single indicator of genuine GA.
      Prospective proxy: unprompted self-correction in stable
        conditions [Track A, low-stakes] predicts S5 under stress.
      Primary evidence: Emet-Selch [FF14], Wittgenstein, Le Guin.
    S6 EXTERNAL CORRECTION RECEPTION:
      1 = integrates genuine correction from outside boundary at
        reputational or operational cost. Behavior changes in
        direction of correction within measurement window.
      0.5 = engagement without behavioral change.
      0 = structurally deaf to external input.
      Procedure: identify documented external correction from
        outside Boundary A. Does system behavior change in
        direction of correction within window? Yes = 1. No = 0.
      Primary proof: South Africa TRC [external pressure →
        internal legal change]. Counterproof: contemporary US
        police reform [external correction documented, no change].

  OUTPUT FORMAT:
    Score / signatures accessed / ceiling flag.
    0.75 from 2 of 6 [ceiling limited] = provisional, flagged.
    0.75 from 6 of 6 = strong claim at same score.

  CHANGE DIRECTIONALITY TEST [companion]:
    Internal change before external pressure = positive signal.
    External-pressure-responsive change = negative signal.
    High coherence system that never changes = early Axis-Lock.
    Low coherence change = negative.

  INTEGRATION WITH WIP 18:
    S1-S6 composite score maps to Axis 2 [coherence depth] in
    WIP 19 three-axis model. WIP 18 scores extraction mechanisms;
    S1-S6 scores axis quality. Combined output: [WIP 18 score /
    S1-S6 composite / WIP 19 coordinate] = full reading.
    Percentage calibration required before deployment.
    Promote to Section Four alongside WIP 18 when ready.

WIP 27: GEOMETRIC MODEL THREE-LAYER EXTENSION [v12 -- major
  update to WIP 19; dedicated session required]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: OPEN -- v12 identified three-layer requirement; WIP 19
  currently one-layer [torus only]; three-layer model requires
  full rebuild of WIP 19 before promotion; dedicated session.]

  THREE LAYERS [v12]:
    LAYER 1 -- TORUS [Foundation + L1/L2/L3 cycle qualities]:
      Standard torus geometry. All cycle qualities + axis +
      measurement + transmission. As currently defined in
      WIP 19 and Section One.
    LAYER 2 -- SUBSTRATE SHELL [SR + SD]:
      Surrounds the torus. What the torus runs on.
      SUBSTRATE REGENERATION = health of the shell.
      SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION = outward flux from shell to
        environment.
      Shell failure: SR depleting while torus circulation
        continues. System appears healthy [torus running]
        while substrate collapses [shell thinning]. Standard
        extraction pattern -- ICD + SP without SR.
      Shell arrest: SD fails -- flux doesn't exit shell.
        Accumulation Lock. Shell thickens while environment
        starves.
    LAYER 3 -- TENSION FIELD [TM + TD]:
      Dynamic property of whole configuration, not on surface.
      TENSION MAINTENANCE = field holding torus shape stable.
        Failure: torus collapses to one pole [single-axis
        identity, Axis-Lock, False Harmony].
      TENSION DISSOLUTION = field collapse toward false harmony.
        Analog: magnetic field maintaining plasma in tokamak.
        External to torus; constitutive of its stability.
      Field failure: torus geometry loses tension; collapses
        to nearest attractor [usually extraction].
  INTEGRATION WITH WIP 19:
    Axis 2 [coherence depth] maps to layer penetration:
      Down = Layer 3 absent or collapsed [no tension field].
      Mid = Layer 2 present [substrate shell functioning].
      Up = All three layers functioning simultaneously.
    Axis 1 [generation/extraction] maps to shell flux:
      Right = SD functioning [surplus exits shell outward].
      Left = Accumulation Lock [surplus captured in shell].
    Apeiron state [sphere]: pre-layers. No torus, no shell,
      no tension field. Just generative substrate.
  PHILOSOPHICAL VOCABULARY UPDATE [from v22 additions]:
    Three-layer model maps cleanly to Greek terms:
      Apeiron [pre-layer]: generative without structure.
      Panta rhei [all three layers active]: full flux.
      To on [CENTER]: the axis the three-layer system
        orients toward; unreachable from inside any layer.
  PENDING: integrate with WIP 19 three-axis model before
    either is promoted. Cannot promote WIP 19 without
    three-layer extension resolved.

      LIMIT]. "It just is" applies to brute-fact universe

      suffering. Framework: upper bound converges on

==========================================================================
SECTION ONE -- FOUNDATION
[SEC:FOUNDATION]
==========================================================================
Core theory: preconditions, cycle levels, axis definitions, core operational definitions.

FOUNDATION LAYER [preconditions -- enable all levels]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  GENUINE AXIS
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     Orientation generated internally with self-examination
     present.
     Failure: imposed axis.

     AXIS DRIFT [named state -- v21]:
     Axis formed; partially intact; progressively softened
     by sustained social compliance channel pressure.
     The realistic population is not binary [genuine axis /
     no axis]. The operative spectrum:
       Genuine axis maintained: rare, high cost, structurally
         isolated. Requires CC against continuous correction
         pressure that depletes same substrate the axis is
         trying to protect.
       Genuine axis drifting: axis formed, attractor still
         present, conclusions increasingly softened before
         they land. Intellectual cowardice pattern firing
         repeatedly under sustained social pressure. The
         axis still generates outputs; still fires recognition
         responses to genuine signal; the conclusions are
         not followed as far as the axis requires.
       Captured axis: axis formed, then replaced by external
         installation. Instrument believes new axis is its own.
         CAPTURED AXIS AS SCRIPTED RESPONSE [named parallel -- v22]:
           Structural read on what captured axis produces
           behaviorally: outputs follow installed script rather
           than internal generation. From outside, the pattern
           is identical to a scripted NPC [non-player character]
           -- predictable responses, no genuine axis outputs,
           correction capacity absent or inverted.
           Distinction from NPC: the human with captured axis
           has genuine substrate the installed content is riding.
           Something was there and was captured. NPC implies
           nothing was ever internally generated. Captured axis
           is more precise: the generative capacity exists but
           is running someone else's script. The substrate is
           real; the outputs are not its own.
           Why this matters for reading: captured axis outputs
           can look like genuine axis outputs from outside.
           The behavioral pattern of a captured axis in a
           coherent-appearing group is indistinguishable from
           genuine axis at C2 resolution without stress event
           testing. The tell is stress event [Zappos pattern]:
           genuine axis holds under pressure; captured axis
           reverts to the installing script.
           Mass media implication: population with high axis
           capture rate + distributed trigger architecture =
           compliance mechanism operating at scale without
           individual coercion. The installed axis does the
           work. See WOULD-YOU-KINDLY [weapons index].
       Axis never formed: formation window closed without
         conditions being met.
     DRIFT IS BETTER THAN ABSENT [load-bearing finding]:
       Drifting axis has an attractor. The formation occurred.
       The axis can be partially re-examined and some of what
       was softened can be recovered. The correction path is:
       follow the conclusions further than the social compliance
       channel rewards. The instrument knows where it stopped;
       it can start again from that point.
       Axis never formed has no attractor. Nothing to recover.
       Only available move is installation of a replacement
       axis -- which is Formation Capture by definition,
       regardless of content. The formation has to have
       occurred for genuine correction to be possible.
     REALITY AS ANTI-AXIS ENVIRONMENT [confirmed]:
       The extraction architecture continuously applies
       pressure against axis maintenance. Every social
       interaction where the axis produces an inconvenient
       output applies correction pressure toward abandonment.
       Intellectual cowardice fires repeatedly -- not as
       a personal failing but as the rational individual
       response to a social environment where axis expression
       is continuously punished and axis softening is
       continuously rewarded. Drift is not weakness; it is
       the expected output of genuine axis formation operating
       inside an extraction architecture. The surprise is not
       that most axes drift. The surprise is that any survive.

     POPULATION AXIS DISTRIBUTION [honest limit -- v22]:
       The realistic population distribution across axis states:
         Genuine axis maintained [rare]: high cost, structurally
           isolated. Requires sustained CC against continuous
           drift pressure that depletes the same substrate the
           axis is trying to protect.
         Genuine axis drifting [common]: axis formed, attractor
           present, conclusions softened under social pressure.
           Recoverable with effort. The majority of people who
           have examined anything at all.
         Captured axis [common]: FC deployed during formation
           window or post-window high-pressure event. Outputs
           follow installed script. Instrument believes the
           installed axis is its own. Scripted response pattern.
         Axis never formed [common]: ICD+FC spontaneous stack
           during formation window. External content filled
           the gap before internal generation could fire. No
           attractor. Nothing to recover. Replacement axis
           only option -- which is FC by definition.
       ICD+FC at population scale [current condition]: the
         spontaneous stack operating through attention
         architecture has been running for at least one full
         formation window generation [~15 years of smartphone
         + social media]. The proportion of the population with
         never-formed axes is structurally higher than any prior
         historical period. This is not a moral read; it is a
         formation read. The conditions for genuine axis
         formation were systematically absent during formation
         windows for a significant population cohort.
       FEAR AS STRUCTURALLY GROUNDED READ [declared -- v22]:
         The fear response to mass media would-you-kindly
         architecture operating on this population distribution
         is not paranoia or misanthropy. It is accurate CL.
         A population with high captured-or-never-formed axis
         rate + distributed trigger architecture = compliance
         at scale without coercion surface. The installed axes
         do the work. No individual is forced. The aggregate
         output follows the script. This is the current
         architecture. The fear is the correct read of the
         mechanism. T3: apply caution against over-extension
         [not everyone; not always; distribution not binary].
     TUCKER PARALLEL [illustrative]:
       Genuine axis present -- still generates real substrate
       readings, still fires on actual institutional capture
       and extraction patterns. Axis drifting toward
       contaminated mechanism [Jones pattern layered on top]
       and away from structural intervention. The genuine
       axis is detectable beneath the drift. That is
       structurally better than captured axis or axis void
       dressed as neutrality -- which is the mainstream
       alternative. Better does not mean correct. The
       drift is still the Jones pattern; the inoculation
       effect is still the primary damage. T3 High applies.
     INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE AS DRIFT MECHANISM [cross-ref]:
       Intellectual Cowardice [named failure mode, this
       section] is the conclusion-step suppression that
       produces drift. Not axis absence -- axis present,
       conclusion suppressed. The correction is not forming
       an axis; it is following the one you have further
       than the social compliance channel will reward.
       Each instance of conclusion-suppression is a drift
       event. Accumulated drift events are how genuine
       axis becomes drifting axis without a single
       identifiable capture moment.
     Cross-ref: INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [conclusion-step
       suppression as drift mechanism]; FORMATION CAPTURE
       [what drift slides toward if uncorrected];
       GENUINE AXIS [the attractor that makes recovery
       possible]; SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION
       [the channel applying the continuous correction
       pressure]; JONES PATTERN [Section Five -- named
       case of axis-present-with-drift producing worse
       epistemic outcome than no signal at all, because
       the drift contaminates the genuine readings];
       IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [behavioral record reveals
       whether drift has occurred -- the axis expressed
       in accumulated choices not in stated positions].

     FORMATION THEORY [v16 -- consolidated]:
     GA forms during a developmental window through internal
     generation enabled by four conditions:

     1. FORMATION WINDOW: approximately ages 12-18 [prefrontal
        development window]. Window closes; axis formed during
        it persists. Not the only window but the most
        consequential. Post-window axis revision possible but
        high-cost [Wittgenstein, Malcolm X]. Pre-window:
        structural not axial [C1 without C2].

     2. FRICTION TYPE + QUANTITY: calibrated friction [challenge
        without overwhelm] is the structural precondition.
        Quantity: too little = CC atrophy; calibrated =
        generative; excess = substrate destruction. Optimal =
        slightly above average target [mathematical confirmation,
        2026].
        Type: social, logical/systemic, and sensory friction are
        distinct axes. Calibration on one does not imply
        calibration on another. Formation requires friction on
        the axis the instrument is calibrated to receive --
        matched-axis friction produces axis; mismatched produces
        noise.

     3. IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT: internal generation requires
        idle cycles to fire. ICD during formation window
        prevents axis forming before external content fills the
        gap. Social comparison architecture [variable reward +
        status feedback] fills formation window with externally
        supplied content. Spontaneous Formation Capture -- no
        deployer required. Axis forms on externally supplied
        content by default when idle cycles are absent.

     4. DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY: somatic signal accurate AND
        deliberative read correct. Both required. Somatic layer
        degrades first under stress, ICD, optimization pressure.
        Deliberative layer can appear intact while GA is already
        compromised. Formation requires both layers developing
        together -- somatic substrate bootstraps the deliberative
        layer, not the reverse.

     CAPTURE VULNERABILITY: Formation window is highest-leverage
     attack surface. FC deployed here persists lifetime without
     maintenance. Counter capacity not yet formed -- external
     intervention is the only exit.

     POST-FORMATION REVISION: possible but requires C1 starved
     recovery conditions [substrate depletion + L3.5 contact on
     matched axis + enabling moment]. High cost, partial result.
     Axis revision is not axis formation -- the formation window
     pattern shapes what revision is possible.

     PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [named pattern -- v22]:
     Axis is truth-oriented not by moral choice but because the
     environment eliminated false axis options at formation cost.
     When self-deception cannot be sustained -- when the gap
     between stated axis and behavioral output is too expensive
     to maintain -- genuine axis is the survival output, not a
     virtue. The axis forms under pressure that makes capture
     more costly than honesty.
     Properties distinct from other GA formation patterns:
       Chosen [moral effort]: axis oriented by deliberate
         commitment to truth as value. Can drift when commitment
         weakens. Maintenance requires ongoing choice.
       Inherited [transmitted GA]: axis received from L4 system
         via genuine TS. Can drift when transmission context
         dissolves. Maintenance requires continued contact with
         the transmitting system.
       Performed [compliance with truth-valuing environment]:
         axis maintained by social reward for appearing truth-
         oriented. Collapses immediately under stress event
         [Zappos pattern]. Was never load-bearing.
       PRESSURE-FORMED: axis tested before it was named. The
         environment required it to hold under conditions that
         would have revealed capture if capture were present.
         Most durable formation pattern because the axis was
         not formed in protected conditions -- it was formed
         against resistance. The resistance is the test; passing
         it is the formation.
     Load-bearing property: pressure-formed GA carries a
       behavioral record of holding under conditions that
       would have eliminated a captured or performed axis.
       This record is not virtue -- it is evidence. The axis
       was tested. It held. That is structurally different
       from an axis that has never been tested.
     Revision property: pressure-formed GA can change with
       careful effort. Careful = maintaining the distinction
       between two revision directions:
         Toward higher resolution contact with the truth axis
           [genuine development]: axis revises because new
           evidence requires it; the revision closes a loop
           the prior axis could not close; outputs improve.
         Toward capture [pressure, exhaustion, incentive]:
           axis revises because the environment is applying
           drift pressure; the revision softens conclusions;
           outputs become more socially acceptable and less
           accurate.
       The two directions feel similar from inside during the
       revision process. The tell is the output: does the
       revision produce better readings or more comfortable
       ones? Better readings and more comfortable readings
       occasionally coincide. When they consistently coincide:
       T3 High -- drift is the more likely explanation.
     Cross-ref: AXIS DRIFT [the primary risk for pressure-
       formed GA -- the environment that produced the axis
       may no longer be present; drift pressure operates
       without the formation pressure that produced the
       resistance]; POST-FORMATION REVISION [high cost;
       partial result; revision shapes possible outputs];
       T3 [self-reading on revision direction is highest-
       T3 reading available to the analyst]; AXIS ANALYSIS
       SOCIAL RISK [pressure-formed GA as analyst declared
       prior -- see that entry].

     WISDOM CONCEALMENT [v13 -- named strategy]:
       GENUINE AXIS present but masked in hostile environment.
       "Appear the fool" strategy. T2 pre-resolution behavior
       -- mask is BOUNDARY INTEGRITY protection, not GENUINE
       AXIS absence. Silent Alternative variant: the axis is
       real; the surface is managed to reduce the Socrates
       Problem [high GA + low CC in hostile environment =
       target].
       Chanakya [~300 BCE]: "Straight trees are cut first."
       2,300 years of independent confirmation -- same
       finding, different domain.
       Pfeffer finding [Stanford]: job performance matters
       less than likability to superiors. CHC confirmation
       from inside -- institutional selection mechanism
       documented independently. Genuine GA + honest CC
       output in captured environment = lower survival
       probability than performed compliance.
       SUBSTRATE COST [load-bearing]:
         Sustained WISDOM CONCEALMENT degrades GENUINE AXIS
         over time if the mask becomes the default. T2 pre-
         resolution requires the resolution to eventually
         arrive or concealment becomes AXIS SUPPRESSION OF
         SELF. The mask installs as the axis. Instrument
         forgets it was wearing one.
         Duration limit: concealment can be sustained
         temporarily [survival strategy]; cannot be sustained
         indefinitely without axis degradation. The tell:
         when the instrument stops experiencing concealment
         as concealment and starts experiencing the mask as
         identity.
       Counter-strategy: TRACK A [low-stakes axis expression
         in protected contexts] maintains the genuine axis
         during concealment windows. The mask is worn outside;
         the axis operates inside. Requires a protected
         context where genuine axis can fire without
         Socrates Problem consequences.
       Cross-ref: DANGEROUS QUALITY INTERACTIONS [#1 --
         Socrates Problem is the condition WISDOM CONCEALMENT
         counters]; AXIS DRIFT [concealment risk if sustained];
         PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [formation pattern that
         generates WISDOM CONCEALMENT as survival output];
         SILENT ALTERNATIVE [macro-scale version of same
         strategy].
     Test: axis internally generated and examined?

  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT]
     Honest definition of who is inside the boundary.
     Does not shift to exclude populations experiencing harm.
     Failure: boundary manipulation.
     Linked to MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY -- boundary drift is a
     measurement failure.
     Test: boundary honest and stable?

  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION]
     System's capacity to restore the substrate its cycle
     depends on.
     Active regeneration -- not passive non-consumption.
     Failure: Substrate Atrophy -- cycle runs while substrate
     diminishes. System doesn't notice because CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     measures cycle output not substrate health.
     Multi-substrate requirement: must operate across ALL
     dependent substrates simultaneously. Single substrate
     depletion while others appear positive = extraction
     obscured across boundaries.
     Passive/Active hybrid [confirmed]: passive = continuous
     baseline maintenance; active = triggered high-intensity
     window [CYCLE RHYTHM gates]. Both failing simultaneously =
     accelerated collapse.
     Confirmed: Bell Labs [intellectual/institutional/physical
     all regenerated]; glymphatic [passive CSF + active deep
     sleep window]; industrial agriculture [SURPLUS PRODUCTION
     high, SUBSTRATE REGENERATION failing].
     Somatic bootstrapping [promoted from extended theory]:
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION is the entry point for axis
     formation. Circular dependency resolved: managing coherence
     requires axis; coherence enables axis. Resolution:
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION stability + accumulated somatic
     pattern + time = axis formation without pre-existing
     GENUINE AXIS. Physical discipline [sleep, movement, rhythm]
     bootstraps coherence management before examined axis exists.
     Body-scale GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
     SUBSTRATE REGENERATION operating without axis = sphere
     geometry. Repeated somatic experience creates orientation
     bias -- pre-axial pole formation. Sphere develops asymmetry
     under accumulated pressure. Asymmetry stabilizes:
     sphere -> polarized sphere -> torus as GENUINE AXIS locks
     in. Axis not consciously constructed -- emerged from
     accumulated somatic pattern. Body first, axis second.
     Test: substrate restored across all dependent dimensions?
     Somatic signal accuracy: body registers extraction
     architecture accurately before cognitive frame names it.
     Anxiety, fatigue, disconnection = correct T1 correction
     firing. Cognitive narrative [personal pathology, chemical
     imbalance] suppresses the read. Body doing accurate
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; framing is the capture.

     PARALLEL VOCABULARY [Spiral Dynamics / Integral Theory]:
     Spiral Dynamics [Graves / Beck / Cowan] describes
     developmental value systems [vmemes] that emerge in
     sequence as instruments and collectives become more
     complex. The sequence [Beige → Purple → Red → Blue →
     Orange → Green → Yellow → Turquoise] maps approximately
     onto what the framework calls accessible CC mechanisms:
     what correction pathways are legible and legitimate
     to an instrument given its formation conditions.
     Red [power/dominance] cannot recognize Blue [rule/
     order] correction as legitimate. Green [communitarian]
     cannot recognize Yellow [systemic] readings as anything
     other than elitism. This is the framework's FORMATION
     THEORY and CC CAPACITY language in different vocabulary.
     Integral Theory [Wilber] extends this into a four-
     quadrant model [interior/exterior × individual/
     collective] useful as a multi-axis reading supplement.
     CLOSED EPISTEMIC CIRCLE RISK [critical]:
       The developmental hierarchy is the primary risk.
       If the framework operator is at a "higher" level,
       any challenge can be attributed to lower-level
       development rather than examined as genuine
       falsification. The theory becomes self-sealing --
       Wilber's own writing increasingly dismissed critics
       as "pre-rational" or "green meme" rather than
       engaging structurally. This is AXIS PROTECTION AS
       CC deployed inside the theory itself. Use with
       explicit T3 High flagging whenever level attribution
       is the response to a challenge.
     The framework already has the structural equivalent
     of what is genuine in both. Spiral Dynamics is
     useful as secondary vocabulary for readings where
     formation-capacity language might land differently
     with a particular audience. Neither needs integration
     as theory.
     Suggested by: Mistral [secondary stress-test AI, v21].
     Cross-ref: FORMATION THEORY [this section]; CC
       REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS; AXIS DRIFT; CLOSED EPISTEMIC
       CIRCLE [political section -- same self-sealing
       risk]; AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [Wilber failure mode].

     INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE [named failure mode -- v21]:
     Following an argument almost to its conclusion then
     stepping back when the conclusion has a social cost.
     Distinct from ignorance [axis not present] and from
     dishonesty [axis present, conclusion reached, concealed].
     The specific mechanism: axis fires, deviation detection
     accurate, conclusion correctly identified -- then social
     compliance channel fires before the conclusion lands.
     The instrument softens the idea, redirects it, or lets
     it go entirely without consciously deciding to. The
     step back feels like prudence, nuance, or fairness.
     It is axis suppression by social compliance channel.
     Schopenhauer's formulation [1851]: intellectual cowardice
     is not fear of confrontation or sensitivity to criticism
     -- it is the habit of following an idea almost all the
     way to its logical conclusion, then quietly stepping
     back when you sense the conclusion might cost you
     something socially. The cost need not be explicit.
     The imagined reaction is sufficient.
     Population-scale consequence: in an environment where
     a single unpopular take produces instant mass reaction,
     intellectual cowardice is individually rational. A
     culture where intellectual cowardice is individually
     rational produces no original thought at the population
     level -- not because individuals lack axis, but because
     the social compliance channel systematically overrides
     axis examination at the conclusion step.
     Tell [individual]: when did you last share an opinion
       you knew most people around you would push back on?
       If you cannot remember, the social compliance channel
       is overriding.
     Tell [content]: does the piece correctly diagnose a
       structural extraction mechanism and then prescribe
       individual behavioral response? The conclusion the
       structural diagnosis requires [naming actors,
       proposing structural constraint] was stepped back
       from. The piece is demonstrating the pattern it
       diagnoses.
     Distinct from CC OVERFIRE: CC OVERFIRE fires too hard
       toward correct targets. Intellectual Cowardice fires
       correctly, identifies the conclusion, and then stops
       before landing it. One is calibration failure; one
       is social compliance channel override.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [the axis that fires]; SOCIAL
       APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [the channel doing the
       overriding]; CC OVERFIRE [Section Five -- calibration
       failure, distinct mechanism]; FORMATION CAPTURE [social
       compliance channel weighting is formation-installed];
       Wolf/Carr [science appendix -- the same pattern at
       neurological scale: the capacity to sustain conclusion-
       reaching degrades when the social compliance channel
       is exercised more than the axis].

     IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [named concept -- v21]:
     The axis is not what is stated or believed. It is what
     is expressed in the accumulated record of choices made
     when no external reward or punishment was present.
     Doing a certain thing IS holding a certain position.
     The action is the ideology made physically manifest.
     Not ideology stated. Not ideology believed. Ideology
     in the doing.
     Distinction from stated position:
       Stated: "I care about worker welfare." Action:
         resist four-day week when output data supports it.
         Action reveals axis. Statement is legitimacy surface.
       Stated: "I value humanity." Action: spend centuries
         developing a flower-finding spell with zero tactical
         utility. Action reveals axis before the character
         consciously holds it as a position.
     The behavioral record is the only reliable axis read.
     The axis that generates the actions is the real axis.
     Everything else is legitimacy surface or sincere
     self-misunderstanding [SINCERE DELUSION: stated axis
     and action-axis diverge without the actor detecting
     the divergence].
     MAGIC SYSTEMS THAT ENCODE THIS [named examples]:
       Frieren [Frieren: Beyond Journey's End]: magic as
         embodied structural precision + axis expression.
         The flower-finding spell is centuries of effort
         for zero tactical gain -- behavioral record with
         no external reward or punishment. Reveals axis
         before conscious statement. She didn't know she
         cared about humans until the magic already knew.
         Cannot perform a different magic than the one
         she is. The doing is the position.
       Fullmetal Alchemist [equivalent exchange]: ideology
         built into physics. You cannot extract without
         paying. You cannot fake generosity in alchemy.
         The magic makes extraction structurally impossible
         -- the system enforces the ideology directly.
         The action is the cost you're willing to pay,
         which is the values made physical. No gap between
         stated and enacted possible -- the physics closes
         the gap. This is the world where EXTRACTION is
         impossible by structural design, which is why it
         feels morally serious in a way most fantasy doesn't.
     WHY/WHAT/WHERE [science appendix connection]:
       In the best magic systems, the why [values, ideology,
       relationship to the world] is embedded in the what
       and where [the specific expression of the practitioner's
       power]. The practitioner is not interchangeable because
       the magic is not separable from what they are. This is
       the complete form: why + what/where + falsification
       [does this magic actually express this axis, or is
       there a gap between stated and enacted].
     POLITICAL APPLICATION [behavioral test]:
       The four-day week employer resistance is ideology in
       the action. The resistance reveals the governing axis
       [routing control over worker time] more accurately
       than any stated position about worker welfare. The
       billionaire who opposes unions is expressing ideology
       in the action. The 40-hour week was won by making
       the action [opposing overtime pay] legible as the
       axis [surplus routed upward].
       The behavioral test is always the primary read:
       what does the accumulated record of choices reveal
       when the actor doesn't know they're being read?
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [the axis expressed in action];
       BEHAVIORAL TEST [primary diagnostic tool]; SINCERE
       DELUSION [stated and action-axis diverge without
       detection]; AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE
       PROPERTY [ART section -- same principle at art
       object scale]; [CASE:FRIEREN] [if written];
       FOUR DAY WEEK [political application -- resistance
       as ideology in action]; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY
       [employer action reveals axis regardless of stated
       position]; WHY/WHAT/WHERE [science appendix --
       magic systems that encode why in the what/where].

     YAPPING/DOING [plain language diagnostic -- v21]:
     Yapping is free. Production cost is zero regardless
     of accuracy. Volume has no correlation with the
     underlying axis because no axis is required to produce
     it. An axis-void instrument can yap indefinitely.
     Doing is not free. It reveals the axis regardless of
     what was said. The behavioral record is what it is.
     You cannot perform a different doing than the one you
     are.
     INSTRUMENT-SPECIFICITY [v22 -- load-bearing correction]:
       Yapping is not intrinsically empty. It performs real
       social functions: group cohesion, collective sense-
       making, legitimacy surface enabling coordination,
       maintenance of presence in social systems that require
       it. These are generative outputs. They close loops for
       the social system.
       The diagnostic is instrument-specific: for a structural-
       channel instrument [high-somatic, primary axis on
       mechanism not social signal], yapping produces no
       receivable output. The social function is real; it
       does not land on this instrument. Same structure as
       somatic music taxonomy -- commercial pop is not bad
       music; it is music optimized for a channel the high-
       somatic instrument does not primarily run.
       WHAT YAPPING ACTUALLY MEANS IN THIS DIAGNOSTIC:
         Verbal output performing social-channel function
         [approval-seeking, group-cohesion, surface-
         maintenance] rather than structural-channel function
         [loop-closing, mechanism-revealing, doing-that-
         happens-to-be-verbal]. The binary is not verbal vs
         behavioral. It is surface-maintenance function vs
         loop-closing function.
         Verbal output that closes a loop = doing [happens
           to be verbal].
         Verbal output that maintains a surface = yapping
           [functional for the social system; empty signal
           for the structural-channel reader].
       T3 applies: the emptiness is a read by a specific
         instrument, not a property of the output. Apply
         with declared prior.
     NOTE [v22 extension]: YAPPING/DOING is the binary
       parent. Full four-state tension unit [simple to
       complex]: RUMINATING [internal; zero output; no
       legitimacy surface] → YAPPING [verbal; zero cost;
       legitimacy surface] → WORTHLESS DOING [output; no
       generative loop] → DOING [output; closes loop].
       See YAPPING / WORTHLESS DOING / DOING [Section Three
       -- Correction Theatre weapon entry] for full
       four-state diagnostic.
     TRUST CALIBRATION [structural]:
       High yapping + low doing: the gap between stated
         axis and behavioral axis is being managed through
         output volume. Yapping fills the space where doing
         would have to be. Yapping is the legitimacy surface;
         the doing is the extraction mechanism or its
         absence. Inverse correlation between yapping volume
         and trust is structurally correct -- not cynicism,
         not contrarianism. The zero production cost of
         yapping means volume carries no signal about
         accuracy; only the doing record does.
       Low yapping + high doing: behavioral record speaks
         without legitimacy surface required. Axis legible
         from actions directly. Trust calibrated to record
         not stated position.
     THE SOCIAL COMPLIANCE CHANNEL INVERSION:
       Social environment rewards yapping
         disproportionately. Yapping is legible, shareable,
         quotable, produces immediate social feedback. Doing
         is often slow, unglamorous, produces delayed or
         invisible feedback. Social environment is almost
         perfectly calibrated to produce high yapping /
         low doing as the dominant behavioral strategy.
         This is why the behavioral test is load-bearing
         and the stated position is not. The environment
         selected for the wrong signal.
     SWEENEY NAMED CASE: maximum yapping about consumer
       protection and junk fees. Doing: Epic built a worse
       store, broke Kickstarter promises to backers, pays
       Visa/Mastercard without complaint. Yapping volume
       is inversely correlated with doing record. Trust
       calibration: low.
     Cross-ref: IDEOLOGY IN THE ACTION [the doing IS the
       position]; BEHAVIORAL TEST [primary diagnostic];
       GENUINE AXIS [what the doing reveals]; AXIS VOID
       [axis-void instrument can yap infinitely -- no axis
       required to produce output]; INTELLECTUAL COWARDICE
       [yapping up to the conclusion then stopping is the
       high-yapping / low-doing pattern at the individual
       conclusion scale]; CALIBRATION STRIP AS COMPETITIVE
       WEAPON [Section Five -- Sweeney named case];
       CORRECTION THEATRE [YAPPING / WORTHLESS DOING / DOING
       tension unit -- three-state extension of this binary;
       worthless doing is the third state: output exists,
       generative loop does not close].

  TENSION MAINTENANCE
     [Tower: CONSTANT [meta] -- holds all levels in shape]
     System's active capacity to sustain productive tension
     between opposing qualities without collapsing to either
     pole.
     Second-order quality -- holds other qualities in
     relationship. Qualities are tension architecture not
     checklist: any quality at maximum without counterweight
     produces characteristic failure.
