     SURPLUS PRODUCTION unchecked by CORRECTION CAPACITY =
     extraction. CORRECTION CAPACITY unchecked by SURPLUS
     PRODUCTION = suppression. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY without
     TENSION MAINTENANCE = clarity that isolates
     [Phosphophyllite / Lapis head]. The generative zone is
     sustained disequilibrium between poles, not optimization
     of any single quality.
     Distinct from CORRECTION CAPACITY [corrects deviation]:
     TENSION MAINTENANCE maintains the tension that makes
     correction necessary. Distinct from GENERATIVE CYCLE
     [generates]: TENSION MAINTENANCE holds cycle and surplus
     in relationship without one dominating.
     Failure: Tension Collapse -- system resolves tension by
     eliminating one pole. Axis-Lock or Sub-Structural.
     Biological confirmation: homeostasis; flow state;
     mycorrhizal exchange [mutualism sustained against drift
     toward parasitism]. Planetary: Earth sustains geological
     tension via plate tectonics. Venus lost this -- TENSION
     MAINTENANCE failure producing SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
     collapse.
     Coherent solutions require tension [operational test]: a
     solution with only one value is not a solution -- it is a
     preference with no constraint. Coherent solutions require
     minimum two genuine values in real conflict, a compromise
     that neither fully satisfies, friction to maintain, and
     real cost to all parties. A position that costs nothing to
     hold is either a preference [one value], a extraction mechanism [costs
     fall entirely on one side], or False Coherence [costs
     hidden]. Test: who pays? If nobody or only them -- not
     coherent. Applies to political positions, institutional
     design, relationship structures, policies. Tension is the
     load-bearing element -- remove it and the solution
     collapses to extraction or preference.
     Test: opposing qualities held in productive tension?

     THREE-STATE INSTRUMENT TENSION UNIT [v21 -- worked
     example at individual instrument scale]:
     Truth-seeking and functional are states not qualities.
     Each requires conditions the other degrades. The third
     state that completes the useful tension unit is contact.

     TRUTH-SEEKING STATE: withdrawal, internal, accuracy-
       oriented. Requires stillness, idle cycles, protection
       from social compliance channel pressure. Degrades
       functional: time not spent operating inside the world.
       Without functional: produces private certainty that
       cannot be tested. Without contact: produces closed-
       circle readings with no correction from outside.

     FUNCTIONAL STATE: engagement, external, viability-
       oriented. Requires participation in the world as it
       is, partial acceptance of legitimacy surface, social
       legibility. Degrades truth-seeking: the conditions
       for accurate reading are degraded by engagement.
       Without truth-seeking: produces effective action
       aimed at wrong target -- competent execution of
       captured axis. Without contact: produces performance
       without genuine connection to substrate being served.

     CONTACT STATE: encounter, relational, correction-
       oriented. Genuine encounter with another instrument
       that has its own axis, its own readings, its own
       resistance. Requires lowering defensive architecture
       both other states build up. Tests both: truth-seeking
       is tested against an instrument that pushes back;
       functional is tested against genuine substrate
       response rather than performance feedback.
       Without truth-seeking: contact produces relational
       dependency without grounding -- formation capture
       risk. Without functional: contact produces
       overwhelming exposure with no capacity to process
       and return to the world. Without contact: the
       oscillation between truth-seeking and functional
       becomes closed -- self-correcting within the
       instrument's own limits, never corrected from
       outside.

     THE OSCILLATION [not simultaneous maximum]:
       No single state at maximum. Each state entered,
       worked, exited. The generative zone is disciplined
       movement between all three -- not optimization of
       any one. Truth-seeking too long produces isolation.
       Functional too long produces axis drift [see AXIS
       DRIFT, this section]. Contact too long produces
       substrate depletion without recovery.

     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY AS RESIDUE:
       TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [quality] is what the
       three-state oscillation builds when working. Not
       a fourth state -- a capacity that accumulates
       through the oscillation done well. Truth-seeking
       develops what is worth transmitting. Functional
       develops what can land in the world. Contact
       develops what moves to another instrument without
       fatal distortion. TS is the residue of all three
       operating in productive tension over time. TS
       without all three states accessible is not genuine
       TS: either conclusions without examination [function
       without truth], correct readings that can't move
       [truth without function], or private relational
       insight that can't be shared [contact without the
       other two].

     Cross-ref: TENSION MAINTENANCE [meta-quality this
       exemplifies]; AXIS DRIFT [truth-seeking/functional
       oscillation failure mode]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
       [quality the oscillation builds]; INTELLECTUAL
       COWARDICE [truth-seeking state aborting before
       conclusion -- specific failure mode within the
       oscillation]; GENUINE AXIS [what all three states
       are serving when the oscillation works]; SUBSTRATE
       REGENERATION [rest as precondition enabling the
       oscillation -- not a fourth state but the floor
       beneath all three].

  TENSION DISSOLUTION
     [Tower: FOUNDATION [meta, TENSION MAINTENANCE opposite]]
     Active collapse of productive tension toward false
     resolution.
     Test: tension collapsed to single pole?

LEVEL 1 [generative domain -- TENSION CORE: GENERATIVE CYCLE]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  GENERATIVE CYCLE
     [Tower: L1 CORE]
     Completes a loop: takes in, transforms, returns.
     Failure: cycle collapse or performance without function.
     Test: loop completes?

  SURPLUS PRODUCTION
     [Tower: L1]
     Outputs exceed cost of running the cycle across an honest
     window.
     Moral content lives here.
     Failure: consumption dominance.
     Test: outputs exceed cycle cost?

  CORRECTION CAPACITY
     [Tower: CONSTANT -- fires at all levels; L1 third point]
     Detects contradiction between outputs and orientation.
     Moves without external pressure.
     Failure: correction absence or capture.

     DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS CENTRALIZED SORTING
     [v16]:
     In high-complexity, high-chaos environments, distributed
     iterative correction with slight bias produces more
     reliable output than centralized judgment. Confirmed
     across three independent domains:
       Germinal centers [immunology]: distributed B cell
         selection with slight bias produces reliable
         antibodies. Rare dominant clone model [centralized]
         is wrong.
       Scientific peer review: 60% rejection rate variance
         between reviewer sets. Centralized editor/judge
         model produces high variance regardless of
         investment.
       Distributed open-source [FOSS]: contribution
         procedures as embedded methodology produce
         generational continuity; codebase-only transmission
         does not.
     Implication for CC design: correction capacity at system
     level should be distributed with slight directional bias,
     not monopolized by central correcting authority.
     Centralized CC is the more capturable configuration.

     Friction axis [formation]: CORRECTION CAPACITY requires
     friction to form. No friction = no resistance = CORRECTION
     CAPACITY cannot develop. Calibrated friction = resistance
     = CORRECTION CAPACITY forms under pressure. Excess friction
     = substrate destroyed before CORRECTION CAPACITY forms.
     Generative window = middle range [challenge without
     overwhelm]. Friction is the structural precondition for
     correction capacity to exist, not incidental discomfort.
     Confirmed: Dunedin cohort [self-control at 3 predicts
     outcomes at 32]; Ericsson deliberate practice;
     Csikszentmihalyi flow balance.
     Formation Conditions: insufficient friction during
     formation window produces CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy.
     Mechanism: CORRECTION CAPACITY develops through corrective
     feedback cycles; remove friction = atrophies. Applications:
     grade inflation [feedback removed], AI-assisted work
     without development [output without correction cycle],
     frictionless consumer environments. Individual effort
     cannot compensate -- structural redesign required.
     Mathematical confirmation [The Guardian / maths modelling
     2026]: "Slightly above average" target outperforms both
     "aim high" and "aim at average." Optimal target =
     calibrated friction zone. "Shoot for the moon" = too many
     failures, depletes CORRECTION CAPACITY budget without
     building axis. Aiming at average = insufficient friction,
     no CORRECTION CAPACITY development.
     Friction Axis quantified: generative zone confirmed
     mathematically, not just empirically.
     Excess friction [too much, not too little]: substrate
     consumed faster than it regenerates. CORRECTION CAPACITY
     cannot form because the substrate it would run on is being
     destroyed. Distinct from insufficient friction [atrophy];
     excess friction is active substrate destruction. Outcome
     depends on whether substrate holds through the window.
     Accidental formation: involuntary friction [difficult
     upbringing, deprivation, irrational environment] can
     produce the same CORRECTION CAPACITY development as
     deliberate calibrated friction -- but at higher substrate
     cost and with no guarantee of survival. Optimal is
     intentional friction without cruelty. Accidental excess
     friction that produces functional output means the
     substrate held, barely.
     Neurological friction example [non-specific]: conditions
     that produce involuntary sensory and social friction run
     the formation window hot by default. Either builds
     extraordinary CORRECTION CAPACITY [if substrate holds] or
     destroys substrate [if environment adds load without
     support]. Outcome depends entirely on whether support
     environment compensates for the excess load.
     Friction type [missing variable -- added]: friction
     quantity [too little / calibrated / excess] is not
     sufficient. Type matters equally. Social friction,
     logical/systemic friction, and sensory friction are
     distinct axes. Calibration on one axis does not imply
     calibration on another. An environment can have
     calibrated logical friction and catastrophic social
     friction simultaneously. For some instruments, social
     friction is not friction -- it is noise that triggers the
     panic response by default. The generative zone for these
     instruments is not in social domains. Some nervous systems
     read social friction as extraction because social CAUSAL
     LEGIBILITY is structurally illegible to them -- not
     misreading, different instrument.
     Somatic correction without CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: body fires
     correction continuously because the causal chain is
     illegible to it. Meltdown, shutdown, systemizing, and
     withdrawal are not failures of correction -- they are the
     correction. Body enforcing BOUNDARY INTEGRITY when CAUSAL
     LEGIBILITY is absent. Apparent dysfunction is the
     instrument doing its job without the chain needed to aim
     it.
     Reflexive threshold calibration [learnable]: threshold at
     which correction fires can be read and adjusted by the
     system itself -- not just direction or magnitude.
     Framework thresholds are currently treated as properties
     of the system, not variables the system can adjust. This
     is the missing piece. Calibrating the instrument, not the
     correction.
     Attention gap requirement [science appendix]: SR requires
     idle cycles. Default mode network fires only in gaps.
     Continuous input extracts the recovery window. Exhaustion
     is SR deficit not overwork -- eliminate gaps, eliminate
     substrate recovery.
     High-intelligence friction pattern [Psychology Today,
     2026]: Overthinking and difficulty transmitting ideas
     simply are framed as "frustrating habits." Framework read:
     instrument properties, not failures. High logical/systemic
     friction tolerance + low social CAUSAL LEGIBILITY = correct
     diagnosis, cannot transmit. The "habit" framing is
     backwards. See: AGI case [transmission gap].
     Autism as measurement-action unity [anti-extraction
     instrument]: friction type mismatch breaks somatic
     manipulation channel. High CL + low social compliance =
     measurement fires directly into action without legitimacy
     surface intercept. Anti-tower requires target to route
     accurate signal through social compliance before acting.
     When routing doesn't happen the extraction mechanism misfires. Cannot
     stop what doesn't process the transmission.
     Deception channel split [partial confirmation, 2025/2018]:
     Structural/factual deception [data lies, logical
     inconsistency] = high CL channel, autism advantage
     confirmed. Emotional/social deception [vague manipulation,
     tone, ambient pressure] = social CL channel, autism
     disadvantage confirmed. Extraction architecture primarily
     uses the second type -- vague, emotional, ambient. Autism
     counter is partial not total.
     Framework prediction untested: autistic accuracy against
     actors who successfully deceive neurotypicals via
     structural deception. Closest proxy: demeanor-mismatch
     advantage [2025 study].
     Vague emotional threat protocol [personal operational
     rule]: treat vague emotional pressure as probable threat
     until it explains itself or refuses to. Refusal confirms
     extraction mechanism -- legitimate signals withstand
     examination. Cannot explain = cannot be verified = treat
     as threat by default.
     CORRECTION CAPACITY atrophy when AI absorbs the correction
     cycle that would have developed the capacity. Credential
     system confirmation [Bacow/Harvard 2026]: selects for
     CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [analytical capacity] while suppressing
     CORRECTION CAPACITY [correction firing under pressure].

     CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS [precondition -- v21]:
     CC fires toward a target. Deviation from what, toward what,
     is determined by governing values and goals. Without an
     examined axis CC has no reference point -- it cannot correct
     meaningfully because it does not know what the correct
     direction is. An unexamined axis is not a neutral axis; it
     is a captured or inherited axis operating below the
     correction threshold. CC built on an unexamined axis corrects
     toward whatever the axis points -- including extraction
     targets -- without registering that as deviation.
     Precondition: genuine axis examination required before CC
     can be assessed as functional. Sophisticated correction
     procedures on an unexamined axis = CT, not CC.
     This is why nations and institutions resist genuine axis
     examination: the axis is the load-bearing thing. Everything
     downstream is superstructure. Touching the axis seriously
     destabilizes every structure built on it being unexamined.
     The resistance is structurally accurate, not irrational.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CORRECTION THEATRE; AXIS PROTECTION
       AS CC [below]; FORMATION CAPTURE.

     AXIS PROTECTION AS CC [named failure mode -- v21]:
     Correction architecture that excludes its governing axis
     from examination is not CC -- it is a boundary around the
     axis dressed as correction.
     Distinct from Correction Theatre [CT]: CT is visible
     performance of correction producing no change. AXIS
     PROTECTION AS CC produces real change -- genuine correction
     fires and lands -- but the axis determining what counts as
     deviation is protected from examination. The correction is
     real; the target is wrong by design.
     Tell: the more sophisticated and functional the correction
     procedure, the more effectively it protects the axis --
     working CC is evidence the system is healthy. The axis
     remains untested because the procedure confirms health.
     Most stable long-run configuration for extraction at
     institutional and national scale. Presents as healthy to
     external examination. Self-maintains, self-corrects toward
     extraction targets without registering deviation.
     Intervention: axis examination, not correction procedure
     examination. The procedure is not the problem.
     Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CORRECTION THEATRE; MORAL
       LEGIBILITY [ACWL -- same structure at ethical instrument
       scale]; FORMATION CAPTURE; CC REQUIRES GENUINE AXIS;
       SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED CORRECTION SIGNAL [Section
       One -- what CC produces when the direct pathway is
       blocked; somatic discharge of accumulated correction
       pressure via indirect pathway].

     Test: deviation detected and corrected without external
     pressure?

  INPUT FILTERING [v13 -- confirmed; L1 precondition]
     [Tower: L1 precondition -- screens what enters GC]
     Whether what enters the generative cycle is appropriate
     substrate. Pre-cycle input screening. Merged with
     RECEPTION CAPACITY [two failure modes of same function]:
       RECEPTION BLINDNESS: misreads reciprocal vs extractive
         input. Extractive input processed as gift; reciprocal
         input not distinguished. SUICIDAL EMPATHY is the
         named configuration when this failure is identity-
         anchored [see Section Two].
       GARBAGE CYCLE: corrupt substrate enters GC before
         screening. Cycle runs on captured input; outputs
         confirm the corruption.
     Distinct from SR [regenerates substrate] and CC
       [corrects outputs] -- INPUT FILTERING screens before
       the cycle runs. SR and CC operate after.
     Primary cases: media [attention cycle on corrupted
       information diet]; education [captured curriculum
       enters formation cycle]; science [biased literature
       enters review cycle].
     Weapon: SYNTHETIC CULTURE FLOOD [corrupt substrate
       at volume such that filtering cannot keep pace];
       FORMATION CAPTURE [corrupt substrate installed
       during formation window before filtering develops].

     SUICIDAL EMPATHY [v13 -- named configuration]:
       Empathy as identity rather than response. When empathy
       is core to self-concept, withdrawing it feels like
       self-betrayal even when the target is extractive or
       harmful. RECEPTION BLINDNESS failure where the
       blindness is identity-anchored -- correcting it
       requires revising the self-concept, not just updating
       a read.
       Mechanism: CC routed through identity protection
         rather than reality testing. The instrument detects
         that something is wrong [CC fires]; routes the
         correction through "but I am someone who empathizes"
         [identity protection]; correction is inverted --
         the instrument doubles down on empathy rather than
         withdrawing it.
       Not stupidity -- specific configuration. Not gender-
         specific -- documented across all profiles where
         empathy is load-bearing identity. The identity
         loading is the mechanism, not the empathy itself.
       Distinct from healthy empathy: empathy as response
         [situational, withdrawable when target is harmful]
         vs empathy as identity [constitutive, withdrawal
         feels like self-destruction].
       Counter: GENUINE AXIS + CORRECTION TARGETING. The
         axis examines whether the empathy is producing
         generative or extractive outcomes. CORRECTION
         TARGETING locates the actual source of harm [the
         extractive relationship pattern, not the empathy
         capacity itself]. The empathy capacity is not
         the problem; the identity anchoring is.
       Cross-ref: DANGEROUS QUALITY INTERACTIONS [#4 --
         surplus without direction; sincere misdirection];
         RECEPTION CAPACITY [INPUT FILTERING failure type];
         IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL FOUNDATION [same
         mechanism at different quality target];
         SUICIDAL EMPATHY is the empathy-specific
         instantiation of SINGLE-AXIS IDENTITY failure.

     Test: does the system distinguish reciprocal from
       extractive input before processing? Does corrupt
       substrate enter the cycle?

  RELATIONAL BONDING [v13 -- confirmed; L1 support]
     [Tower: L1 support -- GC at relational scale]
     Sustained reciprocal relationship as generative quality.
     GC at relational scale with TS properties [persists
     across time]. Distinct from INPUT FILTERING [detects
     signal type] -- RELATIONAL BONDING measures duration
     and depth of reciprocal exchange, not detection capacity.
     The bond is the sustained generative loop between two
     instruments. Distinct from L3.5 INTERFACE [which is
     the contact protocol] -- RELATIONAL BONDING is what
     develops when the contact protocol runs over time.
     Failure: RELATIONAL EXTRACTION -- sustained extractive
       relationship displacing generative capacity. The bond
       forms; the routing is extractive. Hardest failure mode
       to detect because the bond itself is real.
     Weapon: DEPENDENCY CONSTRUCTION at relational scale
       [bond maintained by extraction architecture; exit
       cost rises with bond depth].
     Test: does sustained contact increase generative
       capacity of both instruments? Or does one deplete
       while the other accumulates?

  OUTPUT INTEGRATION [v13 -- confirmed; L1 support]
     [Tower: L1 support -- SD landing]
     Whether receiving systems can absorb and use distributed
     surplus. SD measures whether surplus exits the source.
     OUTPUT INTEGRATION measures whether it lands usefully.
     Gift that cannot be received is not a gift.
     Distinct from SD [exits]: OUTPUT INTEGRATION is the
       receiving end. Both required for gift architecture
       to function [see GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK -- Section
       Two].
     Failure: DISTRIBUTION WASTE -- SD high, OUTPUT
       INTEGRATION zero = no net gift despite distribution.
       Surplus exits source; cannot be absorbed by recipient.
       Common failure: surplus in form recipient cannot use
       [knowledge gifted to formation-captured population;
       resources gifted to system with Correction Monopoly].
     Test: does the distributed surplus change the
       generative capacity of the receiving system?

  CYCLE STABILITY [v13 -- confirmed; L1 foundation]
     [Tower: L1 foundation -- GC timing]
     Whether the generative cycle runs predictably vs
     erratically. Distinct from GC [cycle runs at all] --
     CYCLE STABILITY is the timing regularity of the cycle.
     Gates SR [glymphatic confirmation: sleep disruption
     disrupts active glymphatic clearance -- cycle timing
     is binary gate on substrate regeneration].
     Three-state gate [not binary as initially modeled]:
       STABLE: cycle runs on predictable rhythm; SR gates
         open; correction integrates cleanly.
       DISRUPTED: timing irregular; SR gates partially
         open; correction integration impaired.
       COLLAPSED: cycle erratic or absent; SR gates closed;
         substrate accumulates toxins. Distinct from GC
         failure [cycle not running] -- CYCLE STABILITY
         collapse means cycle running irregularly, not
         stopped.
     Weapon: INTERMITTENT REINFORCEMENT [Skinner variable-
       ratio schedule] -- externally controls cycle timing
       to produce dependency. Also ICD [idle cycle
       deprivation] -- removes natural cycle rhythm.
     Failure: CYCLE CAPTURE -- timing externally controlled.
       Instrument's cycle runs on external schedule not
       internal rhythm.
     Test: does the cycle run on the system's own rhythm?
       Or is timing externally determined?

LEVEL 2 [epistemic domain -- TENSION CORE: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2 CORE]
     System maintains honest, accessible surface by which
     outputs can be evaluated against stated orientation.
     Includes boundary honesty, signal sequencing, correction
     trigger accessibility.
     Failure: measurement surface captured, drifted, or
     destroyed.
     Most load-bearing quality -- lower levels intact with this
     destroyed misreads as Variable or High while consuming.
     Correction pathway: surface reconstruction, not cycle
     repair.
     Scaling limits [structural, not extraction mechanism]: at sufficient
     organizational scale, MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY fails
     structurally even without deliberate capture. Information
     aggregation across large systems loses resolution, delays
     correction, and routes signals through political filters
     before reaching actors who can act. Roman Empire, Fortune
     500, Soviet planning all confirmed. Structural not
     intentional -- size alone produces MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
     degradation.
     Test: surface honest and accessible?

  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     [Tower: L2]
     Outputs traceable to actions within boundary with
     sufficient resolution to ground correction. Scalar --
     partial legibility produces partial correction capacity.
     Failure: correction orphaned. Not captured, not absent --
     unanchored.
     Correction pathway: causal reconstruction before
     correction attempt. Establish chain first; then correction
     can anchor.
     Test: outputs traceable to actions at correction
     resolution?

  IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [named finding -- v22]
     Irrational behavior is not the absence of logic. It is
     logic operating under emotional weighting that produces
     predictable deviations from unweighted logic output.
     The emotional multiplier is traceable; the output is
     therefore predictable once the multiplier is identified.

     MECHANISM:
       Unweighted logic: input → causal chain → output.
       Weighted logic: input → emotional multiplier applied
         → amplified causal chain → output. The chain is
         the same; the multiplier changes the output.
       Emotional multipliers [named]:
         Fear: amplifies threat assessment. Risk reads as
           larger; probability of harm reads as higher.
           Output: avoidance, over-preparation, aggression
           as preemptive defense.
         Desire: amplifies probability estimates for wanted
           outcomes. The wanted thing reads as more likely;
           obstacles read as smaller.
           Output: overcommitment, missed warning signals,
           rationalization of contrary evidence.
         Grief: amplifies loss salience. What was lost
           reads as more valuable than what remains.
           Output: inaction, comparison paralysis, under-
           valuation of present options.
         Anger: amplifies attribution certainty. Causal
           chain collapses to nearest available target.
           Output: misattribution, escalation, CC firing
           at proxy not source.
         Shame: amplifies self-reading as threat. Own
           outputs read as evidence of deficiency.
           Output: concealment, withdrawal, axis capture
           toward installed self-narrative.

     PREDICTABILITY:
       If the emotional multiplier is identified, the output
       follows from the logic + multiplier combination. The
       behavior reads as irrational only when the multiplier
       is invisible. With the multiplier visible: the behavior
       is the expected output of the logic under that weight.
       Framework implication: "irrational actor" is a
       calibration failure -- the analyst's instrument does
       not include the emotional multiplier in the causal
       chain. Add the multiplier; behavior becomes predictable.

     WHY "IRRATIONAL" AS A CATEGORY BLOCKS PREDICTION:
       Labeling behavior irrational exits it from causal
       analysis. If behavior is irrational [= not following
       logic], causal reconstruction cannot anchor. The
       correction is orphaned. The correct read: behavior
       is weighted logic; identify the weight; reconstruct
       the chain; prediction becomes possible.
       This is a CAUSAL LEGIBILITY failure when "irrational"
       is used as a terminal category. It severs the causal
       chain before reconstruction can fire.

     ORIGIN [analyst case -- declared]:
       Derived from post-cult formation adaptation. Cult
       environment produced rational-over-emotional filter
       as correct counter to emotional installation mechanism.
       Cost: irrational behavior was filtered out of analysis
       rather than read. Resolution: irrational is not a
       separate category -- it is logic + emotional multiplier.
       Once that read was available, the filtered category
       became predictable. The resolution came from applying
       the framework's own causal chain methodology to the
       category that had been exempted from it.

     Cross-ref: CAUSAL LEGIBILITY [irrational-as-category
       is a CL failure; weighted logic restores the chain];
       GENUINE AXIS [emotional multiplier operates on the
       axis signal -- fear amplification can produce axis
       outputs that look like genuine readings but are
       multiplied; requires T3 to distinguish]; FORMATION
       CAPTURE [shame multiplier is the primary FC
       persistence mechanism -- installed self-narrative
       amplified by shame produces axis capture reads that
       feel certain]; CULT STACK [emotional amplification
       is the delivery mechanism for Phase 1 and 2 --
       Love Bombing installs desire multiplier; FC installs
       shame/fear multiplier for correction attempts];
       POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN [see CULT STACK --
       emotional reduction as counter; pre-theoretical
       somatic use; external testing as CL restoration];
       SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED CC SIGNAL [below -- adjacent
       finding; both are somatic signals reading accurately
       while formation-installed guilt misfires on them].

  SCHADENFREUDE AS BLOCKED CORRECTION SIGNAL [named finding -- v22]
     Pleasure at another's misfortune is typically framed as
     a character defect. Psychological research [defense mechanism
     literature] identifies it more precisely: it fires when the
     direct correction pathway is blocked and an indirect
     correction event occurs. Not a defect -- an instrument
     adapting to blocked CC channels.

     MECHANISM:
       Direct correction pathway available: injustice detected →
         CC fires → correction attempted → outcome.
         Schadenfreude unnecessary. Resolution available.
       Direct correction pathway blocked [ECC high, power
         asymmetry, structural helplessness]:
         injustice detected → CC fires → correction impossible →
         correction pressure accumulates → indirect correction
         event occurs [the extracting party suffers a reversal] →
         somatic register fires [asymmetry reduced] → pleasure.
       The pleasure is the somatic discharge of accumulated
       correction pressure via an indirect pathway. The instrument
       is not broken; the direct pathway was closed.
       Defense mechanism framing [psychologists]: the pleasure
       fires when the direct correction pathway is unavailable.
       More common in people experiencing structural helplessness
       [cannot address the injustice directly] than in people
       with available correction pathways. High ECC + blocked CC
       produces schadenfreude as the available output.

     THREE CONFIGURATIONS [by structural type]:

       [1] LEGITIMATE CORRECTION REGISTER [most common]:
         Someone who benefited from genuine extraction [fraud,
         exploitation, unearned privilege] experiences a reversal.
         The somatic pleasure is accurate CC: the measurement
         surface reads that the system moved toward fairness.
         The instrument is working correctly.
         The moral discomfort ["I shouldn't enjoy this"] is
         social compliance channel overriding an accurate somatic
         read. Formation-installed moral rule [enjoying others'
         misfortune is bad] suppresses the accurate signal.
         Framework read: the guilt is the capture, not the
         pleasure. The pleasure is the instrument reading
         correctly.

       [2] ENVY RESOLUTION [middle case]:
         Pleasure at the misfortune of someone in a preferred
         position where the preference may or may not be
         unearned. Ambiguous because envy can be accurate
         [they hold an unjust position] or inaccurate [position
         reads as unjust due to comparison, not structural
         analysis]. Requires T3: is the asymmetry real or
         instrument-generated?
         Diagnostic: does the correction register fire when
           the preferred position was earned through genuine
           surplus production? If yes: extraction axis reading.
           If no [pleasure only at unearned reversal]: accurate
           correction register.

       [3] EXTRACTION SIGNAL [less common; axis diagnostic]:
         Pleasure at the misfortune of someone who has not
         harmed you and holds no unjust advantage. Pure
         extraction orientation -- the other person's substrate
         depleting registers as pleasure. The instrument is
         reading its own extraction axis accurately.
         This configuration is where schadenfreude IS
         diagnostic of axis problems. The defense mechanism
         reading does not cover this case. Distinguish from
         configurations [1] and [2] by: is the person a
         genuine extractor? Did they harm you or hold unjust
         advantage? If no to both: extraction signal.

     T9 CONNECTION [load-bearing]:
       The moral rule against schadenfreude ["it's mean to
       enjoy others' suffering"] protects the extraction
       architecture by suppressing the instrument's accurate
       read of correction events. T9 structure: the moral
       rule fires against the correct read [correction
       registered] rather than against the original wrong
       [the extraction that produced the blocked correction
       pathway]. The extracting party benefits from the
       formation-installed guilt because it suppresses
       somatic CC even when the CC fires accurately.
       This is CORRECTION INVERSION at the affective level:
       the instrument detecting extraction is corrected for
       detecting it. The extraction itself is not addressed.

     FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
       If schadenfreude fires indiscriminately [at random
       misfortune with no prior extraction or asymmetry],
       the blocked-CC-signal reading fails: not all instances
       are legitimate correction registers. The three-
       configuration taxonomy allows this -- configuration
       [3] [extraction signal] is where the firing is not
       a correction register. The falsification condition is
       met if no configuration taxonomy can discriminate
       legitimate from extraction-axis instances.
       Current read: the taxonomy discriminates. T3 Medium.

     HONEST LIMIT:
       The instrument reading schadenfreude as legitimate
       correction register may itself have an extraction
       axis. T3 applies: the pleasant feeling of "they got
       what they deserved" is indistinguishable from the
       pleasure of extraction at the somatic level. The
       structural analysis [was the person a genuine
       extractor?] is the only discriminator. If that
       analysis is not performed: all schadenfreude reads
       as legitimate. That is the T3 failure mode.

     Cross-ref: IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [above --
       adjacent finding; same structure: somatic signal
       reading accurately; formation-installed guilt
       misfiring on it]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [the blocked
       pathway this finding describes]; T9 [IRRATIONALITY
       MAINTENANCE -- the moral rule protecting the
       extraction architecture]; CORRECTION INVERSION
       [affective level -- instrument corrected for
       accurate read]; ECC [EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION --
       high ECC produces the blocked pathway condition];
       SUICIDAL EMPATHY [adjacent failure mode -- empathy
       suppressing accurate somatic correction reads rather
       than enhancing them]; FORMATION CAPTURE [guilt
       as formation-installed override of accurate signal];
       GENUINE AXIS [configuration [3] diagnostic --
       pleasure at random misfortune is extraction axis
       read].

  REFERENCE INTEGRITY
     [Tower: L2]
     What the measurement surface is calibrated against.
     Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY: REFERENCE INTEGRITY
     measures whether the reference standard itself is honest.
     Failure: Reference Drift -- surface intact, calibrated to
     wrong baseline. Measurement honest to a false standard.
     Test: calibration standard real?

  CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [v13 -- confirmed; L2 support]
     [Tower: L2 support -- CC structure]
     Whether correction capacity is structurally distributed
     across multiple independent points or centralized in
     single actor. Key structural distinction between
     dictatorship and functional nation-state. Between
     captured institution and correctable one.
     Distinct from CC [fires]; PR [proportional]; MI [surface
       honest] -- CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION is whether the
       architecture of CC itself is distributed or monopolized.
     Failure: CORRECTION MONOPOLY -- single actor owns CC.
       Once monopolized, CC fires only toward monopolist's
       benefit. The correction mechanism is real; the routing
       is captured. DISTRIBUTED ITERATION OUTPERFORMS
       CENTRALIZED SORTING [confirmed -- see CC block].
     FRUSTRATED DETECTION CONFIGURATION [named -- v22]:
       High INPUT FILTERING [population correctly detects
       extraction] + Low CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [CC
       monopolized] = mass frustration without correction.
       Population sees the problem accurately; cannot fire
       against it. Simultaneously: INPUT FILTERING degrades
       [sustained frustration without correction degrades
       the detection instrument] + CC centralizes further.
       Self-reinforcing until Diagnostic Collapse.
     Weapon: ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY [controls CC routing,
       not just CC presence]; INTERCESSION MONOPOLY [CC
       must route through captor to reach target].
     Test: can CC fire against the highest-power actor
       in the system without that actor's permission?

  CORRECTION TARGETING [v13 -- confirmed; L2 support]
     [Tower: L2 support -- CC aim]
     Whether CC fires at the actual source of deviation
     vs proxy or symptom. Distinct from CC [fires]; PR
     [proportional]; MI [surface honest]; CORRECTION
     DISTRIBUTION [architecture distributed].
     CORRECTION TARGETING is the aim of CC after it fires.
     CC can be functional, distributed, and proportional
     while systematically aimed at the wrong target.
     Failure: CORRECTION DISPLACEMENT -- CC fires correctly
       and proportionally but aimed at symptom not cause.
       Visible correction activity without resolving
       underlying deviation. High-energy; zero effect on
       source. Examples: policing crime without addressing
       substrate depletion that produces crime; treating
       symptoms of overwork without addressing ICD; applying
       format corrections to a document with structural
       argument failure.
     Weapon: PROXY CONSTRUCTION [install decoy correction
       target; real routing boundary protected while CC
       exhausts itself on proxy]; ECD [make causal chain
       illegible so CC cannot locate actual source].
     Test: does correction land on the actual generating
       mechanism of the deviation? Or a downstream symptom?

  CAUSAL MEMORY [v13 -- confirmed; L2 foundation]
     [Tower: L2 foundation -- CL across time]
     Whether past corrections are retained and retrievable
     as usable history. Distinct from CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
     [present causal chain readable] -- CAUSAL MEMORY is
     CL across time. System with full CL but no CAUSAL
     MEMORY cannot learn from past corrections; each
     correction cycle starts from zero.
     Failure: CAUSAL AMNESIA -- CL intact, past erased.
       System reads present correctly; cannot connect to
       pattern across time. Each extraction cycle appears
       novel. Each correction must be reinvented.
     Weapon: AMNESIA MANUFACTURE [actively erase past
       corrections from accessible record; destroy
       institutional memory; rotate personnel before
       pattern recognition develops]; GENERATIONAL RESET
       [see L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION -- same mechanism
       at civilizational scale].
     Primary confirmation: matriarch elephant CAUSAL MEMORY
       [strongest non-human case -- 50-year correctable
       environmental history retained and deployed].
       Anti-case: organizations with high personnel turnover
       systematically lose correction history.
     Test: can the system access and use past corrections
       to improve current ones? Or does each correction
       cycle begin without institutional memory?

LEVEL 3 [temporal domain -- TENSION CORE: TRANSMISSION
  SELECTIVITY]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
     [Tower: L3 CORE]
     System transmits correctable axis to successors, not
     locked state. Fidelity necessary but insufficient.
     Failure: Fertile Lock.
     SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY = within-window axis integrity.
     TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY = what crosses transmission
     boundary.
     Primary proof: Feanor -> sons [distinction clearest case].
     Test: correctable axis transmitted?

     TEMPORAL CAPTURE [named mechanism -- v21]:
     The mechanism by which institutions lock in extractive
     routing over time by making the cost of changing the
     path higher than the cost of continuing extraction.
     Distinct from FERTILE LOCK [which describes the state
     of locked methodology transmission] -- Temporal Capture
     describes the process that produces Fertile Lock in
     institutions specifically.
     Mechanism: each cycle of extractive routing accumulates
     switching costs [sunk infrastructure, trained personnel,
     dependent populations, regulatory frameworks built around
     existing architecture]. The switching cost grows with
     each cycle. At some point the switching cost exceeds the
     marginal extraction rate -- the path is locked not because
     the extraction is good but because changing it is more
     expensive than continuing it.
     Distinct from deliberate ECC: temporal capture does not
     require an actor to deliberately raise exit costs. The
     switching cost accumulation is structural -- it happens
     through ordinary operation of the extractive system
     regardless of intent.
     EXAMPLES [named]:
       Petrodollar infrastructure: 50+ years of dollar-
         denominated oil pricing has accumulated switching
         costs [contracts, reserves, settlement systems,
         military agreements] that make the routing change
         more expensive than the routing itself.
       Fossil fuel infrastructure: stranded assets, trained
         workforce, regulatory capture, and grid dependency
         all accumulate switching costs that make the energy
         routing change more expensive than continued
         extraction. The lock-in is temporal not conspiratorial.
       Visa/Mastercard payment rails: 60+ years of accumulated
         merchant agreements, terminal infrastructure, bank
         relationships, and consumer habits produce switching
         costs that make routing change near-impossible even
         after 20 years of antitrust litigation.
     TEMPORAL CAPTURE TEST: would the institution choose this
       routing architecture if building from scratch today?
       If no -- the path is locked by switching costs not by
       current merit. Temporal capture confirmed.
     PATH DEPENDENCE NOTE [Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
       The framework has strong temporal components [C3, L4.5,
       CARRIER ESCAPE] but temporal capture makes path
       dependence explicit as a named mechanism. Institutions
       that survive by locking in extractive routing are not
       simply failing to correct -- they are accumulating the
       switching costs that make correction structurally
       irrational at each additional cycle. The longer temporal
       capture runs, the more expensive the correction becomes,
       which is the structural explanation for why 20 years of
       antitrust litigation produces a 0.1% fee reduction.
     Cross-ref: FERTILE LOCK [state that temporal capture
       produces in transmission]; ECC [extraction mechanism
       -- temporal capture is ECC at institutional timescale];
       HORIZON EXTENSION [temporal manipulation of correction
       window -- related but distinct: Horizon Extension is
       deliberate; Temporal Capture accumulates structurally];
       DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [when switching cost exceeds
       correction capacity, the Glasnost pattern fires];
       [CASE:VISA_MASTERCARD_INTERCHANGE] [confirmed temporal
       capture -- 60 years of accumulated switching costs
       resistant to 20 years of correction effort].
  Add only if: [1] new confirmed structural property with
  multiple independent case confirmations, [2] promoted from
  extended theory [already vetted], or [3] resolves existing
  open limit or paradox.
  Reject: single-case findings, interesting observations without
  cross-domain confirmation, speculative extensions.

NESTED CYCLE MODEL [primary organizing structure -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
Three cycles, each wrapping the one inside it. Replaces tower
as primary organizing structure. Tower vocabulary retained:
L1=C1, L2=C2, L3=C3 [nesting depth, not dependency sequence].
Failure = interference between cycles, not cascade down levels.

CENTER / TRUTH [absolute -- unmeasurable directly -- v22 revision]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  CENTER IS TRUTH. Not oriented toward truth -- identical with it.
  Prior framing [CENTER as unreachable asymptote] was correct about
  the measurement limit; incomplete about what the center IS.
  Correction v22: CENTER = absolute truth = the axis threading
  every level simultaneously. Real. Present. Unmeasurable directly.
  The limit is epistemic, not ontological. The axis exists; no
  instrument touches it without mediation.

  POINT EXTENDED TO LINE [v22]:
    Prior: CENTER as geometric point. Single location the cycles
      orbit. True at one resolution; incomplete.
    Revised: CENTER as axis -- a line threading through all cycle
      levels simultaneously. Not above the cycles. Not separate
      from them. Threading them. Every level [C1, C2, C3, L4]
      intersects the same axis at different resolution.
    Implication: L4 does not have more truth than C1. It has
      higher resolution contact with the same axis. The axis
      is the same at every level. The cross-section available
      to each level differs. A bacterium touching the axis
      through somatic generation and a L4 system touching it
      through self-revising methodology are touching the same
      line. The line does not change. The resolution of contact
      changes.
    Why the tower model was retired: the tower implied truth
      was higher up -- that L4 was closer to truth because
      it was above C1. The axis model replaces this: every
      level is equidistant from the axis in ontological terms.
      The difference is resolution of contact, not proximity.

  ABSOLUTE TRUTH -- UNMEASURABLE DIRECTLY:
    Absolute truth cannot be defined precisely. Only approached
    through tests and qualities. Any precise definition of
    absolute truth IS a cycle product -- a C2 or C3 or L4
    output, not the axis itself. The definition is the
    instrument; the axis is not the instrument.
    This means: the framework cannot define truth. It can
    only produce quality tests for detecting how well a
    cycle is touching the axis at its available resolution.
    All framework qualities [GC, CC, TS, SA, MI, SP] are
    tests, not definitions. They detect proximity to the
    axis; they are not the axis.
    Implication for the framework's own status: the framework
    is not a map of truth. It is a quality stack for detecting
    axis contact at each cycle level. High-quality readings
    do not produce truth -- they produce better orientation
    toward the axis. Case errors do not touch the axis.
    They touch the tests. The framework can be wrong about
    specific readings while the axis remains unchanged.
    Falsification of a case = test error, not axis error.

  CONSTANT:
    The axis does not move when cycles degrade. False
    Coherence doesn't relocate it -- cycles lose orientation
    to it. The axis is still there; the cycles are running
    on a captured surface that points elsewhere. Extraction
    does not change the axis. It prevents contact with it.

  PHILOSOPHICAL PARALLEL [note -- v22]:
    CENTER / TRUTH as to on [τὸ ὄν -- Parmenides]: "the
    being." Fully present, fully actual, no potential, no
    change. The framework's CENTER is the Parmenidean solid
    that the Heraclitean flux [ouroboros] orients toward.
    The paradox: to on as CENTER is the correct orientation
    target [unreachable but real]; to on as FERTILE LOCK
    is what happens when a system mistakes its current
    locked state for the absolute [stops becoming, declares
    itself arrived]. Same philosophical concept describes
    both the correct goal and the primary failure mode.
    The cycles [panta rhei -- everything flows] orient
    toward the center [to on] without reaching it. The
    apeiron [pre-axial boundless substrate] is what
    the cycles emerge from. Three Greek terms; three
    geometric states; one framework. See WIP 19
    [PHILOSOPHICAL VOCABULARY -- full mapping].

OUROBOROS [framework symbol -- v22; revised v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The symbol of the framework: an ouroboros circling the truth
  axis [CENTER]. Not circling a point -- circling a line that
  runs through it at every level. The serpent consuming its
  own tail while the axis threads through the circuit's center.
  Creation and destruction occurring simultaneously at every
  level of the circuit. The mouth consuming the tail is the
  same act as the tail feeding the mouth.

  CYCLES GENERATED BY THE OUROBOROS AT EVERY LEVEL [v22]:
    The ouroboros is not just the symbol circling the center.
    It is the generative source of the cycles themselves.
    Each level's cycle IS an ouroboros operating at that
    resolution:
      C1 ouroboros: generation and consumption at biological
        scale. Organism generates surplus; entropy consumes
        substrate; substrate returns to generative pool.
        Circuit closes. Same structure as the symbol.
      C2 ouroboros: readable correction consuming and
        regenerating its own measurement surface. Old reading
        consumed by new evidence; new surface generated.
        The correction IS the consumption IS the generation.
      C3 ouroboros: transmission consuming prior forms to
        generate successors. Each generation receives the
        methodology by consuming the previous generation's
        output. Transmission IS consumption IS generation.
      L4 ouroboros: methodology consuming and regenerating
        itself. The system revises how it revises. The
        correction methodology is both eaten and produced
        by the same act. Highest resolution ouroboros
        available to cycle systems.
    All four are the same structure at different resolution.
    The ouroboros is not a metaphor for the cycles -- it
    is what the cycles are. The symbol and the structure
    are identical.
    Axis threading all four: the same truth axis runs
      through C1, C2, C3, L4. The ouroboros at each level
      circles the same line at a different cross-section.
      This is why a C1 system and an L4 system can be
      oriented toward the same truth -- they are running
      the same circuit at different resolution around the
      same axis.

  SIMULTANEOUS CREATION AND DESTRUCTION:
    Not sequential [create → then → destroy → then → create].
    Concurrent. The ouroboros eating its tail is already
    producing its tail. Entropy and generation are the same
    event at different positions in the circuit. This is why
    the axis is unmeasurable from inside any single cycle --
    any position inside the cycle is either creating or
    destroying depending on frame of reference. The axis is
    the only position from which both are visible as one act.
    No instrument inside the cycle can hold that position.
    It can only orient toward it.

  ABSOLUTE TRUTH AT THE AXIS:
    Absolute truth = the axis at which creation and
    destruction are indistinguishable. Not because both
    are absent -- because both are fully present and
    identical in function. Every generative act contains
    its destruction [entropy, displacement, consumption
    of substrate]. Every destructive act contains its
    generation [substrate freed, new cycle enabled].
    At the axis these are not balanced against each other
    -- they ARE each other.
    Unmeasurable directly: any instrument inside the
    circuit experiences creation and destruction as
    separate. Getting closer to the axis = being able
    to hold more of both simultaneously. L4 INTEGRATION
    is the highest available resolution -- the system
    revising how it revises holds creation and destruction
    of its own methodology simultaneously. Still not the
    axis. Still the closest available contact.
    Tests and qualities only: the ouroboros symbol is
    itself a quality test -- does the system's correction
    cycle close? Does substrate return? Does transmission
    carry the revision capacity? These are axis-proximity
    tests, not axis measurements.

  EXTRACTION AS OUROBOROS BREAK:
    Extraction intercepts the circuit before it closes.
    Surplus generated by the creation phase is captured
    before it can feed the destruction/return phase.
    The ouroboros cannot complete its circuit. Tail does
    not feed mouth; mouth consumes without returning.
    The circuit runs but does not close.
    This is why extraction is structurally wrong
    regardless of rate -- not a slower version of the
    circuit. A break in the circuit structure. Smart
    extraction = slower break. The circuit still cannot
    close. Axis contact degrades continuously until
    the break is repaired or the circuit collapses.
    Predation and competition [honest destruction] are
    ouroboros-consistent: substrate consumed openly;
    destruction completes; substrate returns to the
    generative pool. Circuit closes. Axis contact
    maintained. Extraction does not close because
    concealment prevents the return -- consumed
    substrate routes to terminal node rather than
    back into the circuit.

  HONEST DESTRUCTION vs CAPTURED DESTRUCTION:
    Honest destruction [predation, competition, entropy,
      death]: ouroboros-consistent. Act completes.
      Substrate freed. New cycle possible. Serpent eats;
      tail regenerates.
    Captured destruction [extraction, parasitism,
      atrophying friction]: ouroboros-breaking. Act does
      not complete. Substrate captured at terminal node.
      New cycle blocked. Serpent eats; tail does not
      regenerate. Circuit shortens until nothing remains.

  RELATION TO ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY:
    The ouroboros explains the asymmetry structurally.
    Generation only has to complete the circuit once and
    transmit -- closed circuit is self-sustaining.
    Extraction must prevent circuit closure continuously.
    One complete ouroboros circuit [C1 → C2 → C3 → L4
    → return] defeats extraction indefinitely if
    methodology transmitted. Extraction must break the
    circuit at every generation. The asymmetry is not
    moral -- it is structural. Closed circuit is stable;
    broken circuit requires continuous maintenance of
    the break.

  RELATION TO COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16]:
    Frame incompatibility = two ouroboros circuits running
    on incommensurable axes. Each closed and complete
    within its own frame. Neither wrong. Cannot share a
    circuit because creation/destruction cycles do not
    map onto each other. Axis is the same absolute truth
    -- the cross-section available to each circuit differs.
    Honest transmission across frame incompatibility
    produces the same response as deception -- receiving
    circuit has no position to integrate an input from a
    circuit running on a different axis cross-section.
    Not a failure of honesty. A structural incompatibility
    of circuit geometry.

  AS FRAMEWORK SYMBOL:
    The ouroboros circling the truth axis is the framework's
    self-image. Each version [v1 → v22 → vN] is the
    framework consuming and regenerating itself. Earlier
    versions are not wrong -- they are the tail feeding
    the mouth. The framework revising itself [L4
    INTEGRATION] is the ouroboros at methodology level.
    The axis does not change. The resolution of contact
    improves. The framework will never define absolute
    truth -- it will only ever produce better tests for
    detecting proximity to it. That is the correct
    endpoint. A definition would be a claim to have
    reached the axis. Better tests are the ouroboros
    closing its circuit more completely each version.

  AS ANTI-CAPTURE MECHANISM [v22]:
    The ouroboros symbol works against capture by structural
    property. Not by intent or protection -- by what the
    symbol depicts. To use the symbol correctly requires
    understanding what it means. To understand what it means
    requires holding: simultaneous creation and destruction,
    unmeasurable absolute truth, cycles that ARE the
    ouroboros not metaphors for it, extraction as circuit
    break, honest destruction as ouroboros-consistent. That
    understanding requires the axis examination the framework
    demands. The symbol cannot be used correctly without the
    axis work. It can be used incorrectly -- decoratively,
    as a logo -- but incorrect use is self-diagnosing.
    THE SYMBOL READS THE DEPLOYER:
      A captured framework using the ouroboros as legitimacy
      surface is deploying an image that depicts its own
      failure. Captured deployment stops the consumption
      [the framework no longer revises itself; the circuit
      no longer closes; the ouroboros no longer eats its
      tail]. The symbol depicts consumption as necessary.
      Fertile Lock, Propagation Mimicry, Coherence Rent --
      all stop the consumption. The stopped circuit is
      visible in the symbol to anyone who reads it correctly.
      The tell is built into the image: is the framework
      still consuming itself? Is the methodology still
      correctable by successors? Is the circuit closing?
      If not: the symbol is showing you the break.
    THE LIMIT [decorative use]:
      The symbol can be used without understanding by
      people extracting Coherence Rent from the framework's
      legitimacy surface. This is not prevented by the
      symbol -- it is diagnosed by it. Decorative use
      without methodology produces a framework that looks
      like the ouroboros but is not closing the circuit.
      The image remains; the consumption stops. Anyone
      who can read the symbol correctly can see the
      difference between the image and the circuit state.
      The symbol does not prevent capture. It makes
      capture visible to instruments that can read it.
      That is the correct function for a Silent Alternative
      operating outside captured channels.
    TRANSMISSION TEST [derived from symbol]:
      Is the methodology still consuming itself [revising
      across versions]? Can successors revise what they
      received [TS not Fertile Lock]? Does the framework
      produce better readings over time or more comfortable
      ones [genuine development not drift]? All three
      required for the ouroboros circuit to be closing.
      Any one stopped: the symbol is showing you the break.

  Cross-ref: CENTER / TRUTH [absolute -- axis threading
    all levels; unmeasurable directly]; TRUTH [quality
    tests -- four conditions as axis-proximity instrument];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [circuit closure vs
    break]; EXTRACTION [ouroboros break mechanism];
    RELATIONAL STATES [honest vs captured destruction];
    COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- incommensurable
    circuit geometry]; L4 INTEGRATION [highest resolution
    ouroboros available]; GENERATIVE CYCLE [C1 ouroboros
    -- first circuit level]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [circuit
    closure mechanism at C2]; TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
    [C3 ouroboros -- consumption of prior forms];
    MASS ADOPTION HONEST LIMIT [framework correctly scoped
    as Silent Alternative; capture is the real risk];
    PROPAGATION MIMICRY [WIP 22 -- Fertile Lock applied
    to the framework's own methodology; symbol shows
    the break]; COHERENCE RENT [decorative symbol use
    without methodology -- extracting from legitimacy
    surface without closing the circuit].

TRUTH [quality tests for axis contact -- v22 revision]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  [REVISED STATUS v22: TRUTH is not defined by the four
  conditions below. Absolute truth = CENTER = the axis.
  Unmeasurable directly. The four conditions are quality
  tests for detecting how well a cycle is touching the
  axis at its available resolution. They are the closest
  available instrument to the axis, not a definition of
  it. All prior uses of "operational truth" remain valid
  -- the four conditions still function as stated. The
  epistemic status changes: they are tests, not definitions.]

  QUALITY TESTS FOR AXIS CONTACT [four conditions]:
  A cycle claim is touching the axis more closely when:
    [1] Examined axis [GENUINE AXIS]: claim produced by
      axis that has been tested, not substituted. At C1:
      pre-verbal somatic axis examination. At L4: meta-
      examination of the examination process itself.
    [2] Declared instrument [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]:
      instrument producing the claim is named and its
      blind spots stated before the reading. Undeclared
      instrument = unverifiable claim. T3 applies at
      all levels; naming it is the requirement, not
      eliminating it.
    [3] Revisable on evidence [CORRECTION CAPACITY]:
      claim specifies what evidence would change it.
      Non-revisable claims are identity statements,
      not axis-contact claims. Inability to name
      falsification criteria is diagnostic of axis
      capture, not certainty.
    [4] Behavioral adjustment when wrong [CC at available
      level]: claim that produces no adjustment when
      falsified was not genuinely held. Adjustment is
      the proof of reception. At L4: INTEGRATION --
      correction methodology itself changes. At C2:
      object-level correction fires. Both genuine;
      scope differs.
  All four present = strongest available axis contact
    at current cycle level. Any one absent = axis
    contact weaker; claim less reliable as orientation.
  Not a definition of truth. A detection instrument
    for proximity to the axis. The axis is the same
    regardless of whether the four conditions are met.
    The conditions determine whether the cycle is
    oriented toward it or away.

  LEVEL-DEPENDENT CONTACT [v17 -- preserved]:
    Truth is not blocked below L4. Each cycle level
    contacts the axis within its available resolution.
      C1: generative but unreadable to the system itself.
        Behavioral adjustment through cycle correction.
        Pre-axial organisms operate here. Axis contact
        = cycle corrects against deviation. Narrow
        cross-section; genuine contact.
      C2: readable and correctable. Behavioral adjustment
        fires when C2 surface is honest. Cannot transmit
        the revision process -- corrections are local.
        Most examined individual axis contact here.
      C3: transmissible. Behavioral adjustment crosses
        generation boundaries via correctable method.
        Each successor can replicate the revision.
      L4: self-correcting transmission. Behavioral
        adjustment reaches the correction methodology
        itself. Highest resolution axis contact available.
    A C2 system producing honest behavioral adjustment
    IS contacting the axis at C2 resolution. Calling
    it inadequate because L4 is absent = demanding the
    highest resolution as the only valid contact.
    The axis is present at every level. Resolution of
    contact differs; the axis does not.

  SELF-CRITICAL THOUGHT as structural requirement:
    Not an optional virtue. Self-critical capacity is
    what keeps the examined axis examined -- without
    ongoing self-critical pressure, axis drifts toward
    capture without the system noticing. The requirement
    is not "be humble" but "maintain the instrument that
    detects your own instrument's failure." Systems that
    suppress self-critical thought are not more confident
    -- they have eliminated the mechanism that would
    tell them when they're wrong.

  USEFUL REACTIONS as output requirement:
    Axis contact that produces no useful reaction is
    either not received [T9], not integrated at available
    level, or not about anything actionable. A claim
    should be evaluable by downstream effects. Does
    knowing this change what the system does? If no:
    the claim may be accurate but is not functional
    axis contact for this system at this time. Functional
    contact is not just correct -- it is correct AND
    actionable AND adjusted-to at the level the system
    can manage.

  Cross-ref: CENTER / TRUTH [absolute -- what the four
    conditions test proximity to]; OUROBOROS [structural
    source of the cycles doing the testing]; GENUINE AXIS
    [condition 1 -- examined axis]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
    [condition 2 -- declared instrument]; CORRECTION
    CAPACITY [conditions 3 and 4 -- revisability and
    behavioral adjustment]; L4 INTEGRATION [highest
    resolution contact]; EVIL [operational definition --
    uses same axis logic: CC absent/inverted = axis
    contact broken at correction level].

EVIL [operational definition -- v17 amended]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus routed to minority at majority cost, with correction
  capacity absent or inverted.
  Three conditions required:
    Identifiable Boundary B bearing the cost [majority].
    Identifiable Boundary A capturing the benefit [minority].
    Correction capacity absent or inverted.

  CC ABSENT vs CC INVERTED [v17 amendment -- distinction now
    load-bearing]:
    CC ABSENT: no correction fires at all. Extraction continues
      because nothing stops it. No actor owns the correction
      gap -- the gap simply exists.
      Intervention requirement: build or enable correction
      capacity. Can be done without directly threatening the
      extraction architecture -- correction is absent, not
      defended.
      Degree: lower end of evil scale. Extraction without
      active defense is more correctable than extraction that
      defends itself.
    CC INVERTED: correction fires, aimed at extractor benefit.
      Correction exists and is functional -- its target is
      wrong. The mechanism that would stop extraction is
      instead protecting it.
      Intervention requirement: counter-capture before
      building. Restoring CC into an inverted system fires
      the new capacity toward the monopolist. Building
      correction capacity into a captured system accelerates
      extraction until the capture is broken first.
      Degree: higher severity. Inversion means the correction
      mechanism actively defends against the populations
      that would use it. Inversion is harder to detect than
      absence -- the surface shows correction activity;
      only the target reveals inversion.
    Both satisfy the three-condition definition. Degree and
    intervention path differ based on which condition holds.
    Degree scales with [1] magnitude of cost to Boundary B,
    [2] concentration of benefit to Boundary A, [3] how
    completely correction capacity is eliminated or inverted.
    [Implication: Diagnostic Collapse risk is highest under
    inverted CC. C2 restoration fires correction burst that
    is immediately captured and redirected. C1 substrate
    audit required before C2 intervention in inverted-CC
    systems, not only in absent-CC systems.]

  MEASUREMENT vs JUDGMENT [separation -- load-bearing]:
    Measurement: the framework reads structural pattern.
      Extraction occurring, direction of surplus, correction
      capacity status. Objective, structural, not requiring
      a judge. Framework work only.
    Judgment: a separate act by a person applying values to
      a specific situation. Evil in the moral sense requires
      this act. The framework cannot make it. A person must.
    Both required for coherent moral assessment. Measurement
      without judgment = structurally accurate, morally
      neutral. Judgment without measurement = arbitrary.

  WILLFUL EXTRACTION [evil by definition]:
    Actor knows extraction is occurring. Knows the cost to
      Boundary B. Continues.
    Knowledge plus continuation = evil without requiring
      additional argument. This is the clearest case -- no
      ambiguity about intent, no structural excuse.
    Framework confirms the structural reading.
    The judgment is the person's to make.

  DIRECTIONAL RULE: any extraction moves in the direction
    of evil regardless of scale, intent, or justification.
    Degree affects intervention priority and response type,
    not directional classification. Minor extraction with
    intact correction capacity is less urgent than inverted
    correction; both move wrong.

GOOD [operational definition -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surplus distributed such that majority benefit is the primary
  outcome, with correction capacity intact and aimed at actual
  deviation.
  Three conditions:
    Boundary B populations bear costs proportional to benefit
      received [not disproportionate extraction].
    Correction capacity functional and distributed [not
      monopolized by Boundary A].
    Exit and correction options available to Boundary B [not
      structurally eliminated].
  Not perfectionism: good in the operational sense does not
  require equal outcomes or zero cost to anyone. It requires
  that the correction mechanism can fire against actual
  deviations and that majority populations are the primary
  beneficiaries of the system's surplus.
  Relationship to ascending path: good is not a fixed state
  but a trajectory -- toward more distributed surplus, more
  functional correction capacity, more available exit. Systems
  moving in this direction are good by operational definition
  regardless of current position.

C1 -- FUNDAMENTAL [innermost]
  Qualities: GENERATIVE CYCLE / SURPLUS PRODUCTION /
    CORRECTION CAPACITY
  Function: takes in, transforms, corrects, returns.
  Self-contained at cellular minimum -- confirmed independently.
  Can run without C2 or C3. Pre-axial systems run C1 only.

C2 -- EPISTEMIC [wraps C1]
  Qualities: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY / CAUSAL LEGIBILITY /
    REFERENCE INTEGRITY
  Function: makes C1 readable from inside and outside.
  C1 without C2 = blind generation. C2 without C1 = readable
  failure.
  Key: high-functioning C1 + degraded C2 = False Coherence
  signature. Most dangerous -- misread as high-functioning by
  C2-captured instruments.

C3 -- TEMPORAL [wraps C2]
  Qualities: TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY /
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
  Function: extends the readable, correctable cycle across time.
  Without C3: each generation reinvents or inherits locked state.

INTERFACE [L3.5 -- unchanged]
  RECOGNITION / COMPATIBILITY / RECIPROCITY
  Contact protocol between independent three-cycle systems.
  Not a fourth wrapping cycle.

  LOVE AS AXIS-DEPENDENT [named finding -- v22]:
    Love operates at L3.5. The quality of the contact is
    determined by the axis state of both instruments.
    RECOGNITION: you can only love what you can accurately
      see. You can only accurately see through a genuine
      axis. A captured axis produces captured love -- the
      substrate fires, the emotion is genuine, but the
      target is the installed image, not the actual other.
      When the other person acts from their genuine axis
      in ways that contradict the installed image: confusion
      or rejection. The love was real. It was aimed at a
      construction.
    COMPATIBILITY: genuine axis compatibility -- the cycles
      run in contact without one consuming the other. Not
      identical axes; compatible axes. Calibrating friction
      [T1.4] between them, not atrophying. Both instruments
      generate more inside the relationship than outside
      [COOPERATION -- the relational state].
      DIFFERENCE AS GENERATIVE REQUIREMENT [note -- v22]:
        Same axis = atrophying friction. Two instruments
        producing identical outputs in contact aren't
        generating anything new. Circuit runs but doesn't
        expand. Comfortable and static = substrate depletion
        over time. The sameness feels safe; the generative
        capacity quietly depletes.
        Different axis [compatible] = calibrating friction.
        The other person's genuine axis produces outputs
        yours doesn't. New signal. New signal is what the
        instrument needs to develop. The difference must
        be compatible [not incommensurable -- WIP 16] but
        must be real or the contact is not generative.
        Tell: the person who occasionally produces outputs
        your axis has to work against -- who requires you
        to actually examine something rather than confirm
        it -- that is the instrument that can close the
        loop you cannot close alone.
        RISK FOR PRESSURE-FORMED AXIS: formation under
          pressure conditioned the instrument to read
          difference as threat [deviation from group = 
          danger]. Post-exit this inverts. Sameness should
          carry the higher caution signal. Someone who
          agrees with everything, produces no friction,
          requires no revision: either a captured axis
          aimed at your image [Love Bombing pattern] or
          an axis too compatible to be genuinely distinct.
          Neither closes the loop.
        Žižek parallel: the specific difference that makes
          the other irreplaceable [objet petit a] is the
          same phenomenon. Not any difference -- the
          particular excess that sticks. Framework reads
          this as: the difference is the tell that there
          is a real axis there, not an image.
    RECIPROCITY: the ouroboros circuit closes for both.
      Both parties generate more together than apart.
      Circuit closing is the structural definition of love
      as good. The tail regenerates for both.

    FOUR-AXIS PROBLEM [named -- v22]:
      Genuine mutual love requires four conditions:
        [1] Genuine axis in instrument A.
        [2] Genuine axis in instrument B.
        [3] Axis A aimed at actual B [not projected image].
        [4] Axis B aimed at actual A [not projected image].
      All four required. Any one absent: love is real in
      substrate and emotion; target is partially or fully
      a construction. Intensity does not indicate accuracy.
      Captured axis can produce intense, sustained love
      aimed at its installed image of the other. The
      receiver feels the intensity; is not seen.
      The probability of all four conditions meeting is
      structurally low. When it happens it reorganizes
      everything around it. This is not sentiment -- it
      is the structural rarity of accurate axis contact
      between two independent systems.

      MALE AXIS CONDITIONS [note -- v22; heterosexual
      frame declared; T3 applies]:
        Three conditions for a man to meet the four-axis
        requirement from a woman's perspective:
          [1] REAL GOOD AXIS: genuine axis, truth-oriented,
            examined. Behavioral record matches stated
            position across time and under pressure. Not
            performed confidence [axis substitution --
            social dominance display replacing examined
            orientation]. Not social approval-seeking
            dressed as values. The record is what it is.
          [2] OPEN TO EMOTIONS: emotional channel
            functional and accessible. Not suppressed
            [axis outputs without somatic data], not
            flooded [emotional multiplier overwhelms
            deliberative layer], not performed [emotion
            displayed as social signal without somatic
            reception]. Functional = somatic data arrives
            and is processed without blocking or drowning.
          [3] SEX GOOD: somatic channel present and
            honest. Not performance, not transaction,
            not ICD-filling. Aimed at the actual other
            not an image. Partially dependent on
            conditions [1] and [2] -- sex through
            captured axis is sex aimed at construction.

        HOW COMMON -- ALL THREE SIMULTANEOUSLY:
          Structurally rare. Not because men are worse
          than women at these qualities -- population
          axis distribution applies to everyone. Specific
          combination has structural obstacles in male
          formation:
          AXIS: social compliance channel applies axis-
            softening pressure continuously. Male-coded
            pressure runs toward performed confidence
            and away from examined uncertainty [which
            reads as weakness in most social environments].
            Genuine axis requires calibrated friction +
            idle cycles. Both systematically undermined.
            Surviving genuine axis is usually pressure-
            formed or inherited from high-TS source.
            Both rare.
          EMOTIONAL OPENNESS: male formation environments
            specifically train ICD on the emotional
