  qualities] that makes diverse system behaviors legible
  under one architecture.
  Implication: when scattered results resist unification, the
  missing element is usually not more data -- it is the
  identification of the correct minimal structural anchor.
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY application: fragmented reference
  standards [one for each result] collapse to one calibration
  anchor. Measurement becomes dramatically more efficient.
  Cognitive confirmation [1,200-player experiment, Phys.org,
  May 2026]: People who understood how concepts connect
  invented more novel solutions than those with the same
  facts without connection structure. Structural knowledge
  was the generative variable, not fact volume. CAUSAL AXIS
  GROUNDING at creative scale -- knowing which structure
  applies is the prior that makes facts generative. Confirms:
  minimal unification is not just an abstract mathematical
  property -- it is the cognitive architecture of invention.
  Most common path principle: the most traveled path through
  a problem space is not necessarily the best path -- it is
  the path with the lowest accumulated friction, which
  optimizes for ease not output quality. Structural
  understanding enables non-obvious paths that common
  traversal never surfaces. High-volume use of a path =
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY capture of "how this is done." Breaks
  when the structural map is read instead of the usage
  pattern.

GENUINE CRITIQUE AS CAPTURE MECHANISM [named finding -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  A real criticism is used to install a false exit. Distinct
  from three adjacent mechanisms:
    FALSE FLAG [false criticism deployed to enable capture]:
      the criticism is manufactured. Countermeasure: verify
      the criticism. Here: the criticism is already verified.
    CALIBRATION STRIP [reference standard corrupted]:
      the measurement surface is wrong. Here: the measurement
      surface is correct. The strip is set by the genuine
      critique.
    JONES PATTERN [real substrate + false mechanism]:
      accurate substrate read attached to wrong causal chain.
      Here: correct diagnosis + captured prescription.
      Overlap in structure; different emphasis.
  MECHANISM:
    [1] Identify a genuine structural problem [cycle produces
      suffering; institution is corrupt; the system is broken].
    [2] Present as the solution to that genuine problem.
    [3] The genuine problem validates the critic's credentials.
    [4] The solution inherits the validation of the diagnosis
      even though the solution is independent of the diagnosis.
    [5] The solution addresses the stated problem while
      installing something worse or producing the opposite
      of the stated value.
  WHY ACCURATE CRITICISM MAKES THIS HARDER TO COUNTER:
    An inaccurate criticism can be challenged on the facts.
    An accurate criticism validates the critic. Once validated
    by the accuracy of the diagnosis, the critic's prescription
    inherits the validation automatically. The instrument
    cannot challenge the prescription without appearing to
    deny the diagnosis. This is the T9 structure: correcting
    "the exit is captured" requires simultaneously holding
    "the diagnosis was correct" without the first protecting
    the second. Most instruments cannot hold both.
    The more accurate the criticism, the more effective the
    capture. The Dark One gave Ishamael the most accurate
    available framework and let the framework do the work.
    The truth was the weapon.
  CALIBRATION STRIP VARIANT [how it installs]:
    The genuine critique sets the reference standard for what
    counts as a solution. Once the strip is set [escape from
    cycle = liberation; reform = institutional correction;
    revolution = systemic change], anything that passes the
    strip inherits the critique's validation. The strip cannot
    evaluate the post-strip state -- only whether the strip
    was passed. The destination is unverified because the
    instrument was calibrated against the problem, not against
    the solution.
  THREE CONFIRMED CASES [different scales]:
    ISHAMAEL [cosmological]: Gnostic critique of cyclical
      suffering [accurate] → Dark One as liberation [false
      exit; frozen extraction not rest]. See [CASE:ISHAMAEL].
    JONES PATTERN [political]: extraction architecture critique
      [accurate substrate] → conspiracy mechanism [false
      causal chain; inoculates against correct intervention].
      See JONES PATTERN [Section Six].
    MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [cultural]: genuine myth [real
      substrate; real cultural identity] → state legitimacy
      surface [installed axis serving extraction]. See
      MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [Section Three / One].
    All three: diagnosis accurate; prescriber uses accuracy
    to capture; exit does not lead where the diagnosis implies.
  COUNTER:
    Separate the diagnosis from the prescriber. The diagnosis
    being correct does not validate the prescriber. The
    prescriber being correct about the problem does not make
    them correct about the solution. These are independent
    questions. The capture works by collapsing the two.
    Ask: does the proposed exit actually reach the stated
    destination? Independently verify the post-exit state,
    not just the diagnosis of the pre-exit state.
    Hardest case [ISHAMAEL configuration]: when the exit
    cannot be independently verified because the exit is
    cosmological / irreversible / pre-event. The only counter
    is: who benefits from the exit? [COST IDENTIFICATION AND
    MINIMIZATION] -- does the prescriber benefit from you
    taking the exit regardless of whether it leads where
    claimed? If yes: strong prior that the exit is captured.
  Cross-ref: [CASE:ISHAMAEL] [primary fictional proof];
    JONES PATTERN [Section Six -- political scale parallel];
    MYTHIC FORMATION CAPTURE [Section One / Three --
    cultural scale parallel]; CALIBRATION STRIP [weapon --
    genuine critique as strip-setting mechanism]; FORMATION
    CAPTURE [Section One / Three -- argument-level variant];
    T9 [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- holding correct
    diagnosis and wrong conclusion simultaneously is the T9
    structure of this capture]; COST IDENTIFICATION AND
    MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS -- who benefits from
    the exit is the counter]; CONVERGENCE WITH WESTERN
    ESOTERIC TRADITION [Section Eight -- Gnostic parallel;
    the diagnosis Ishamael used is a real occult finding].

INFRASTRUCTURE CANNIBALISM [named failure mode -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  An organization consumes its generative tools to maintain
  current substrate, reducing future capacity to solve the
  same problem the tools were solving.
  MECHANISM: immediate substrate costs [food, housing, care]
    are non-deferrable. Capital maintenance costs [vehicles,
    equipment, facilities] are deferrable. Under funding
    pressure, the organization defers capital maintenance
    first [correct immediate decision] → capital degrades
    or is sold → future generative capacity reduced → same
    problem recurs with less tool capacity than before.
    The correction for the immediate problem destroys the
    capacity to correct the same problem next cycle.
  PRIMARY CASE [Charlotte-area animal rescue, 2024]:
    Rescue organization sold its transport van to fund
    current animal care costs. The van was the mechanism
    for pulling animals from high-kill shelters and moving
    them through the regional rescue network to placement.
    Selling the van: correct decision given immediate animal
    substrate cost [non-deferrable]; wrong cost to cut given
    generative function [van saves more animals per dollar
    than equivalent direct care spending -- force multiplier
    on rescue capacity]. Result: future pulls constrained;
    future animals not rescued cannot be counted; the
    cost of not having the van is invisible while the cost
    of feeding current animals is visible.
  DISTINCT FROM:
    FERTILE LOCK [transmission blocked]: infrastructure
      cannibalism is active consumption of the tool, not
      structural blockage of transmission.
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [general depletion]: cannibalism
      specifically targets the generative tool, not just
      substrate. The specific loss is the multiplier.
    WORTHLESS DOING [output without generative function]:
      the tool being consumed HAS genuine generative
      function; the problem is the consumption, not the
      absence of function.
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [anti-coherence wins
      continuously; generation only needs to win once]:
      cannibalism closes the "only needs to win once" path.
      The single loss event IS the generative tool.
      Anti-coherence doesn't need to win again; the tool
      that would have enabled the generative win is gone.
  FUNDING ARCHITECTURE CAUSE [structural, not moral]:
    The funding surface [donation behavior] systematically
    rewards visible acute need [animal in crisis] over
    invisible generative capacity [transport van maintenance].
    Calibration Strip on donor behavior: emotional salience
    of individual animals is the strip; infrastructure
    investment is invisible to the strip. The organization
    optimizing for the strip produces correct individual
    decisions [feed the animal] while the cumulative
    output is infrastructure depletion.
    This is COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION failure at
    funding architecture level: costs are correctly
    identified [animals need feeding; van needs maintenance];
    minimization is wrong [the van saves more animals per
    dollar than direct feeding; the correct cost to minimize
    is direct feeding relative to transport capacity]. But
    the funding surface makes the wrong cost visible and
    the correct cost invisible. The organization cannot
    minimize the correct cost because the donor cannot see it.
  FORCE MULTIPLIER PRINCIPLE [why infrastructure costs first
  is wrong]:
    The transport van enables pulling 10 animals from a
    high-kill shelter that would otherwise be euthanized.
    The van costs less per animal saved than the equivalent
    direct care. The infrastructure is the higher-value
    expenditure per unit of mission output. Cutting
    infrastructure first to protect direct care costs
    is cutting the higher-value expenditure to protect
    the lower-value one. Correct immediate decision;
    wrong strategic decision. The funding architecture
    inverts the value ordering because it makes direct
    care visible and infrastructure invisible.
  SCALE PATTERN [applies beyond animal rescue]:
    Any mission-driven organization with non-deferrable
    immediate substrate costs and capital-intensive
    infrastructure:
      Community health clinics [vehicles for mobile care
        sold to fund medications].
      Food banks [refrigeration units sold to fund
        immediate food purchases].
      Volunteer fire departments [apparatus deferred to
        fund training and equipment].
      Schools in budget crises [building maintenance
        deferred to fund salaries].
    In all cases: the infrastructure is the generative
    multiplier; the immediate costs are the visible need;
    the funding surface rewards visible need; the
    multiplier degrades.
  CORRECTION PATHWAY:
    Infrastructure-specific funding [donor understands
      the force multiplier; funds the tool not the animal].
    Operational reserve [buffer between acute costs and
      capital maintenance].
    Volunteer transport network [reduces van dependency
      but increases coordination cost and reliability risk].
    Regional infrastructure sharing [multiple rescues
      sharing one transport vehicle reduces per-org
      capital cost].
    None of these are available by default under the
    current funding surface. The surface must change or
    the cannibalism continues.
  HONEST LIMIT:
    Selling the van may be correct even with full
    information if: alternative transport is available
    and reliable; the immediate animal costs are truly
    acute [animals will die without the funding];
    the organization has a credible path to replacement.
    Infrastructure cannibalism is a failure mode, not a
    universal condemnation of the decision to sell. The
    failure is in the funding architecture that makes
    this decision necessary repeatedly, not in any
    individual decision.
  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [above --
    cannibalism closes the generative win path]; COST
    IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS
    -- the operative principle; minimization is being
    applied to the wrong cost]; CALIBRATION STRIP
    [donor funding surface as strip -- emotional salience
    of animals vs invisible infrastructure]; EXTRACTION
    CYCLE [civilizational -- same depletion pattern at
    organizational scale]; GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK
    [rescue runs gift architecture; infrastructure
    cannibalism degrades SP -- the source IS being
    depleted]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [the van is a CC
    tool for the rescue ecosystem; cannibalism reduces
    CC at regional scale]; WORTHLESS DOING [contrast --
    infrastructure cannibalism is NOT worthless doing;
    the loop was real; the tool is being consumed].

SMART VS STUPID EXTRACTION [revised -- extraction always wrong]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Previous framing: smart extraction [below innovation
  threshold] vs stupid extraction [past threshold]. Revised:
  both are wrong. Smart extraction is slower destruction --
  the threshold is still crossed, just later. Smart death is
  still death.
  The distinction that matters: predation and competition
  [honest boundary, visible conflict] vs extraction [captured
  measurement surface, hidden mechanism]. Speed of destruction
  is irrelevant to the structural judgment.
  Explanatory residue retained: some systems generated genuine
  surplus despite extraction overhead [Song Dynasty, Bell
  Labs] because extraction floor existed below which
  suppression became unprofitable. This explains the
  mechanism without endorsing it.
  See: EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION
  [updated].

EXTRACTION WITH LIMITS VS CENTRALIZED EXTRACTION [revised]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Both are extraction -- both wrong. Distinction is speed and
  legibility, not moral category. Centralized is faster
  destruction; distributed with limits is slower destruction.
  Useful structural distinction retained: centralized
  extraction fails faster [condition 2 absent, no cross-
  correction]. Distributed with limits sustains longer but
  endpoint is the same.
  Confirmation: mit'a -> encomienda = slow destruction to
  fast destruction. Not slow destruction as acceptable
  alternative.
  Framework position: predation and competition are the honest
  alternatives. Extraction in any form is captured
  measurement.

TOPOLOGY FINDINGS + DISTRIBUTED CAPTURE NETWORK [merged]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every sustained evil: consumption-dominant terminal node
  fed by capture chains through intermediate nodes that
  believe they operate normally. Highest surplus under
  external pressure; lowest during dominance. Transition =
  highest-value measurement point.
  Distributed Capture Network type: nodes believe independent,
  terminal extraction point invisible from inside, correction
  and extraction share identical infrastructure. Mont Pelerin
  [complexity as illegibility], WEF [complexity as
  legitimacy]. Same Fertile Lock trajectory, different optics.

ANTI-TOWER [confirmed structural inverse]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Anti-coherence has predictable structure operating as tower
  inverse. Noise -> Corruption -> Destruction sequence; extraction mechanism
  stack [MSC + ECD + Calibration Strip + Formation Capture];
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY suppression as primary mechanism. Not
  chaos -- inverted tower with consistent function.
  Unfalsifiability as confirmation: if anti-tower were absent,
  counter-attempts would succeed. The fact that every counter-
  attempt routes through the same mechanisms -- noise
  suppresses signal, corruption captures the counter-movement,
  destruction targets what survives -- is the signature of a
  functioning structure. The inability to disprove the anti-
  tower is proof of the anti-tower.
  T8 partial: recursive closure is the anti-tower working as
  designed, not a measurement failure. Does not void the
  finding.

CAPTURE MECHANISMS [cross-ref to political -- confirmed at
  multiple scales]:
  Research MI capture, suppressed variable, sub-national RI
  capture.
  See: CAPTURE MECHANISMS [Section Five -- Political].

POWER STABILITY [cross-ref to political -- confirmed
  structural]:
  Extraction architecture stable across ideological
  transitions. Branding changes; mechanism stable. 10,000
  years consistent output.
  See: POWER ARCHITECTURE [Section Five -- Political].

DANGEROUS QUALITY INTERACTIONS [v13 -- confirmed finding]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Five named configurations where quality combination produces
  predictable dangerous failure. Not random -- structural.
  Each is a specific instrument configuration with known output.

  [1] HIGH GA + LOW CC IN HOSTILE ENVIRONMENT [SOCRATES PROBLEM]:
    Examined axis visible without correction protection = target.
    Genuine axis produces outputs that contradict extraction
    architecture. CC absent or captured = no self-defense.
    The examined axis is the attacker's diagnostic instrument --
    it identifies who is safe to attack and who is dangerous.
    High GA makes the instrument maximally legible; low CC makes
    it maximally vulnerable. Output: destruction or suppression.
    Primary case: Socrates [hemlock]. WISDOM CONCEALMENT [below
    in Section One] is the named counter-strategy.

  [2] HIGH CC + LOW CL [CORRECTION FIRES BUT CANNOT ANCHOR]:
    CC fires against detected deviation; causal chain illegible;
    correction cannot locate source. Produces visible correction
    activity without resolving deviation. High energy expenditure;
    zero structural effect. Instrument appears functional --
    CC fires, PR is proportional -- but the target is a symptom.
    Correction Displacement [CORRECTION TARGETING failure] is
    the mechanism. ECD [Engineered Causal Diffusion] is the
    weapon that produces this configuration deliberately.

  [3] HIGH TS + LOW SA [FERTILE LOCK TRAJECTORY]:
    Transmission without correctable methodology. Successors
    receive destination [the conclusion, the doctrine, the
    practice] without the method of examining and revising it.
    High fidelity transmission of locked state. Each generation
    receives more completely what it cannot revise.
    Distinct from no transmission [which at least leaves
    successors to discover fresh]: TS without SA transmits
    the lock itself. The most dangerous transmission failure
    because it appears as strong institutional continuity.

  [4] HIGH PR + LOW GA [SURPLUS WITHOUT DIRECTION]:
    Proportional Response fires correctly; axis not genuine or
    absent; surplus distributed to wrong population. Generous
    without accuracy. Distribution looks like gift; routing
    serves extraction architecture. Sincere but misdirected.
    Extraction looks like gift when axis is captured and PR
    is functioning -- the instrument genuinely intends gift;
    the routing is captured. Most dangerous sincere
    configuration: no deception required; instrument self-
    deceives through installed axis.

  [5] HIGH INPUT FILTERING + LOW CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION
    [FRUSTRATED DETECTION CONFIGURATION]:
    Population detects extraction accurately; CC monopolized;
    cannot fire. Mass frustration without correction. Self-
    reinforcing: sustained frustration without correction
    degrades INPUT FILTERING [detection instrument depletes];
    while CC centralizes further. Ends in Diagnostic Collapse
    or substrate exhaustion. The Glasnost pattern is the
    stress-event version -- when the lid comes off, the
    accumulated frustration fires against everything
    simultaneously.

  Cross-ref: CORRECTION TARGETING [#2 mechanism]; FERTILE
    LOCK [#3 endpoint]; SUICIDAL EMPATHY [#4 specific
    configuration when empathy is installed axis]; FRUSTRATED
    DETECTION CONFIGURATION [#5 named -- Section One L2];
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [#5 endpoint]; WISDOM CONCEALMENT
    [#1 counter-strategy].

BI/MI CO-FAILURE PATTERN [confirmed structural finding -- v11]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Boundary manipulation [BI] and measurement inversion [MI] consistently
  co-occur in False Coherence cases with dual-boundary structure. BI
  failure shifts who counts. MI failure inverts how harm to the
  excluded population registers. Combined: harm produced, excluded
  from boundary, measured as non-harm.
  Three configurations:
    SEQUENTIAL [BI failure causes MI failure]: boundary manipulation
      first; measurement surface then captures the new boundary as
      legitimate. Primary case: Marley [AoT].
    CO-DEPLOYED [weapon package]: BI and MI failures deployed
      simultaneously as deliberate stack. Primary case: Enron.
    STRUCTURALLY IDENTICAL [boundary IS the surface]: the
      boundary definition and measurement surface are the
      same object. Most dangerous -- no entry point for
      partial correction. Correcting BI automatically
      corrects MI and vice versa; but attacking either
      faces the other's defense simultaneously.
      Primary cases: Sibyl System [Psycho-Pass]; WATF towers.
  Diagnostic: when BI and MI failures co-occur, treat as stack
    not coincidence. Correct BI first [shift who is counted];
    MI restoration follows. Reversing the order leaves
    measurement surface defending the wrong boundary.
  Cross-ref: BOUNDARY INTEGRITY; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY;
    WEAPON STACK DETECTION [Section Three]; EXTRACTION
    [requires both to sustain at scale].

MIXED COHERENCE [formal designation -- v11; restored v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Sufficient CC and MI to detect and resist weapon deployment
  while accepting lower surplus ceiling and constant extraction
  pressure. Not a compromise -- the only stable configuration
  under current conditions.
  True Coherence remains the measurement ceiling and direction
  of orientation. Mixed Coherence is the load-bearing practical
  category for any real-world system operating inside an
  extraction architecture.
  CONDITIONS FOR MIXED COHERENCE [minimum]:
    CC functional enough to detect weapon deployment.
    MI intact enough to read own outputs honestly.
    Exit options present [even if costly].
    Axis genuine [even if drifting].
  NOT MIXED COHERENCE [failure states]:
    CC present but aimed at proxy [Correction Theatre].
    MI captured [reads extraction as health].
    Exit costs total [ECC complete].
    Axis captured [installed content running as identity].
  POPULATION CEILING IMPLICATION:
    True Coherence structurally incompatible with scale and
    survival simultaneously under current extraction
    architecture. Anti-coherent systems outcompete by
    accumulating faster. No exception confirmed in case
    library. Mixed Coherence is the operational ceiling,
    not a stepping stone. Environmental change required
    for True Coherence viability at scale -- circular
    dependency unresolvable without external intervention.
  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [why True
    Coherence cannot sustain at scale under current
    conditions]; POPULATION AXIS DISTRIBUTION [most
    instruments operating at Mixed or below]; SILENT
    ALTERNATIVE [Mixed Coherence is the operating state
    of a Silent Alternative during its silent window];
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [what Mixed Coherence is resisting].

GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK [v13 -- confirmed finding]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Required for gift architecture to function. All five required.
  Missing any one: gift degrades or fails to transmit.

  [1] SP [SURPLUS PRODUCTION]: something to give. If the
    source is not generating above its own cycle cost,
    distribution depletes rather than gifts.
  [2] TS [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]: correctable method
    transmitted, not locked conclusion. Gift of method
    allows recipient to generate independently. Gift of
    conclusion creates dependency.
  [3] SA [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]: recipient can revise what
    was received. Without SA, even correct method
    transmission locks the recipient into the transmitter's
    axis. Gift becomes Fertile Lock.
  [4] SD [SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION]: surplus actually exits
    the source boundary. SP without SD = accumulation.
    The surplus exists; the gift doesn't until it crosses
    the boundary.
  [5] OUTPUT INTEGRATION [lands usefully]: recipient
    system can absorb and use the distributed surplus.
    Gift that cannot be received is not a gift. SD without
    OUTPUT INTEGRATION = Distribution Waste.

  WEAPON MINIMUM [inverse -- three to break]:
    Capture MI [measurement surface -- recipient cannot
      verify what they received].
    Break CL [causal legibility -- gift-to-harm chain
      illegible; recipient cannot trace damage].
    Manipulate BI [boundary integrity -- shift who counts
      as recipient; gift routed to wrong population].
    Three qualities broken vs five required. Extraction
    is structurally easier than gift.

  ASYMMETRY [load-bearing finding]:
    Extraction breaks three; gift requires five working
    together simultaneously. Extraction structurally
    easier to deploy. Also structurally terminal --
    extraction destroys the substrate gift architectures
    run on. Gift architectures outlast extraction by
    orders of magnitude when they survive the initial
    competition window.
  Cross-ref: OUTPUT INTEGRATION [condition 5 -- Section
    One L1]; SD [SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION -- Section One
    Foundation]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed
    -- why extraction loses long; same asymmetry from
    different angle]; GIFT INDEX [Section Six -- confirmed
    gift cases]; SECONDARY PROOFS [below -- gift case
    library additions].

PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION [fundamental distinction]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Predation: honest violence. Boundary visible. The prey
  knows the relationship. A lion kills. Does not build a
  farm, keep prey mostly alive, and call it efficient
  resource management.
  Competition: honest conflict. Boundary visible. Both
  parties know the terms. Zero-sum possible but declared.
  Extraction: trickery as foundation. Requires prey to
  misunderstand the relationship, cooperate with its own
  consumption, and call it beneficial. Measurement surface
  capture is the mechanism.
  Slightly wrong is still wrong -- smart extraction is slower
  destruction. The innovation threshold is still crossed,
  just later. Smart death is still death. The crime is not
  the degree; it is the lie that makes cooperation possible.
  Framework: predation and competition are structurally
  honest. Extraction is not. Politics at scale = extraction
  [stealing] not predation -- requires the legitimacy surface
  that predation doesn't need.
  Note [v21]: distribution strategy and extraction mechanism
  are not opposites on a generation axis -- both require
  generation to function. They are opposites on a routing
  axis. The problem is never generation itself; it is where
  surplus goes and who controls the boundary determining
  the destination. See FRAMEWORK PURPOSE [GENERATION
  CLARIFICATION].

RELATIONAL STATES [four-state typology -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Extension of PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION to include
  COOPERATION as a named peer state and to formalize all four
  as states rather than fixed identities. States are relational
  and dynamic -- the same actors can move between states across
  time, context, or scale. Applies at any level: individual,
  population, species, cellular, bacterial, civilizational.

  FOUR STATES:

  COOPERATION:
    Mutual surplus production. Both parties generate more
    inside the relationship than outside it. GC completes
    in both; SP positive in both; CC functional in both.
    Exit options available and unused because relationship
    is net positive for both parties.
    Tell: remove one party and the other's generative
      capacity degrades. Both degrade on exit.
    Biological confirmation: mutualism [clownfish/anemone,
      mycorrhizal fungi/plant roots, gut microbiome/host].
      Both parties structurally dependent on surplus the
      other produces. Exit = degraded function for both.
    Not equivalent to harmony or absence of tension --
      cooperative relationships contain friction [T1.4
      calibrating friction]. Cooperation is defined by
      net surplus direction, not by comfort.
    Failure mode: cooperation read as extraction when
      asymmetric surplus is visible [one party generates
      more than the other]. Asymmetric cooperation is
      still cooperation if both are net positive relative
      to exit state. Comparison is to exit baseline,
      not to each other.

  PREDATION:
    Honest asymmetric cost. One party terminates or
    substantially depletes the other. Boundary visible.
    Prey knows or can know the relationship. Concealment
    absent or non-load-bearing.
    Tell: boundary between predator and prey is legible
      to an external observer and not actively obscured
      by the predator. The prey's misunderstanding is
      not required for predation to function.
    Biological confirmation: standard predator-prey
      dynamics. Lion and gazelle. The gazelle is not
      deceived about the lion's intent -- it runs. The
      predation does not require the gazelle's cooperation.
    Distinguished from extraction: extraction collapses
      if concealment is removed. Predation continues
      regardless of prey's understanding. A lion does
      not need the gazelle to believe it is a friend.

  COMPETITION:
    Honest symmetric conflict. Both parties contest the
    same resource or position. Both parties know the
    terms. Outcome uncertain; neither party requires the
    other's misunderstanding to function.
    Tell: both parties can accurately describe the
      relationship from outside their own position.
      The contest is legible to both.
    Biological confirmation: intraspecies competition
      [territory, mating rights, resource access].
      Both parties compete from known positions using
      known mechanisms. Outcome determined by capacity,
      not deception.
    Distinguished from extraction: competition does not
      require one party to misread the relationship.
      Both parties are running the same contest with
      full information about what the contest is.
    Note: competition can produce extraction outcomes
      [winner captures all surplus] without being
      extraction in mechanism. The mechanism is honest;
      the routing outcome may still be extractive.
      Mechanism and outcome are separate readings.

  EXTRACTION:
    Trickery as foundation. Requires the target to
    misunderstand the relationship. Concealment is
    load-bearing -- remove it and extraction collapses
    or requires conversion to predation.
    Tell: target's cooperation is required and depends
      on target not accurately reading the relationship.
      If target understood, it would exit or resist.
    Biological confirmation: parasitism. Parasite
      requires host to not recognize it as threat.
      Immune system = CC firing against extraction.
      Parasite's survival depends on evading detection
      [concealment as load-bearing mechanism].
      Also: brood parasitism [cuckoo/reed warbler] --
      host raises parasite's offspring because it
      cannot distinguish them from its own. Deception
      is the mechanism; remove it and the host ejects.
    Distinguished from predation: predation does not
      require host cooperation or misunderstanding.
      Extraction does. This is the load-bearing
      distinction across all scales.

  STATE DYNAMICS [v22]:
    States are not fixed identities -- they are relational
    readings at declared window and population. Same actors,
    different states across contexts:
      EMPLOYER / EMPLOYEE: cooperative at task level
        [both generate more together than apart] while
        simultaneously extractive at surplus routing level
        [employee does not receive surplus proportional
        to contribution; concealment of routing boundary
        is load-bearing]. Multi-state reading required.
      IMMUNE SYSTEM / PATHOGEN: extraction [pathogen
        requires host misidentification] transitioning
        to predation [immune response] when detection
        fires. State transition triggered by CC.
      MARKET COMPETITION: competition in mechanism
        [both parties know the contest] while producing
        extraction outcomes [monopoly, regulatory
        capture] at aggregate scale. Mechanism and
        outcome states can diverge.
    TRANSITION TRIGGERS:
      Cooperation → extraction: concealment installed
        after relationship established [late-stage
        platform capture; employer increasing routing
        capture after dependency construction].
      Extraction → predation: concealment removed or
        failed; predator no longer needs target's
        cooperation [extraction architecture sufficiently
        entrenched to operate openly].
      Predation → competition: power differential
        closes; prey develops capacity to contest.
      Competition → cooperation: shared threat or
        surplus opportunity exceeds contest value;
        both parties generate more by combining.

  SCALE APPLICABILITY [confirmed across levels]:
    Bacterial: quorum sensing = cooperation [collective
      surplus production]; antibiotic resistance sharing
      = cooperation; phage infection = predation;
      competitive exclusion = competition; pathogenic
      mimicry of host signals = extraction.
    Cellular: immune cooperation = cooperation; cancer
      = extraction [tumor requires host to not recognize
      it as threat; evades immune detection; concealment
      load-bearing]; apoptosis = predation [host
      terminates own cells openly].
    Animal: mutualism = cooperation; predation = predation;
      intraspecies contest = competition; parasitism =
      extraction.
    Human individual: friendship = cooperation; assault
      = predation; fair contest = competition; fraud =
      extraction.
    Institutional: generative partnership = cooperation;
      war [declared] = predation; market competition =
      competition; regulatory capture = extraction.
    Civilizational: alliances = cooperation; conquest
      = predation; geopolitical competition = competition;
      technofeudalism / cloud capital = extraction.

  HONEST LIMIT:
    Multi-state readings are common. Most complex
    relationships operate in two or more states
    simultaneously at different scales or subsystems.
    Single-state readings are diagnostic simplifications
    useful for intervention targeting, not complete
    descriptions of the relationship. Always declare
    which scale and subsystem the state reading applies to.

  Cross-ref: PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION
    [foundational definitions above -- states extend
    this block]; EXTRACTION [operational definition --
    trickery as foundation; preserved unchanged];
    GENERATIVE CYCLE [cooperation requires GC in both
    parties]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [immune system as
    CC firing against extraction; state transition
    trigger]; L3.5 [RECIPROCITY -- cooperation at
    relational level]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY
    [extraction must maintain concealment continuously;
    cooperation only has to establish surplus once];
    COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 -- scale gap can
    produce predation-reading when cooperation or
    necessity is the actual mechanism].

ASYMMETRY TYPES [predatory / developmental / compatibility --
  all three confirmed; predatory existing framework; developmental
  promoted v21; compatibility promoted v22]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surface reading identical across types: information withheld;
  receiver does not understand the full situation. Mechanism,
  intervention path, and ethical status differ. Collapse into
  single category produces wrong intervention.

  PREDATORY ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- existing framework]:
    Information withheld serves operator at receiver's expense.
    Operator benefits from ignorance persisting.
    Tell: closing the asymmetry would damage operator's position.
    Intervention: MSC counter [independent measurement].

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- promoted v21]:
    Information withheld serves receiver's eventual capacity.
    Asymmetry designed to close over time as instrument develops.
    Operator benefits from the asymmetry closing -- a developed
    receiver is the intended output, not a permanently dependent one.
    Tell: operator actively works toward asymmetry closing; full
    information is the goal, not the threat.
    Requires GENUINE AXIS in operator [load-bearing -- confirmed
    integration check v21]: developmental framing without GA in
    operator is predatory asymmetry with developmental cover.
    Behavioral test catches it at condition 4.
    Failure mode: developmental asymmetry used as cover for
    predatory. Tell: asymmetry never closes; operator benefits from
    continued dependency. Revert to predatory reading if any
    condition fails regardless of stated intent.

    FOUR-CONDITION MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [all required]:
      [1] Asymmetry maintained on receiver's developmental
          schedule, not operator's convenience.
      [2] Asymmetry closes when receiver reaches capacity.
      [3] Closure serves receiver's autonomous function.
      [4] Operator has no structural interest in asymmetry
          persisting beyond receiver's readiness.
      All four required. Any one absent: revert to predatory
      reading. Behavioral test, not intent test.

    Extraction definition sharpened [not amended]: operator benefit
    from ignorance persisting is the load-bearing extraction
    criterion. Developmental asymmetry explicitly fails that
    criterion -- operator benefits from ignorance closing. The
    distinction is clean.
    Primary case: DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY [S:3 -- v20].
    Cross-ref: EXTRACTION [operational definition]; MSC [not
    triggered by developmental withholding -- mechanism inverted];
    GENUINE AXIS [required in operator]; [REF:KYUBEY] [compatibility
    asymmetry mixed case -- developmental confirmation does not
    resolve]; WIP 16 [STUB -- all three types now confirmed; see
    CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY for compatibility primary case].

  COMPATIBILITY ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- promoted v22;
    real-world primary case CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY S:3]:
    Asymmetry produced by structural incompatibility between operational
    frames. Not withheld by choice; not closeable by time or volume.
    Tell: honest transmission produces same response as deception --
    instrument registers violation regardless of operator intent.
    Mechanism: frame structure mismatch, not information deficit.
    Critical discriminator: operator has no structural interest in
    incomprehension persisting [contrast: predatory asymmetry].
    Primary fictional candidate: Yaldabaoth [Gnostic -- S:2].
    Kyubey: mixed case [compatibility + predatory co-deployed].
    Primary real-world case: CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY [Double
    Empathy Problem -- Milton 2012; bidirectional frame incompatibility
    confirmed; five-condition instrument passed v22; S:3].
    Full five-condition instrument and case detail: see WIP 16
    [stub] and CASE:AUTISM_DOUBLE_EMPATHY.
    Asymmetry produced by structural incompatibility between operational
    frames. Not withheld by choice; not closeable by time or volume.
    Tell: honest transmission produces same response as deception --
    instrument registers violation regardless of operator intent.
    Mechanism: frame structure mismatch, not information deficit.
    Critical discriminator: operator has no structural interest in
    incomprehension persisting [contrast: predatory asymmetry].
    Primary candidate: Yaldabaoth [Gnostic -- fictional S2].
    Kyubey: mixed case [compatibility + predatory co-deployed].
    See WIP 16 staging for full entry and five-condition instrument.

MEASUREMENT-ACTION UNITY [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Measurement that produces no action = observation captured
  by the observer. Action without measurement = force without
  calibration -- extraction's operating mode.
  Legitimate conflict [predation, competition]: measurement
  drives action, both visible to parties bearing the cost.
  Extraction: action separated from honest measurement.
  Requires the gap to function -- prey must not see the
  measurement surface.
  Test: is the measurement driving the action visible to the
  party bearing the cost? If no, extraction.

FRAMEWORK AS PRAXIS [v11 -- restored v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The framework is not a descriptive theory -- it is a praxis
  instrument. Its value is not that it correctly maps coherence;
  it is that it identifies where correction is possible and where
  it is not, orienting action toward leverage points rather than
  dead ends.
  Marx [11th Thesis on Feuerbach, 1845]: "Philosophers have
  hitherto only interpreted the world in various ways; the point
  is to change it." The framework collapses the distinction --
  getting the structure right IS the precondition for effective
  action. Cannot change what cannot be correctly described.
  INSTRUMENT NOT CRITERION [load-bearing]:
    Framework qualities are instruments not criteria. Once a
    quality becomes a criterion of reality rather than a
    revisable tool, the system stops asking "under what
    conditions does this pattern hold?" and starts asking
    "why does this case deviate?" -- exchanging correction
    for policing. This is MSC applied to the framework itself.
    Guard against by treating all qualities as falsifiable tools.
  GENERATION CLARIFICATION [v21]:
    Generation is a structural requirement for anything to function
    -- not the problem. Extraction mechanisms require generation
    as much as distribution strategies do. Both depend on generative
    capacity. The problem is never generation itself. The problem
    is routing: where does surplus go, who controls the direction,
    who set the boundary that determines the destination.
    Distribution strategy and extraction mechanism are not opposites
    on a generation axis -- they are opposites on a ROUTING axis.
    Implication: attacking generation to counter extraction destroys
    the substrate both depend on. Correct intervention targets
    routing and boundary control, not generation itself.
  AXIS CAPTURE WARNING [v21]:
    The three operative goals are structurally neutral as analytical
    tools. The same map [value identification, boundary control,
    resource management] can be run with extraction as the governing
    purpose instead of distribution. A sophisticated extractor
    arrives at identical outputs; they route them differently.
    This is the ACWL failure mode [MORAL LEGIBILITY]: instrument
    functional; axis captured. The distribution framing does not
    protect against axis capture. What orients the framework toward
    distribution rather than extraction is the GENUINE AXIS of the
    operator -- not the tool itself.
    Tell: who absorbs the cost of the map being made? If the
    people providing the data absorb the cost of the analysis,
    the framework is running in extraction orientation regardless
    of stated purpose.
  Practical implication: use the framework to find leverage,
    not to classify. Classification without correction pathway
    is description. The framework produces value only when it
    identifies what can be moved and in which direction.

  COST IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [v22 -- fifth operative
  principle]:
    Everything has a cost. The goal is not to eliminate cost --
    it is to identify it accurately, assign it honestly, minimize
    it relative to the output it produces, and keep examining it.

    IDENTIFY: who pays the cost of each generative output?
      Named specifically, not abstracted. "The mission requires
      difficult tradeoffs" is not identification. "These specific
      people pay this specific cost for this specific output" is.
      Unidentified cost = cost absorbed invisibly by the most
      vulnerable participants by default.

    ASSIGN HONESTLY: does the cost fall on people who agreed to
      bear it and can exit if they choose not to?
      If yes: legitimate tradeoff.
      If no: extraction regardless of stated purpose. The
      "needed actions" framing does not change the assignment.
      Honest assignment requires exit capacity in the cost-bearing
      population. No exit capacity = cost cannot be honestly
      assigned regardless of mission quality.

    MINIMIZE: is this the lowest-cost path to the output?
      If higher-cost paths are chosen without examined reason:
      extraction embedded in operational design.
      Minimization requires actively searching for lower-cost
      alternatives, not assuming current cost is necessary.
      "We've always done it this way" = minimization stopped.

    EXAMINE CONTINUOUSLY: does the institution regularly revisit
      whether current cost is still the minimum? If the
      examination stops: Fertile Lock on the cost structure.
      The cost that was necessary at founding may not be necessary
      now. The examination is the CC mechanism for the cost itself.

    WHAT THIS RESOLVES [framework gaps]:
      SILENT ALTERNATIVE TENSION: "the mission requires extraction"
        was previously a tension between coherence goal and
        extraction tool. Sharper read: every mission has cost.
        The question is identification, assignment, minimization,
        examination. D-class isn't wrong because it has cost.
        It's wrong when cost isn't identified [institution stops
        examining], isn't assigned honestly [D-class have no
        representation in cost decision], isn't minimized [use
        extends beyond operational necessity].
      GIFT ARCHITECTURE: gifts have cost too -- SP requires
        substrate from the source. Gifts aren't free; they're
        cost-appropriate [source's cost absorbed by existing
        surplus; target not depleted; exit preserved]. Extraction
        is cost-inappropriate -- target absorbs cost they didn't
        agree to and can't exit from.
      POLITICAL INTERVENTION: attacking generation to counter
        extraction [GENERATION CLARIFICATION] destroys substrate
        both depend on because it misidentifies where the cost
        sits. Correct identification of cost location is the
        precondition for minimizing it.
      LEGITIMATE TRADEOFF VS EXTRACTION [the test]:
        Legitimate: cost identified [named], assigned honestly
          [bearers agreed and can exit], minimized [lowest-cost
          path], examined continuously [institution keeps asking
          whether cost is still necessary].
        Extraction: cost obscured [abstracted or unnamed], 
          misassigned [bearers didn't agree; can't exit],
          not minimized [higher-cost paths chosen without reason],
          not examined [institution stopped asking].
        The same action can be legitimate or extraction depending
          on which of these four conditions hold. Stated purpose
          does not determine which. The cost structure does.

  Cross-ref: OUROBOROS [AS ANTI-CAPTURE MECHANISM]; CORRECTION
    CAPACITY [CC without correction pathway = Correction Theatre];
    MORAL LEGIBILITY [ACWL -- instrument functional, axis captured];
    GENUINE AXIS [what orients the tool toward distribution];
    MASS ADOPTION HONEST LIMIT [framework as Silent Alternative];
    [CASE:SCP_FOUNDATION] [D-class as primary case -- needed
      actions vs genre convention; real Silent Alternative
      application]; GIFT QUALITY MINIMUM STACK [gifts are
      cost-appropriate not cost-free]; GENERATION CLARIFICATION
      [cost misidentification as the mechanism of wrong
      intervention]; SILENT ALTERNATIVE [institutional type
      facing this tension at every scale].
MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [confirmed structural property]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Single-axis identity ["I am X"] is structurally fragile and
  maximally capturable. Multi-value axis identity [holding several
  values in productive tension] has CC built into its structure and
  resists capture because all values must be simultaneously captured
  to install a replacement axis.

  SINGLE-AXIS FAILURE MODES [three simultaneous]:
    [1] CC-immune to its own axis: X is the foundation, not a
      position to be corrected. The instrument cannot apply CC
      against X because X IS the standard. IDENTITY-ANCHORED
      MORAL FOUNDATION failure is automatic -- membership check
      replaces examination requirement.
    [2] Existential threat response to challenge: every challenge
      to X is a total threat because no other axis exists to
      stand on. Defensive response is total, disproportionate,
      and CC-blocking [cannot examine what threatens existence].
    [3] Maximally capturable: one capture surface. Install the
      correct content on X and the instrument is captured.
      Formation Capture via single-axis identity is the most
      efficient deployment in the weapons index.

  MULTI-VALUE AXIS IDENTITY [structural properties]:
    Values held in productive tension correct against each other.
    Each value is checked by the others -- the examination
    requirement is built into the identity structure itself.
    TENSION MAINTENANCE at identity level: the same mechanism
    that makes opposing musical qualities held simultaneously
    more durable than either pole alone [see ART COHERENCE TYPE
    -- TENSION MAINTENANCE] makes multi-value identity more
    durable than single-axis identity.
    Capturable only if all values are simultaneously captured
    -- much harder than single-axis capture because each value
    functions as a correction check on the others. Capturing
    the freedom axis doesn't capture the labor dignity axis.
    Capturing the in-group solidarity axis doesn't capture the
    structural honesty axis.

  PREDICTABILITY AS GENUINE AXIS CONFIRMATION:
    A multi-value axis produces consistent, legible behavioral
    outputs -- the instrument's state can be predicted from the
    values it holds. This predictability is not a vulnerability;
    it is the confirmation that the axis is real, examined, and
    grounded rather than performed or reactive. An instrument
    whose outputs cannot be predicted has either: no stable axis
    [reactive, captured], or an axis not yet sufficiently
    examined to produce consistent outputs. Predictability from
    a multi-value axis = GENUINE AXIS confirmed at behavioral
    output level.

  SURVIVING MORAL FRAMEWORKS [confirmation across 2,000+ years]:
    Frameworks that survive across generations hold multiple values
    in productive tension and derive behavioral requirements from
    outputs rather than from identity or authority:
    Stoicism [2,300+ years]: virtue AND acceptance AND action --
      tensions between values are the CC mechanism.
    Talmudic tradition [2,000+ years]: competing opinions
      preserved in permanent tension; minority positions kept
      specifically because the tension is load-bearing for CC.
    Buddhist ethics [2,500+ years]: compassion AND non-attachment
      AND right action -- each value corrects against the others.
    Confucian ethics [2,500+ years]: ritual AND reciprocity AND
      self-cultivation -- multi-axis compound surviving Mandate
      of Heaven political capture because the ethical axis is
      distinct from the political deployment axis.
    Common property across all survivors: derivation from
      behavioral outputs and consequences, not from identity
      membership or institutional authority. Calibration standard
      operates independent of who holds institutional power.
    Frameworks that didn't survive: those whose calibration
      standard was the authority of the institution rather than
      the output of the behavior. Single-axis identity structures
      [state religions fully captured, revolutionary ethics,
      utopian moral frameworks] failed when the axis was captured
      because no corrective value axis remained.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    Multi-value axis identity falsified by: documented case where
    an instrument holding multiple values in genuine tension was
    captured by attacking a single value without the others
    providing correction. Not yet confirmed -- the structure
    predicts this should be difficult.
    Single-axis fragility falsified by: documented case where
    single-axis identity survived sustained challenge to X over
    multi-generational window without either: [a] becoming
    multi-value through examined extension, or [b] collapsing
    into extraction architecture serving whoever controls X.

  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- multi-value
    axis produces confirmed GA at behavioral output level];
    TENSION MAINTENANCE [quality definition -- same mechanism
    at identity scale]; IDENTITY-ANCHORED MORAL FOUNDATION
    [Section Five -- single-axis failure mode]; Formation Capture
    [weapons index -- single-axis maximally capturable]; WIP 15
    [MORAL LEGIBILITY -- surviving moral frameworks as primary
    confirmation cases]; STRUCTURAL ISOLATION [Section Five --
    multi-value axis produces enemy-of-everyone-simultaneously
    as structural consequence, not personal failure].

IDLE CYCLE REQUIREMENT [promoted from science appendix]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork.
  Applies at all scales: individual [attention gap
  elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate].
  Test: does the system have protected idle cycles? If no,
  SR is being extracted.

DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [confirmed case type -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cross-ref: RECOVERY TRAJECTORIES [cycle model -- predictive
  detail].
  C2 restoration in a system where C1 is already terminal
  produces collapse rather than recovery. The collapse is the
  correct outcome -- accurate diagnosis of a terminal state
  the False Coherence surface had concealed.
  Mechanism: False Coherence surface masks terminal C1
  trajectory. C2 partial restoration makes accumulated
  deviations simultaneously legible. Correction burst fires.
  C1 cannot absorb correction load -- already terminal.
  Collapse follows.
  Distinct from:
    C1 blind recovery failure [C1 was recoverable --
      correction burst should produce stabilization;
      Diagnostic Collapse means C1 was never recoverable
      before C2 opened].
    Simple collapse [no C2 restoration -- C1 just fails].
    Coherence Mimicry [false C2 restoration -- new surface
      capture].
  Confirmed cases:
    GLASNOST [primary]: Soviet C1 terminal 15+ years before
      Glasnost. C2 partial opening made terminal state
      legible. Collapse = correct diagnosis. Not recovery
      failure.
    ARAB_SPRING_DIFFERENTIAL [Libya]: 42-year deliberate C1
      destruction. C2 opening via protest + NATO. Diagnostic
      Collapse confirmed by paired comparison with Tunisia
      [C1 recoverable, same C2 opening, recovery trajectory].
  Intervention implication: C1 substrate audit required
    before any C2 restoration intervention. Opening C2 on
    terminal C1 is irreversible -- correction burst fires
    regardless of whether C1 can absorb it. External actors
    [NATO, sanctions, information operations] enabling C2
    opening without C1 audit produce Diagnostic Collapse or
    False Coherence reformation.
  Status: CONFIRMED [two primary cases + controlled
  comparison]. S4.

C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT [provisional
  finding -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  C1 starved recovery does not occur through individual
  correction capacity alone. External relational substrate
  via L3.5 COMPATIBILITY contact is the structural
  precondition.
  Confirmed across five independent cases:
    Farnese [Berserk]: relational contact with Guts/Schierke.
    Zuko [Avatar]: Iroh as substrate source restored.
    Joshua Graham [Fallout NV]: New Canaan community contact.
    Wittgenstein [real]: Cambridge re-engagement 1929
      preceded Investigations revision. Isolation [1920-1926]
      produced zero revision despite C2 intact. Near-control
      condition.
    Le Guin [real]: feminist community engagement preceded
      documented gender self-corrections.
  No counterexample in library.
  Mechanism: C1 substrate too depleted for internal
  generation to restart. External contact provides substrate
  that internal generation cannot supply until above
  threshold. L3.5 COMPATIBILITY is the delivery mechanism --
  not willpower, insight, or accurate self-diagnosis. Contact
  is the substrate source.
  Status: PROVISIONAL. S3.

L3.5 CONTACT TYPE MUST MATCH INSTRUMENT FRICTION AXIS
  [v16 extension]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Contact type varies across all five recovery cases. What
  is consistent is not type but structural fit: the contact
  that fired recovery matched the target instrument's primary
  receivable friction axis. Mismatched contact provides L3.5
  presence without substrate transfer.

  CASE MAPPING:
    Farnese: primary axis = sensory/somatic. Contact type =
      physical danger + combat survival + bodily co-presence
      with Guts. Logical argument or emotional reassurance
      would not have reached her instrument. Somatic contact
      did. Evidence: recovery fires during physical ordeal
      episodes, not during conversations.

    Zuko: primary axis = social/relational. Contact type =
      Iroh's unconditional presence, patience, warmth without
      correction pressure. Iroh never argued Zuko into
      correction; he waited. Evidence: intellectual argument
      from other characters [Katara, Aang] produced no
      recovery. Iroh's relational contact did. Same
      information content, different axis.

    Joshua Graham: primary axis = logical/social hybrid.
      Contact type = structured community with coherent
      meaning framework [New Canaan + scripture + Burned Man
      narrative]. Not pure emotional support; the community
      provided both belonging AND interpretive structure.
      Both axes required. Evidence: isolation post-Legion
      destruction did not produce recovery; community contact
      at New Canaan did.

    Wittgenstein: primary axis = logical/systemic. Contact
      type = adversarial intellectual friction [Vienna Circle,
      Russell, Sraffa's specific gesture challenge]. Not
      emotional support -- he had social stability in Austria,
      produced nothing. Cambridge intellectual challenge
      fired correction. Evidence: the isolation period had
      social contact [village community] but zero
      logical/systemic friction. Cambridge provided logical
      friction; revision began. Key case: Sraffa's reportedly
      single adversarial gesture challenge [pointing to
      Neapolitan chin flick, asking "what is the logical form
      of that?"] may have been the specific trigger. Single
      contact event on correct axis.

    Le Guin: primary axis = logical/social hybrid [writer +
      intellectual]. Contact type = feminist community of
      practice -- relational belonging + intellectual
      challenge to prior work simultaneously. Both axes
      active; both required.

  STRUCTURAL FINDING [v16]:
    L3.5 contact type must match target instrument's primary
    receivable friction axis. Three axes confirmed relevant:
      Sensory/somatic: physical co-presence, shared bodily
        risk, survival-level stakes. Not reducible to
        emotional warmth.
      Social/relational: unconditional presence, consistent
        availability, warmth without agenda. Not intellectual
        challenge.
      Logical/systemic: adversarial intellectual contact from
        peer who takes work seriously enough to contest it.
        Not social support.
    Mismatch: contact on wrong axis provides L3.5 presence
      [counts toward contact requirement] but does not
      transfer substrate. Explains failed recovery attempts:
      therapeutic approaches aimed at wrong axis for
      instrument type.

  INTERVENTION IMPLICATION [updated]:
    Correct intervention is not just relational contact --
    it is contact on the axis the target instrument can
    receive. Prescribing social/emotional support for a
    logical/systemic instrument [Wittgenstein type] provides
    presence without substrate transfer. Prescribing
    intellectual challenge for a somatic instrument [Farnese
    type] same failure. Assessment required: identify
    target's primary receivable axis before prescribing
    contact type.

  FALSIFICATION CRITERIA [updated]:
    1. C1 starved recovery without L3.5 contact [original].
    2. C1 starved recovery via L3.5 contact on demonstrably
       mismatched axis -- would falsify axis-matching
       requirement.
    No such case in current library.

  STATUS: PROVISIONAL extended [five contact type mappings,
  axis-matching pattern consistent, no falsification].
  Promote axis-matching to confirmed when: independent case
  confirms recovery fired on matched axis AND failed on
  mismatched axis for same instrument within one case window.

SUBSTRATE REGENERATION requires idle cycles. Default mode
  network [consolidation, memory integration, meaning-making]
  fires only in attention gaps. Continuous input extracts the
  recovery window before it can fire. Exhaustion is SR deficit
  not overwork. Applies at all scales: individual [attention
  gap elimination], institutional [no recovery time between
  correction cycles], civilizational [extraction past the
  regeneration rate]. Test: does the system have protected
  idle cycles? If no, SR is being extracted.

SPEED AS ERROR SIGNAL [framework-derived principle]:
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The faster a solution is implemented the more likely it is
  wrong. Speed compresses the correction cycle -- insufficient
  time for MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY to verify, CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
  to trace, or CORRECTION CAPACITY to fire before the action
  lands. Best solutions are slow and careful: each step
  generates feedback that calibrates the next. Speed
  eliminates the feedback loop.
  Aggression and violence as reaction not initiation: if force
  is required it should fire as T1.3 [proportional response
  to detected deviation], not as primary axis expression.
  Initiated aggression = CORRECTION CAPACITY misfired before
  the deviation is confirmed. The asymmetry: reactive force
  has a measured target; initiated force creates the target
  retroactively. One is correction; one is extraction mechanism.

FAMILY COHERENCE TYPE [confirmed -- v14; restored v19]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Highest surplus density per boundary size of any named coherence
  type. The boundary is small [household/kinship unit] and the
  surplus density within it is structurally higher than any larger
  formation. This is not sentimental -- it is a structural property
  of small-boundary high-contact formations.
  Primary vulnerability: CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING / SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY.
  Identical delivery mechanism for distribution strategy and extraction mechanism: care, provision,
  instruction, and boundary-setting look identical whether they serve
  the child's actual development or the parent's interest in
  maintaining dependency. CAUSAL LEGIBILITY must be intact to
  distinguish them. When CL is absent [child cannot read the
  mechanism], the vulnerability is maximum.

  LOVE [genuine -- mostly automatic coherence]:
    When genuine, fires SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION, CORRECTION CAPACITY,
    SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY, and GENUINE AXIS without deliberate effort.
    Axis grounded in the other's actual flourishing, not in the
    relationship's surface [presence, compliance, performance of
    gratitude]. The automatic quality is the structural tell:
    genuine love doesn't require the loved person to maintain a
    specific state to continue receiving it.
    Direct extraction mechanism inversions [failure modes]:
      Dependency [EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION]: love that requires
        continuous presence; exit costs installed.
      Performance requirement [CALIBRATION STRIP]: love contingent
        on the receiver maintaining a specific surface.
      Intolerance of correction [TENSION DISSOLUTION]: love that
        cannot survive honest feedback; collapses to single pole.
    The failure modes are not love degrading -- they are the
    extraction architecture deploying the love channel as a
    delivery mechanism. The channel is real; what is carried
    on it may not be generative.

  DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [primary site -- connects to WIP 16]:
    Family is the primary real-world site for developmental
    asymmetry. Parents withhold information from children not
    because they benefit from the ignorance persisting but because
    the child's instrument cannot hold it yet. The asymmetry is
    designed to close as the child develops.
    Good developmental asymmetry [confirmed]: progressive
      disclosure on the child's developmental schedule.
      Mortality, sexuality, social complexity -- withheld at 2,
      introduced at 7, expanded at 15, fully open at adulthood.
      The parent benefits from the asymmetry closing, not
      persisting. Child's developed capacity is the goal.
    Contaminated developmental asymmetry [predatory dressed as
      developmental]: "you're not ready" used permanently. The
      adult child at 30 is still told they cannot handle the
      information. Tell: the asymmetry never closes regardless
      of the receiver's demonstrated capacity. The withholder
      benefits from continued dependency. Reverts to predatory
      asymmetry classification regardless of stated intent.
    Falsification test for developmental type [from WIP 16]:
      [1] asymmetry maintained; [2] closed when receiver ready;
      [3] closure served receiver; [4] withholder had no interest
      in asymmetry persisting. All four required.
    Family cases are the clearest pool for confirming or
      falsifying WIP 16's developmental asymmetry type because
      the mechanism, intent, and outcomes are observable across
      generational time.

  FORMATION CAPTURE VULNERABILITY [highest in library]:
    Family is the highest-risk Formation Capture site because:
    [1] axis formation occurs before the instrument can evaluate
      what axis is being installed; [2] the installer has maximum
      trust and contact; [3] exit options are structurally
      unavailable to children; [4] the installation is socially
      legitimate and legally protected.
    Captured family formation produces the most durable axis
    installations in the framework because the formation window
    is longest and the trust channel is highest.
    Counter: delayed axis formation [exposure to competing axis
      sources before lock-in]; SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY as explicit
      parental goal [axis the child will examine, not the axis
      the parent prefers].

  Cross-ref: SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY [quality definition];
    CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING [quality definition]; EXIT COST
    CONSTRUCTION [extraction mechanism -- love channel deployment]; Formation
    Capture [extraction mechanism -- highest vulnerability site]; WIP 16
    [ASYMMETRY TYPES -- family as primary developmental asymmetry
    case pool]; DEVELOPMENTAL ASYMMETRY [WIP 16 staging];
    SOCIAL APPROVAL AS PRIMARY CRITERION [Section Five --
    family is primary installation site for social compliance
    channel weight in the instrument].

ART COHERENCE TYPE [framework-derived]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  ENCOUNTER DEPENDENCY [load-bearing constraint]:
    Art has no coherence properties. The work is not generative,
    extractive, somatic, epistemic, or relational -- the encounter
    between a specific instrument and a specific work can be any
    of these things. This is not a limitation to note and move
    past; it is structural. ART type readings are instrument-
    encounter readings, not object readings. Every claim about
    what a piece of music "does" is shorthand for what it does
    in the encounter with a matched instrument. A different
    instrument produces a different encounter -- neither is wrong.
    A piece of music can be coherent in a way you dislike. A piece
    you love can be structurally extractive for you specifically.
    Sustained engagement [track count] confirms instrument match,
    not structural quality. Dislike confirms mismatch, not
    extraction.
    EPISTEMIC STATUS of all ART type readings: T3 applies by
    structural necessity, not just by analyst bias risk. The
    object of measurement is experiential and accessible only
    from inside the encounter. No external observer can verify
    whether substrate transfer occurred. All ART type readings
    carry [ENCOUNTER-DEPENDENT -- instrument-specific; not
    transferable to different instrument without fresh reading].
    This marker appears in all music case entries.

    INSTRUMENT CURATION PRECEDES STRUCTURAL READING:
    When an instrument sorts music into playlists or categories
    by vibe, that sorting is pre-theoretical -- it happens before
    any structural analysis and is uncontaminated by framework
    vocabulary. This makes vibe-sorted playlists more reliable
    as raw instrument data, not less. The instrument encountered
    the music and registered a response; the structural reading
    is a post-hoc map of what structural properties consistently
    produce that response in that instrument.
    Implication: playlist taxonomies [Aggressive, Pleasant, Best]
    are instrument-response data, not structural classifications.
    A structural reading of the playlist is legitimate only as
    an attempt to understand what properties the instrument
    consistently encounters in each vibe-space -- not as a
    claim that the instrument applied structural criteria.
    Corollary: when structurally incompatible works appear in
    the same vibe-category, that is evidence about the
    instrument's primary receivable axis, not a classification
    error. See: [REF:THE_ILYS] [Death Grips and The I.L.Y's
    in the same Aggressive vibe-space despite opposite somatic
    addresses -- the shared property is pre-theoretical vibe,
    not somatic type].

  WHAT THE FRAMEWORK CAN READ ABOUT ART:
    Structural properties of the work itself -- what axes the
    work has available, what channels it addresses, what CL
    or CC properties it exhibits independent of reception --
    are readable without the encounter dependency caveat.
    Gorillaz' fictional persona structure enables CC regardless
    of whether any specific listener responds to it. The
    Protomen's causal chain narrative is present in the work
    whether or not the listener tracks it. These are structural
    properties of the object, not encounter properties.
    Encounter-dependent: somatic response, substrate transfer,
    emotional geometry, weight, immersion. All claims about
    what the music "feels like" or "does to the body."
    Object-readable: CC record, axis structure, capture
    resistance, CL in lyrical content, TS in discography
    pattern, TENSION MAINTENANCE as compositional design.

  ART TYPE DEFINITION [revised]:
    Creative output with structural properties that make
    coherence-relevant encounters possible for matched
    instruments. Distinct from SCIENCE type [CC as primary
    output] and DISTRIBUTION STRATEGY type [SP distribution as primary output].
    Not all creative output has these structural properties.
    The distinction: does the work's structure address coherence-
    relevant channels [somatic, epistemic, relational, causal],
    or does it optimize for attention extraction regardless of
    channel? Structure-addresses-channel = ART type candidate.
    Optimizes-for-attention-without-channel = Coherence Mimicry
    at aesthetic scale.
    The encounter still has to occur and match. The structural
    property is necessary but not sufficient.
    Falsification condition: a work classified as ART type
    is falsified if its structure can be shown to optimize
    for attention extraction [ICD production, legitimacy
    surface maintenance, dependency construction] rather than
    channel address. The falsification is about the work's
    structure, not about any specific listener's response.

  AXIS PRESENCE AS PRIMARY READABLE PROPERTY [v21]:
    The most fundamental object-readable property of a work is
    whether it was produced from a genuine examined axis or not.
    This is distinct from quality, polish, complexity, or genre.
    Crude, strange, low-budget, deliberately ugly, technically
    simple -- none of these determine axis presence. A work
    produced from a genuine axis carries internal coherence that
    is readable structurally: the strangeness, crudeness, or
    wrongness is generated -- it traces back to a coherent
    generative source. A work produced from axis void has no
    such coherence: its properties are default-attractor outputs,
    parameter noise, or attention-optimization residue. The
    properties are present but nothing is generating them.

    The distinction is not taste. It is structural:
      Axis-present work [any production value]: structural
        properties are generated from an examined source.
        Internal consistency between the work's choices --
        even when those choices are dissonant, ugly, or
        difficult -- because all choices trace to the same
        generative point. The strangeness is intentional in
        the specific sense that it is produced by something.
      Axis-void work [any production value]: structural
        properties are residue of absent axis. Choices do
        not trace to a coherent generative source. Smoothness
        is default smoothness; strangeness is default
        strangeness or algorithmic noise. Nothing is
        producing the properties -- they are what you get
        when nothing is producing anything.

    SLOP DEFINED STRUCTURALLY: axis-void output regardless of
      surface finish. High production value slop exists [polished
      content optimizing for attention extraction with no
      generative axis]. Low production value genuine art exists
      [crude work carrying genuine axis contact]. The detection
      axis is axis presence, not quality assessment.

    Object-readable tells for axis presence:
      Internal consistency of choices across the work --
        including consistent strangeness, consistent ugliness,
        consistent wrongness that traces to the same source.
      Resistance to legitimacy surface optimization -- axis-
        present work makes choices that would be easier not
        to make; it doesn't smooth away the things that would
        reduce engagement if the axis required them.
      Structural specificity -- the work could not have been
        produced by a different axis; the choices are not
        interchangeable with choices from a different source.
    Object-readable tells for axis void:
      Default-pattern smoothness -- choices that minimize
        friction without generating anything.
      Interchangeable properties -- any part of the work
        could be swapped for a part from a different work
        with no loss of coherence, because there was no
        coherence to lose.
      Attention-optimization signature -- choices that
        consistently move toward engagement regardless of
        whether they serve a generative function.

    Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [quality definition -- same
      structural property at individual instrument scale];
      AI AXIS VOID [Section Five -- axis void at deployment
      scale; slop as the cultural output of axis-void
      production]; COHERENCE MIMICRY [attention optimization
      without channel address]; ICD [axis-void content
      delivering hedonic component without SP output].

  FOUR STRUCTURAL VARIETIES [properties of the work]:

  SOMATIC [addresses sensory/subcortical channel]:
    Work whose structural properties address the sensory
    friction axis before the deliberative layer. Subcortical
    rhythm processing, timbral geometry, spatial density,
    low-frequency pressure -- channels that fire before
    cognitive framing routes the input. A work can address
    this channel and produce no somatic response in a
    mismatched instrument; the structural address is still
    present.
    Object-readable tells: rhythm density above cognitive
    tracking threshold; timbral complexity without melodic
    resolution anchor; spatial density without narrative
    structure; absence of social compliance signal in
    delivery [no approval-seeking surface].
    Failure mode [structural]: intensity without geometry.
    High amplitude/density without distinct configurations
    = noise, not somatic address. Saturation without
    structure. Extractive deployment uses somatic channel
    to bypass legitimacy evaluation -- ICD via continuous
    somatic stimulation that builds dependency without SR.

    SOMATIC MUSIC [subtype]:
    --------------------------------------------------------------------------
      Music whose structural properties primarily address
      the subcortical channel rather than harmonic resolution,
      lyrical meaning, or social/emotional recognition.
      The structural address precedes and frames all other
      channels the work may also carry.

      INSTRUMENT PROFILE [high-somatic]:
        Instrument calibrated primarily to the subcortical
        channel. Standard commercial music [relatable lyric,
