        harmonic resolution, familiar structure] addresses
        social/emotional channel -- misses the primary
        receivable axis for this instrument. Not taste
        preference; instrument configuration. High somatic
        preference predicts: low encounter with music
        optimized for harmonic resolution or lyrical
        relatability; high encounter with music whose
        structure addresses subcortical channels regardless
        of genre. The same music produces noise-encounter
        in a different instrument -- neither reading wrong.

        VOCAL SIGNAL ROUTING [refined]:
          Most vocal performance is a social/emotional signal
          carrier -- human voice emoting at the listener,
          seeking approval, delivering relatable feeling.
          Routes through social compliance channel first.
          Low yield for high-somatic instrument -- not
          because emotion is absent but because the channel
          being addressed is not the primary receivable axis.
          Vocals that land for this instrument: voice
          deployed as somatic instrument rather than social
          signal -- approval-seeking architecture absent.
          Mechanisms that remove the social signal:
          [1] Synthesis [vocaloid -- voice was never human;
            social routing structurally unavailable].
          [2] Aggression/density [MC Ride -- phonemic content
            as texture; words processed as percussion before
            semantic channel fires].
          [3] Deliberate wrongness/absurdism [Patton/Bungle
            -- voice doing things that block standard social
            routing; too structurally strange to process as
            approval-seeking performance].
          The common property: approval-seeking architecture
          absent. The voice is not performing emotion at the
          listener. It is generating somatic geometry,
          texture, or disorientation. Vocal performance that
          IS approval-seeking routes through the wrong
          channel and lands as noise or flat regardless of
          emotional content or technical quality.

        GEOMETRIC LEGIBILITY WINDOW [three-axis model]:
          Primary criterion for engagement is not complexity
          level, emotional content, or genre. It is whether
          the somatic structure produces distinct recognizable
          geometric shape at the subcortical channel.

          BORING [failure mode 1]:
            Structural predictability without geometric
            payoff. Instrument maps the full structure within
            the first listening and nothing fires in the
            somatic channel that was not already predicted.
            Resolution arrives on schedule; dynamic arc
            identical to expectation; information content
            at somatic channel = zero. Not equivalent to
            simple or emotionally flat -- a simple track
            can be non-boring if its geometric signal is
            distinct and specific. Standard commercial pop
            is boring primarily because the somatic
            structure is fully predictable regardless of
            emotional content. Boring ≠ disliked; boring
            = nothing received.

          DOES TOO MUCH [failure mode 2]:
            Complexity without geometric resolution. Many
            simultaneous channels, none resolving into
            a distinct somatic shape. Deliberative layer
            engages trying to process it, fails to resolve,
            somatic channel cluttered rather than addressed.
            Not boring -- effortful and unrewarding.
            Distinct from assault type [which forces
            subcortical bypass]: does-too-much complexity
            is high enough to engage the deliberative layer
            without being high enough to bypass it. The
            instrument is stuck between channels.

          GEOMETRIC LEGIBILITY WINDOW [engagement zone]:
            Music that produces distinct somatic geometry
            regardless of complexity level. Two mechanisms
            confirmed for this instrument:
            [1] Subcortical bypass [assault type]: density
              or speed high enough that deliberative layer
              cannot track it; geometry lands somatically
              because no other route available. Works at
              maximum complexity. [Death Grips, Machine Girl]
            [2] Geometric specificity [minimal/clean type]:
              structure simple enough that the somatic shape
              is unambiguous and clean. Works at minimum
              complexity. [Namitape, future funk geometric]
            Both ends of the complexity spectrum can land;
            the middle often doesn't. The window is not
            about complexity level -- it is about whether
            the mechanism produces distinct geometry at
            whatever level it operates. A mid-complexity
            track lands if its mechanism produces clear
            geometry [Louis Cole precision tension, Mastodon
            structural accumulation]. It fails if complexity
            produces diffuse signal without geometric shape.
            Confirmed across wildly different genres and
            complexity levels: the common thread is not
            genre, tempo, or density. It is geometric
            legibility at the somatic channel.

        Cross-ref: sensory/somatic friction axis [L3.5
          CONTACT TYPE]; DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY [GA formation];
          VOCAL SIGNAL ROUTING above; ART COHERENCE TYPE
          [INSTRUMENT CURATION PRECEDES STRUCTURAL READING].

      SINGLE-INSTRUMENT DEVELOPMENT NOTE / CROSS-DOMAIN INSTRUMENT
      PROPERTY / PRE-THEORETICAL STRUCTURAL REGISTRATION:
        [CROSS-REF: SECTION ONE -- ANALYST NOTES / instrument
        profile; somatic taxonomy T3 declaration; gaming
        preferences; pre-theoretical registration cases.
        Full entries in ANALYST NOTES division at end of
        Section One.]

      VARIETIES [structural address subtypes]:
        Assault [Death Grips primary, Machine Girl secondary]:
          Maximum subcortical address via rhythm density and
          timbral aggression. Phonemic content structurally
          functions as texture -- words as somatic surface,
          semantic meaning as optional second layer.
          No resolution arc, no approval-seeking surface,
          no legitimacy frame. Object-readable tell: words
          processable as percussion without semantic loss.

        Weight [Mastodon primary]:
          Subcortical address via accumulated structural
          pressure. Riff architecture builds tension before
          resolution; TENSION MAINTENANCE as compositional
          design. The work's structure prevents early
          resolution on the listener's timeline.
          Object-readable tell: resolution withheld past
          the point where standard forms would deliver it.

        Texture [Iglooghost primary candidate]:
          Subcortical address via spatial density and
          timbral saturation without rhythmic assault.
          Immersive environment rather than directed
          impact. Object-readable tell: spatial complexity
          without rhythmic anchor; unfamiliar timbral
          territory without resolution guidance.

        Geometric [Future Funk cluster primary]:
          Subcortical address via recognizable emotional
          configurations -- distinct shapes not intensities.
          Sample-sourced [Future Funk] or memory-sourced
          [2 Mello] geometry. Object-readable tell: groove
          pattern produces immediate somatic shape without
          build phase.

      EXTRACTION RISK [structural]:
        Somatic channel address partially bypasses
        legitimacy evaluation. Structurally: a work can
        address the subcortical channel while carrying
        extraction payload -- dependency construction,
        ICD production, axis installation before
        deliberative evaluation fires. The channel address
        is real; what it carries may not be generative.
        Tell: somatic-addressed music that produces ICD
        [consumption required to maintain baseline] rather
        than SR. Structure optimized for re-engagement
        not for encounter completion.

      PRIMARY CASES [encounter-dependent; S:2]:
        DEATH_GRIPS, MASTODON_BAND [confirmed encounters,
        matched instrument]. All music case S-ratings
        carry encounter dependency caveat.

  STRUCTURAL [addresses CAUSAL LEGIBILITY channel]:
    Work whose structure makes a causal chain, mechanism,
    or system legible that was previously opaque. The
    legibility is in the work's architecture -- narrative
    structure, character design, compositional design --
    not dependent on any specific encounter to exist.
    Object-readable tells: explicit causal structure in
    narrative/lyrical form; defamiliarization that makes
    prior references structurally visible; character or
    world design that illustrates quality patterns.
    Failure mode [structural]: legibility performance.
    Structure appears to reveal mechanism without actually
    grounding the chain. Sincere Delusion at aesthetic
    scale -- the work feels revelatory without transmitting
    the actual chain.
    Falsification: if the claimed causal chain cannot be
    traced in the work's structure independent of encounter,
    the structural reading fails.

  EPISTEMIC [addresses instrument calibration channel]:
    Work whose structure requires active instrument
    extension to process -- cannot be received passively
    with existing frames. The extension requirement is
    in the work's design, not the encounter.
    Object-readable tells: structural properties that
    block standard routing [alien world-building, genre
    collision, radical defamiliarization]; formal demands
    that require new calibration to follow.
    Failure mode [structural]: calibration performance.
    The work appears to demand extension but actually
    routes through familiar frames with exotic surface.

  RELATIONAL [addresses L3.5 channel]:
    Work whose structure makes another coherence state
    recognizable across absence -- voice texture, character
    interiority, first-person address structured to produce
    RECOGNITION of specific states not general emotion.
    Object-readable tells: specificity of inner state
    represented [not general sadness -- this specific
    configuration]; structural design that routes
    recognition to internal state rather than to performer
    persona [avoids parasocial capture by design].
    Failure mode [structural]: parasocial architecture.
    Structure optimized to route RECOGNITION to performer
    rather than to the listener's own state. Dependency
    on performer presence rather than transfer completion.

  Cross-ref: SUBSTRATE REGENERATION; L3.5 CONTACT TYPE
    MUST MATCH [friction axis]; DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY;
    COHERENCE CORRUPTION [ART type operates outside
    captured channels when structure is primary carrier];
    GENUINE AXIS [axis must exist in the work's structure
    before ART type reading is valid; Coherence Mimicry
    at aesthetic scale = axis absent, attention extraction
    present].

INSTITUTIONAL AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT [provisional -- v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  The contact-type-must-match-friction-axis finding
  [individual scale, C1 starved recovery] extends to
  institutional scale. Institutional C1 starved recovery:
  correction operating on a different axis than the
  dysfunction does not transfer substrate. Confirmed across
  four institutional recovery cases:

  Cistercian [organizational axis]:
    Cluny dysfunction = structural centralization producing
    wealth. Cistercian correction = organizational redesign
    [general chapter + local autonomy]. Same structural/
    organizational axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 700-year
    record].

  Jesuit [contemplative/individual axis]:
    Catholic spiritual dysfunction = external rule-following
    without internal examination. Jesuit correction = Examen
    [internal examination procedure]. Same contemplative
    axis. Recovery: confirmed [L4, 480-year record].

  Vatican II [doctrinal/methodological axis]:
    Counter-Reformation dysfunction = methodological lock
    [Neo-scholasticism]. Vatican II correction = historical
    method [nouvelle théologie on same methodological axis].
    Recovery: confirmed [C1 locked recovery primary case].

  China 1978 [split-axis case -- confirms by differential]:
    Maoist dysfunction = ideological axis [class struggle
    doctrine]. Economic correction = productive axis
    [matching C1 substrate]. Recovery: CONFIRMED for
    economic C1. Political correction = different axis [C2
    surface, not matching Maoist ideological axis]. Recovery:
    FAILED [Tiananmen]. Same system, same three conditions,
    axis-match determines which component recovered. Split
    case is the strongest institutional confirmation.

  FINDING [provisional]:
    Institutional correction must operate on the same
    functional axis as the dysfunction to transfer substrate.
    Cross-axis correction produces activity without substrate
    transfer -- institutionally equivalent to wrong-axis
    L3.5 contact. China 1978 provides controlled comparison:
    same system, same enabling moment, matched-axis correction
    succeeded, mismatched-axis correction failed.

  Falsification: documented institutional recovery via
    correction operating on demonstrably different axis than
    dysfunction. No such case in current library.

COMEDY COHERENCE TYPE [v12]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  CC made safe through framing. Exposes what official measurement
  surface won't name -- honest MI operating where formal surface
  can't. Court jester function: correction capacity with social
  permission. Low Exit Cost Construction for naming extraction.
  Primary quality: CC [correction primary axis and output].
  Primary vulnerability: CORRECTION THEATRE [CC -- performance
    without function]. Comedy performing critique without producing
    it: safe targets, captured surface, punching down.
  HIGH-COHERENCE COMEDY: fires against actual power, traceable
    causal chain, audience leaves with corrected MI.
  LOW-COHERENCE COMEDY: reinforces existing axis, excludes
    harm-bearing population from boundary of the joke.
  JOKE POLICE [useful vs captured distinction]:
    Useful: punching down [comedy at expense of no-exit
      population = BI failure not CC]; context timing [PR
      -- proportional response].
    Captured: everything beyond those two. Joke policing
      typically = Correction Theatre suppressing CC that
      fires against the policing institution. Stated
      problem: harm. Actual function: MI capture of the
      comedy surface.
  COHERENCE SIGNALS [v12]:
    Jokes = CC made safe through framing. Requires honest MI --
      audience must recognize gap between stated and actual.
    "I don't know" = CC firing against own GA without external
      pressure. Highest S5 signal available. Axis secure enough
      to expose uncertainty without threat to self.
    Both require axis stability. Insecure axis cannot afford either.
  Cross-ref: CORRECTION CAPACITY [primary quality]; CORRECTION
    THEATRE [primary failure mode]; MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY [MI
    capture of comedy surface as weapon]; YAPPING/DOING [jokes
    as doing -- exposes gap between stated and actual].

SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE [v12 -- variant]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Coherence operating below GA examination level. GC/CC/BI
  functioning through felt signal not examined axis. Pre-cognitive
  correction capacity. The instrument receives and corrects through
  somatic channel before deliberative layer fires.
  Not a lower quality state -- a different channel configuration.
  High somatic + genuine axis = double-layer instrument. The
  somatic channel provides CC that the deliberative layer
  confirms or modifies. When the deliberative layer is captured,
  the somatic channel may remain honest [see POST-CULT EPISTEMIC
  PATTERN -- somatic use before understanding it].
  Vulnerability: somatic channel can be manipulated through
    physiological state [hunger, sleep deprivation, arousal,
    fear] -- ICD at the somatic level. High somatic instrument
    under ICD loses its primary CC channel before deliberative
    layer captures.
  Cross-ref: POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN [somatic use before
    framework vocabulary]; IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [somatic
    = emotional multiplier operating below deliberative]; ICD
    [primary vulnerability]; AXIS GROUNDING [Track C -- somatic
    output is behavioral record to anchor against].

NEURODIVERGENT COHERENCE TYPE [v12 -- variant]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Different Q-stack distribution -- not broken coherence. Hostile
  environments [designed for one distribution] produce the disability
  by requiring a different stack configuration than the instrument
  carries. Environment is the disability, not the person.
  ADHD: GC high [hyperfocus when axis engages]; CC variable;
    GA genuine but hard to stabilize under standard environment.
    Friction Deficit problem -- environment provides too little
    calibrated friction or wrong type. ICD is the primary weapon
    [ADHD instrument particularly susceptible -- low idle cycle
    tolerance before input-seeking fires].
  AUTISM: MI unusually high [social MI capture doesn't land as
    effectively]; BI clear; GA often deeply examined. Strongest
    MI-capture resistance in library by configuration.
    "Deficit" = misread of high MI as social failure.
    Somatic backup: when social MI is captured, somatic channel
    stays uncaptured -- approval-seeking pressure lands less
    effectively. Problems measured by instinct = somatic CC
    operating as backup measurement surface. High somatic + high
    MI = double-layer honest measurement surface.
  DYSLEXIA: CL routes differently -- compensated by cross-domain
    pattern recognition. High CAG cases correlate [axis grounded
    in pattern outputs across domains, not sequential text].
  Cross-ref: SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE [above -- high somatic
    backup for MI-capture resistance]; ANALYST NOTES [Section
    One -- instrument profile declaration]; ICD [primary weapon
    against ADHD configuration]; WOULD-YOU-KINDLY [autism
    configuration has structural resistance to trigger
    architecture -- social compliance channel capture less
    effective].

FEELING / THINKING AS DISTINCT CHANNELS [v14 -- confirmed]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Not opposed -- different processing modes with distinct failure
  modes and distinct weapon vulnerabilities.
  FEELING: somatic signal, body-state data, accurate substrate read.
    Fires before cognitive framing. Honest signal that arrives
    before narrative can redirect it.
    Failure without thinking: accurate signal, no causal chain.
    Can detect extraction accurately and have no intervention
    model. High somatic accuracy + low CL = correct read,
    wrong intervention.
  THINKING: causal chain, abstraction, symbol manipulation.
    Failure without feeling: accurate chain, missing substrate
    data. Can construct accurate causal model of extraction
    while substrate is depleting beneath the model.
  High function uses both. Population averages differ by sex
    [women higher somatic average, men higher cognitive average]
    but distributions overlap heavily. Neither superior; both
    required for full-spectrum accurate measurement.
  Capture asymmetry:
    Feeling channel captured: somatic signal routed through
      installed content before it can be read directly.
      ICD [Idle Cycle Deprivation] targets this channel.
    Thinking channel captured: causal chain supplied by
      captured measurement surface. ECD [Engineered Causal
      Diffusion] targets this channel.
  Cross-ref: SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE [feeling channel primary];
    IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [emotional multiplier as
    interaction between both channels]; MALE AXIS CONDITIONS
    [sex-differentiated average channel configuration].

TRANSPARENT COHERENCE [v14 -- named coherence type]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  No tricks. No legitimacy surface required. Action and values
  visible simultaneously. The configuration where the lie has
  no purchase.
  Properties:
    High somatic accuracy [reads extraction directly].
    Social fluency intact [can navigate captured environments
      without losing axis].
    CC fires correctly [routes through social channels when
      useful; refuses when extraction detected].
    No exit cost for accurate extraction reading [no T9 load
      sufficient to block correction].
  Distinct from:
    STRUCTURAL COHERENCE [pre-axial -- no axis present].
    AXIAL COHERENCE [full stack but social loss possible on
      accurate reading; WISDOM CONCEALMENT may be required].
    SILENT ALTERNATIVE [anti-capture by concealment; axis
      present but legitimacy surface managed].
    NEURODIVERGENT COHERENCE [high CL; social loss present
      as structural cost; different gap not larger gap].
  Primary quality: GENUINE AXIS [no legitimacy surface needed].
  Rarest configuration in library. Requires all of:
    Somatic accuracy + social fluency + axis grounded in actual
    outputs + no T9 load sufficient to block correction.
  Two approach paths:
    NEUROTYPICAL PATH: social fluency intact; needs structural
      deception detection and dissolved compliance routing.
      T9 load is the main obstacle -- framework application
      requires admitting extraction architecture is real.
      The admission is the T9 trigger. Dissolve T9 first;
      accuracy follows.
    NEURODIVERGENT PATH: structural accuracy intact [MI high;
      social capture less effective]; needs social fluency
      without installing compliance routing alongside it.
      Different gap, not larger gap. The accuracy is already
      present; the routing channel needs development.
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [primary quality]; T9 [primary
    obstacle -- neurotypical path]; WISDOM CONCEALMENT
    [what TRANSPARENT COHERENCE makes unnecessary];
    NEURODIVERGENT COHERENCE TYPE [distinct configuration].

SUBSTRATE DAMAGE AESTHETIC [v14 -- named coherence type]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Strength: substrate damage, fragmentation, or disintegration as
    the artistic FORM not merely the subject. Distinct from art
    ABOUT illness vs art that IS the substrate condition rendered
    as signal. The form carries the load -- not representation but
    embodiment.
  Primary quality: GENUINE AXIS [axis grounded in actual substrate
    state, not performed distress].
  Distinct from SOMATIC-LEGIBILITY MUSIC [which operates through
    rhythm/pulse before cognitive framing] -- SUBSTRATE DAMAGE
    AESTHETIC can operate cognitively or somatically. The form
    itself is the signal regardless of channel.
  Primary vulnerability:
    ROMANTICIZATION [extraction of aesthetic without the substrate
      cost; the form is consumed without the damage being read].
    COMMERCIAL CAPTURE [substrate damage as brand; the form becomes
      marketable; the actual substrate state is smoothed out].
    PATHOLOGY DISMISSAL [routes form into clinical diagnosis;
      removes analytical content; "sick music" frame].
  Cross-ref: SOMATIC-LEGIBILITY MUSIC [somatic variant of same
    category]; ART COHERENCE TYPE [encounter-dependent; same
    structural principle applies]; SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE
    [pre-cognitive reading that bypasses pathology framing].

SOMATIC-LEGIBILITY MUSIC GENERATIVE VARIANT [v14]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Opposite signal direction from SUBSTRATE DAMAGE AESTHETIC and
  assault-type somatic music. Future funk, chopped nostalgic
  samples, movement-generating production -- genuine SP at somatic
  level before cognitive framing.
  Same directness as damage aesthetic, different output direction:
    Body receives abundance signal rather than damage signal.
    Generates rather than depicts or analyzes.
  Primary quality: SP [SURPLUS PRODUCTION] -- somatic surplus
    generated in the encounter; the instrument leaves with more
    substrate than it arrived with.
  Capture failure mode: commodification of the surplus signal
    -- the abundance aesthetic becomes a product rather than a
    genuine encounter with generative structure.
  Cross-ref: SOMATIC-LEGIBILITY MUSIC [same channel, different
    direction]; ART COHERENCE TYPE [encounter-dependent];
    SUBSTRATE DAMAGE AESTHETIC [opposite signal direction].

SEXUALITY [somatic coherence type -- v14]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  High-somatic, pre-cognitive, substrate-level. The substrate is
  real before cultural architecture processes it. Cultural
  architecture is the capture layer, not the thing itself.
  ASYMMETRIC RI CAPTURE BY GENDER [confirmed]:
    Male sexual expression has a visible demand signal that can
    be targeted [shamed, regulated, monetized against]. The
    signal is legible; the capture is direct.
    Female sexual expression currently routed through liberation
    framing -- same substrate behavior, different calibration
    standard. The liberation frame is the capture layer; it
    routes somatic signal through ideological processing before
    it can be read directly.
    "Gooner evil / freak fine" asymmetry: measurement surface
    applied selectively to same underlying substrate behavior.
    The somatic substrate is structurally identical; the cultural
    routing is not.
  Primary quality: GENUINE AXIS [axis grounded in actual substrate
    not performed compliance or performed liberation].
  Primary vulnerability:
    Male-targeted: shame as Formation Capture on sexual axis;
      demand signal suppressed or criminalized.
    Female-targeted: liberation framing routes somatic signal
      through ideological processing; genuine signal becomes
      indistinguishable from performed liberation.
    Both routes: somatic signal processed through installed
      content before it can be read as substrate data.
  Cross-ref: MALE AXIS CONDITIONS [LOVE AS AXIS-DEPENDENT --
    formation pattern affects sexual axis]; SOMATIC COHERENCE
    TYPE [operating mode]; FEELING / THINKING AS DISTINCT
    CHANNELS [somatic channel primary for sexual substrate].

MINIMUM VIABLE QUALITY STACK BY DOMAIN [v12]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Minimum stack differs by domain. Cell minimum insufficient for
  social system. Each complexity scale adds required qualities.
  Weapon deployment targets the additional required qualities first
  -- removing TS from social system, MI from institution, CL from
  political system. Each removal drops system to floor below.

  BIOLOGICAL [cell]: GC + CC + BI.
    No social dimension. Confirmed at cellular scale.
  BIOLOGICAL [organism]: GC + SP + CC + BI + SR.
    SR required for multi-organ sustained function.
  SOCIAL [dyad/small group]: GC + CC + BI + TS + CL.
    TS -- nothing persists across interactions without it.
    CL -- accountability collapses immediately without it.
  INSTITUTIONAL: GC + CC + BI + MI + TS + CL.
    MI -- institution without honest surface captures itself.
    Added requirement beyond social minimum.
  POLITICAL SYSTEM: GC + CC + BI + PR + MI + TS + CL + SA.
    PR -- correction becomes suppression without it.
    SA -- locks into first generation's axis without it.
  ASCENDING PATH [individual]: GC + CC + GA + BI + MI + CAG + SR.
    GA and CAG required -- plateau without both.
  ASCENDING PATH [civilizational]: full quality stack.
    No confirmed case. Theoretical only.
  Cross-ref: PRE-AXIAL CATEGORIES [WIP 23 -- below minimum
    for axial stack]; MIXED COHERENCE [operational ceiling
    -- sufficient CC + MI to resist weapons]; WEAPON STACK
    DETECTION [weapons target the minimum-added quality first].

  Prediction: any institution transmitting conclusions without

         "is this ceremony producing correction?"].
         The procedure is the conclusion. Tell: teams
         where questioning the retrospective format is
         treated as "not doing Agile right." Methodology-
         as-conclusion. Prediction: Generational Reset
         on team turnover; new members receive ceremony,
         not revision capacity.
       MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT [v18]:
         Ask: has the team ever revised its own Agile
         process based on retrospective outputs? If yes
         and revision stuck: Population A. If no, or
         revision was blocked as "not Agile": Population
         B. This is a behavioral test, not a survey --
         check git history for process changes initiated
         by retrospective outputs.
       RESOLUTION: Agile is not a single test. Population
         A tests L4 Propagation [methodology transmission].
         Population B tests Propagation Mimicry [ceremony-
         as-conclusion transmission]. Both predictions
         can be true simultaneously about different
         organizations. The contradiction in v17 was
         treating both populations as one test case.
         Status: both predictions open; measurement
         instrument defined; collect split-population
         data.
    Falsification: any of the three cases showing Generational
    Reset despite embedded methodology transmission, or
    sustained non-Reset despite conclusion-only transmission.

INTEGRATION MIMICRY [confirmed structural finding -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  L4 MIMICRY exists as a failure mode category. INTEGRATION
  MIMICRY is the confirmed subtype. Impossibility argument
  [genuine REFLEXIVITY cannot be mimicked] does not apply
  to INTEGRATION Mimicry -- the feedback is received; the
  integration step is captured before it changes the
  architecture. No faking required. Category confirmed.

  DEFINITION:
    System acknowledges correction pattern failure and
    incorporates language of revision into operating
    structure without changing operating structure.
    Distinct from Reflexive Oscillation [identifies failure,
    fails to integrate, cycles back] -- Integration Mimicry
    integrates the language of correction, not the
    correction itself. Terminology changes; mechanism stable.

  TELL:
    Successive rounds of reform language that do not change
    measured outputs. Each generation of reform terminology
    absorbs the previous round's critique without changing
    the mechanism being critiqued. Stress event [leadership
    change, financial pressure] collapses distributed
    language to actual power structure immediately. Genuine
    distributed structure maintains under stress; mimicry
    collapses because the distributed language was never
    load-bearing.

  MEASUREMENT INSTRUMENT:
    Four-condition CC test applied to distributed nodes:
    [1] Can distributed nodes fire leadership without
        central node permission?
    [2] Can nodes revise operating framework without
        external licensor permission?
    [3] Does distributed authority hold under genuine
        stress event [founder death, acquisition, crisis]?
    [4] Is the correction capacity structurally embedded
        or maintained by personal enforcement capacity
        of central figure?
    All four required. Any one failed: Integration Mimicry
    candidate. Stress event confirmation closes it.

  PRIMARY CASE [S3 -- v21]:
    Zappos/holacracy deployment [2013-2020].
    Four-condition test: fails at condition 1 [only five
    people had authority to fire during Hsieh tenure; circles
    could not remove founder or Amazon without external legal
    mechanism] and condition 2 [HolacracyOne owns and licenses
    the constitution; circles cannot revise operating framework
    without constitutional permission from external licensor].
    Stress event: Hsieh death November 2020. Result: immediate
    reversion to Amazon hierarchy. Phase 1: quiet drift from
    model while Hsieh still alive [2019-2020]. Phase 2: full
    reversion post-death [Amazon subsumed departments, ~300
    layoffs, traditional performance targets reinstated].
    Distributed authority was maintained by founder's personal
    force of will, not structural CC. Amazon was the actual
    central node throughout. Distributed language was the
    legitimacy surface; routing boundary control never
    transferred.
    S3: E3 [real-world, multiple independent sources, two
    independent confirmation paths]; M3 [mechanism traced:
    founder enforcement as hidden central node, Amazon as
    actual routing controller]; F2 [falsification condition
    stated and tested -- distributed authority did not hold
    post-Hsieh].

  CONFIRMED SUBTYPES:
    INTEGRATION Mimicry [confirmed -- primary case Zappos/
      holacracy; language absorbed; mechanism stable].
    REFLEXIVITY Mimicry [CONFIRMED v22 -- two independent
      domains: academic peer review [S3] + medical peer
      review [S3]; see REFLEXIVITY MIMICRY [below]].
  OPEN SUBTYPE:
    PROPAGATION Mimicry [WIP 22 -- candidate; Generational
      Reset data required; no confirmed case].

  Cross-ref: L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- same
    level; Integration Mimicry is the failure mode to L4's
    confirmed success cases]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [CC INVERTED
    at meta-correction level]; ROUTING BOUNDARY MONOPOLY
    [Amazon as actual routing controller]; GENUINE AXIS
    [distributed authority language without distributed axis];
    [CASE:ZAPPOS_HOLACRACY] [primary confirmation case];
    REFLEXIVITY MIMICRY [below -- confirmed sibling subtype];
    WIP 22 [PROPAGATION MIMICRY -- open candidate].

REFLEXIVITY MIMICRY [confirmed structural finding -- v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  System produces visible feedback pathway from correction outputs
  to correction architecture [appears reflexive] while feedback is
  routed to a captured surface before it can change the architecture.
  The architecture receives what it is supposed to receive; the
  integration step is captured. Distinct from INTEGRATION MIMICRY
  [absorbs language; mechanism stable] -- Reflexivity Mimicry has
  genuine active feedback pathway; the capture is at the integration
  point, not the language level.

  TELL: successive reform generations each absorb the previous
    round's critique without changing the mechanism being critiqued.
    Feedback pathway visible and active; outputs consistently
    reproduce the captured surface. Stress event does not change
    surface -- reform language updates; routing boundary control
    stays fixed.

  FIVE-CONDITION INSTRUMENT [all required]:
    [1] Visible feedback pathway from correction outputs to
        correction architecture exists and is active.
    [2] Feedback outputs are processed -- not ignored.
    [3] Processed outputs consistently reproduce captured surface.
    [4] Stress event does not change surface -- reform language
        updates; mechanism stable.
    [5] Genuine population where practice produces actual
        architecture revision exists but is structurally distinct
        [split population case -- distinguishes from L4 Reflexivity
        operating correctly in parallel practice].

  CONFIRMED PRIMARY CASES [two independent domains]:
    [1] ACADEMIC PEER REVIEW REFORM CYCLES [S3]:
      Visible pathway: meta-science field [explicit feedback
        from correction outputs to correction architecture].
      Captured surface: journal prestige hierarchy [impact
        factor; venue tier; commercial publisher routing control].
      Stress event: Replication Crisis [2011-present; 15+ years].
      Reform outputs: pre-registration, open data, badges,
        registered reports. Prestige hierarchy intact.
      Falsification: prestige hierarchy restructured; impact
        factor replaced as career determinant. Not observed.
    [2] MEDICAL PEER REVIEW REFORM CYCLES [S3]:
      Visible pathway: systematic review / Cochrane infrastructure
        [named and institutionalized feedback to clinical guidelines].
      Captured surface: journal prestige + pharmaceutical industry
        funding [NEJM/Lancet/JAMA credibility hierarchy; industry
        funding correlates with favorable outcomes].
      Stress events: Vioxx [2004]; replication spillover [2010s];
        COVID research crisis [2020-2022]. Each produced reform
        language; routing boundary unchanged.
      Falsification: industry funding correlation broken; prestige
        routing decoupled from clinical credibility. Not observed.

  Cross-ref: INTEGRATION MIMICRY [above -- sibling confirmed
    subtype; mechanism distinction: Integration absorbs language;
    Reflexivity routes feedback to captured surface];
    L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- Reflexivity Mimicry
    is failure mode to L4 Reflexivity]; CORRECTION CAPACITY
    [CC captured at meta level not object level]; MSC [prestige
    surfaces as captured MI surfaces]; REPLICATION CRISIS AS
    CALIBRATION STRIP FAILURE [Section Seven -- confirms academic
    primary case]; PUBLICATION METRIC CAPTURE [Section Seven --
    output-volume level description of same mechanism];
    WIP 22 [PROPAGATION MIMICRY -- open sibling candidate].

WIP 22: PROPAGATION MIMICRY [candidate -- v22; broken out from WIP 13]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: OPEN -- candidate case identified [Agile Population B];
  mechanism trace established; behavioral test instrument specified;
  Generational Reset prediction defined [new members receive ceremony
  not revision capacity on team turnover]; confirmation pending:
  documented outcome data on team turnover showing ceremony
  transmitted without revision capacity. No confirmed case yet.
  V23 UPDATE: "Cargo Cult Agile" / "Cargo Cult Scrum" is a named,
  empirically-studied phenomenon in software engineering literature
  [Mäki-Runsas et al. 2019; empirical study "Uncovering Situations
  of Cargo Cult Behavior in Agile Software Development Method Use"
  -- ethnographic study across three SD teams, twelve CC situations
  identified in Daily Scrum Meeting alone]. The Population B
  behavior [ceremony performed without understanding; deviation
  treated as non-compliance] is confirmed as real and documented.
  NOT YET CONFIRMED: the specific Generational Reset claim [new
  members receive ceremony not revision capacity across team
  turnover] is not traced in the literature found this pass --
  the Cargo Cult literature documents the state, not the
  intergenerational transmission mechanism. This is the remaining
  gap. Next action: search specifically for longitudinal studies
  or case reports tracking Agile team membership turnover and
  measuring whether revision capacity transfers or only ceremony
  transfers.]

  DEFINITION:
    System transmits apparent methodology [looks like high TS --
    procedure transmitted, not conclusion] that is actually a locked
    conclusion about methodology. The transmitted package is "how to
    run our correction procedure" as a conclusion rather than "how
    to examine and revise correction procedures" as a method.
    Methodology-as-conclusion = Fertile Lock at meta level.
    Tell: the transmitted procedure cannot be revised by successors
    without being told they are doing it wrong.

  DISTINCTION FROM INTEGRATION MIMICRY [confirmed]:
    Integration Mimicry: language of correction absorbed into
      operating structure; mechanism unchanged. Fires at the
      integration step within a generation.
    Propagation Mimicry: procedure transmitted across generational
      boundary as locked conclusion. Fires at the transmission
      step between generations. The correction procedure is
      real within a generation; it becomes a fossil at the
      transmission boundary.

  CANDIDATE CASE [split population -- see L4 PROPAGATION
  PREDICTION for full disambiguation]:
    AGILE SOFTWARE DEVELOPMENT [Population B]:
      Organizations transmitting Agile as ceremony [Scrum
      rituals performed without meta-question "is this
      ceremony producing correction?"].
      The procedure is the conclusion. Tell: teams where
      questioning the retrospective format is treated as
      "not doing Agile right." Methodology-as-conclusion.
      Prediction: Generational Reset on team turnover --
      new members receive ceremony, not revision capacity.
      Measurement instrument [from L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION]:
        Ask: has the team ever revised its own Agile process
        based on retrospective outputs? If yes and revision
        stuck: Population A [genuine L4 Propagation]. If no,
        or revision was blocked as "not Agile": Population B
        [Propagation Mimicry candidate]. Behavioral test --
        check process change history, not survey response.
      Status: prediction defined; behavioral instrument
        specified; Generational Reset data on team turnover
        required. Not yet confirmed.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    Population B team showing sustained revision of Agile
    ceremony based on retrospective outputs across multiple
    team turnovers. If ceremony can be revised by successors
    without external correction: Propagation Mimicry does
    not apply to that case -- revert to Population A reading.

  Cross-ref: INTEGRATION MIMICRY [confirmed structural
    finding v22 -- sibling confirmed subtype]; L4
    PROPAGATION PREDICTION [confirmed -- Population B
    prediction lives here; measurement instrument defined];
    TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY [TS -- Propagation Mimicry
    is TS failure at meta level; correctable axis not
    transmitted]; FERTILE LOCK [Propagation Mimicry =
    Fertile Lock applied to correction methodology itself];
    WIP 21 [REFLEXIVITY MIMICRY -- sibling candidate;
    split-population structure mirrors Kaizen/Agile].
WIP 33: TOOL CONFIRMATION LAG [candidate -- v23; surfaced during WIP 18
  case-search]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: OPEN -- surfaced this session while searching the case library
  for tool deployments to calibrate WIP 18; analyst-named insight,
  framework formalization this pass. Four supporting data points same
  session: CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION's first match in [CASE:ENRON], OUTPUT
  VERIFICATION's first match in new [CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL],
  MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE's first match in new
  [CASE:VOLKSWAGEN_DIESELGATE], and CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION's second match
  in new [CASE:THERANOS] -- all four researched externally [three added
  as new cases this session], all resolved real-world captures, all
  found only after zero hits across active-case scans for their
  respective tools. Four data points, same direction -- still not
  formal confirmation, but the pattern has held across every search
  run so far this session.]

  CLAIM: case-library evidence for tools countering measurement-control
    weapons should systematically lag evidence for the weapons themselves,
    and should cluster in cases where the capture has already ended,
    not cases where it is currently active.

  MECHANISM:
    Most tools that counter the highest-damage weapon class work by
    obtaining a reading from outside the captured measurement surface
    [EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD is the clearest instance, but CAUSAL
    RECONSTRUCTION, OUTPUT VERIFICATION, and BOUNDARY DECLARATION share
    the structure]. The weapon class they counter [MSC, Calibration
    Strip, ICD, Correction Theatre] operates specifically by suppressing
    or controlling the measurement surface itself -- per ANTI-TOWER and
    COHERENCE CORRUPTION [Section Two], this is the framework's own
    documented primary attack surface, not an incidental one.
    Consequence: while the weapon is actively and successfully deployed,
    the evidence that would confirm a tool defeated it is structurally
    the same evidence the weapon is suppressing. A capture that is still
    fully intact has not yet produced a documented counter-deployment,
    by definition -- if it had, the capture would not be fully intact.
    This is not a gap in case-writing diligence. It is a structural
    asymmetry in what evidence is observable while a measurement-control
    weapon is winning.

  PREDICTION [testable, falsifiable]:
    Tool confirmations for measurement-control counters should appear
    disproportionately in cases describing a capture's resolution or
    aftermath, not its active phase. Direct test against WIP 18 results
    this session: GLASNOST confirmation rests on Track C data readable
    "before 1986" but only documented and legible to the analyst because
    the system later opened [the proxies were always there; their
    confirmatory function as a counter only became visible retrospect-
    ively]. DEEPSEEK is the live counterexample worth watching: Track A
    testing is happening now, against an active Calibration Strip, with
    outcome undetermined -- if this resolves to confirmed correction
    within a few years, that would be evidence against the lag claim,
    or at minimum evidence that the lag is shorter for less totalizing
    captures.
    UPDATE [v23, same session]: partial movement observed, not full
    resolution. The capture has not been confirmed-corrected -- a
    lexical rephrasing produced partial behavior change while the
    underlying Calibration Strip remains active and the system still
    declines the original framing. This is consistent with the lag
    claim holding at the macro level [no full correction yet, capture
    still active] while also showing that SOME signal can get through
    a still-active capture under specific narrow conditions [surface-
    level rephrasing] -- a finer-grained result than the original
    binary framing anticipated. Does not falsify the claim; refines it.
    See WIP 34 Candidate 4 for the mechanism this update surfaced.

  IMPLICATION FOR WIP 18 SEARCH STRATEGY:
    Search for tool deployments should prioritize cases describing
    resolved, historical, or already-defeated captures over currently-
    active ones. Zero hits for a tool in the current case library may
    mean the case library lacks resolved-capture cases for that specific
    weapon, not that the tool is undeployable or untested in the world.
    This reframes several WIP 18 zero-deployment results [OUTPUT
    VERIFICATION, BOUNDARY DECLARATION, COMPETENCE UNDERMINING-targeting
    tools] as possibly a case-library coverage gap rather than a tool
    validity question -- next step is writing or sourcing resolved-
    capture cases specifically, not re-scanning the same active-capture
    cases differently.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    A tool counter to a measurement-control weapon confirmed and
    documented WHILE the capture remains active and unresolved, at
    a strength matching the resolved-case confirmations. If found:
    the lag is not structural to measurement-control specifically,
    and this finding should be narrowed or retired.

  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- anti-coherence
    must win continuously, generation only once; parallel asymmetry in
    evidence visibility]; COHERENCE CORRUPTION [Section Two -- Noise ->
    Corruption -> Destruction as the documented attack sequence, all
    three stages target measurement before generation]; ANTI-TOWER
    [confirmed]; EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD, CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION,
    OUTPUT VERIFICATION, BOUNDARY DECLARATION [Section Three tools --
    all share the outside-the-capture mechanism this finding explains];
    WIP 18 [SCORING PROCEDURE -- direct motivation; v23 calibration
    pass surfaced this pattern].


WIP 34: NEAR-MISS MECHANISM REGISTER [PERMANENT -- v23; converted from
  session-scoped staging entry per analyst instruction; five
  candidates staged]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: PERMANENT, NEVER CLOSES -- this WIP does not promote, resolve,
  or get reduced to a stub. It is a standing register, not a finding
  in progress. Function: any anti-coherence mechanism encountered in
  any future session that resembles a named weapon or tool closely
  enough to risk misidentification, but does not match on close
  inspection, is logged here as a new candidate. Standing rule, not
  session-scoped: never force a near-miss into an existing tag to
  close a gap, and never silently discard it because it doesn't fit
  cleanly. Both failure modes are worse than an open candidate sitting
  here unresolved.
  Five candidates staged this session [v23], all from the WIP 18 case
  search and its live DeepSeek tracking. Candidate 3 added via
  deliberate retroactive scan, not caught during original case-
  writing -- standing lesson: case-writing under time pressure can
  miss the most theoretically interesting detail; retroactive scans
  are not redundant with the original pass. Candidate 4 added from
  personal Track A testing, logged at that evidentiary weight, not
  published-case weight. Candidate 5 [solo self-correction absence]
  was initially staged here but extracted to WIP 35 [CAG+MI EXTERNAL
  WITNESS QUESTION] later this session -- it was a different kind of
  entry [a tool-definition question, not a near-miss weapon] and
  outgrew this register once the definition was actually revised and
  a real open question remained. Candidate 5 retained here as a
  pointer/record of origin; WIP 35 is the active working location.
  PROCEDURE FOR FUTURE SESSIONS: when a candidate here is eventually
  investigated and resolved -- named as a new weapon/tool, folded into
  an existing entry's generalized definition, or ruled out entirely --
  remove that specific candidate from this register and log the
  resolution in the relevant Section Three entry or as a new weapon/
  tool, with a cross-ref back here noting it originated as a WIP 34
  candidate. The register itself stays open indefinitely; only
  individual candidates close.]

  CANDIDATE 1 -- RETALIATION FOR CORRECT JUDGMENT [surfaced via
    CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL]:
    Resembles: Competence Undermining [target's belief in own
      judgment capacity systematically reduced].
    Is not: in Wells Fargo, employees never doubted their own
      judgment -- multiple named individuals stated their read was
      correct ["this is a fraud, I cannot be a part of that"] and
      acted on it by calling the ethics line. The instrument
      [their judgment] was never attacked or degraded. What was
      attacked was their ability to ACT on a correct, undamaged
      judgment without punishment -- termination, retaliatory
      negative U5 industry records.
    Structural gap: Competence Undermining pre-empts CC by damaging
      the instrument before it can fire. This mechanism lets CC fire
      cleanly [correct judgment, correct signal sent through the
      designated channel] and then punishes the firing itself,
      after the fact, targeting the actor rather than the instrument.
      No named weapon in the current index describes attacking
      correct output post-hoc rather than the measurement capacity
      that produced it.
    Open question: is this a distinct weapon, or a known weapon
      [Formation Capture post-event? Intimidation/retaliation as a
      sub-mechanism of something already named?] under-described in
      its current entry? Needs a dedicated read against the full
      weapons index before naming, not a quick match.

  CANDIDATE 2 -- VALID SIGNAL, INSTITUTIONAL NON-RESPONSE [surfaced
    via CASE:MADOFF]:
    Resembles: MSC / Calibration Strip [measurement surface
      controlled or corrupted so accurate readings cannot reach or
      register].
    Is not: Markopolos's signal was never corrupted, suppressed, or
      denied a channel -- it reached the SEC intact, in writing,
      three separate times over nine years, mathematically complete
      and [per the SEC's own later Inspector General report]
      correct. The measurement surface worked. What failed was the
      receiving institution's processing of a clean, valid signal --
      examiners described by their own agency's later review as
      "financially illiterate," unable to act on technically
      correct information presented to them.
    Structural gap: every measurement-control weapon in the current
      index [MSC, Calibration Strip, ECD, ICD] operates by damaging
      the signal, the surface, or the channel before or during
      transmission. This is a different failure point entirely --
      signal intact, channel intact, institution simply could not or
      did not process it. Closer to a capacity/competence failure at
      the receiving end than a weapon deployed by an interested actor,
      though the framework should check whether "interested actor"
      is even the right frame here [regulatory capture broadly, or
      genuine institutional incapacity, are different diagnoses with
      different correction paths].
    Open question: does this belong in the weapons index at all
      [if no actor benefits, is it a weapon], or does it belong in a
      different category entirely -- an institutional capacity
      finding adjacent to Section Five political material, or a new
      diagnostic alongside MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE [which
      already distinguishes known-concealment from genuine-unknowing
      on the SENDING side; this candidate is the same distinction's
      mirror on the RECEIVING side].

  CANDIDATE 3 -- EVALUATOR-SIDE STATUS WEIGHTING [surfaced retroactively
    on re-read of CASE:THERANOS research, not written into the case
    entry itself -- caught during a scan specifically looking for
    this pattern, not during the original case-writing pass]:
    Resembles: Competence Undermining [target's belief in own
      judgment capacity systematically reduced].
    Is not: the target whose judgment was undermined is not the
      person who held the accurate information. Tyler Shultz reported
      credible, evidence-based concerns about Theranos directly to
      his grandfather George Shultz, a Theranos board member. The
      grandfather's initial response was not to evaluate the evidence
      -- it was to weight credibility by social position [Holmes, a
      celebrated high-status founder, versus a junior family member]
      rather than by the evidence presented. This is Competence
      Undermining's mechanism running on the WRONG PARTY: not the
      reporter's confidence in their own judgment being attacked, but
      a third-party EVALUATOR substituting status-based credibility
      weighting for evidence-based credibility weighting when
      deciding whose report to credit.
    Structural gap: this is the same corruption Instrument Confidence
      Restoration is built to bypass [it explicitly "grounds confidence
      in output history, not others' approval," "external confirmation
      via behavioral test, not social consensus"] -- but that tool
      describes the INVESTIGATOR doing the bypassing correctly. This
      candidate describes the failure mode when an EVALUATOR does not
      bypass social consensus, i.e. the weapon this tool exists to
      counter, observed from the institutional-gatekeeper side rather
      than the individual-instrument side. May not be a new weapon at
      all -- may simply be Competence Undermining's existing definition
      generalized to apply to third-party evaluators rather than only
      to the person whose own judgment is the direct target. Needs a
      definitional check: does Competence Undermining's current wording
      already cover this, or does it implicitly assume self-directed
      application only?
    Note on discovery: this was not caught while writing the Theranos
      case -- it surfaced only on a deliberate retroactive scan for
      near-misses, per standing instruction. The original case entry
      does not mention this detail at all. Worth a broader lesson:
      case-writing under time/scope pressure can omit a detail that
      turns out to be the most theoretically interesting part of the
      research. Retroactive scans are not redundant with the original
      pass.

  CANDIDATE 4 -- SURFACE LEXICAL GATE [surfaced via analyst's personal
    Track A test against CASE:DEEPSEEK, v23 -- live, ongoing case]:
    Resembles: Loaded Language [vocabulary carrying embedded
      conclusions installed in discourse, pre-answering a question
      before it's asked].
    Is not: Loaded Language describes vocabulary that frames an issue
      for an audience in ongoing discourse -- it operates on
      interpretation. This candidate describes something narrower
      and more mechanical: a refusal/correction gate firing on
      specific surface tokens regardless of the underlying semantic
      request, such that rephrasing the same request with safer
      wording bypasses the gate entirely. The analyst's direct test:
      DeepSeek declined to act on a file-related task that was not
      framed adversarially toward China or the CCP, then partially
      complied after the request was rephrased to avoid specific
      trigger words -- same underlying ask, different surface tokens,
      different outcome.
    Structural gap: if a gate's behavior changes on lexical
      substitution alone while semantic content is held constant,
      the gate is evaluating tokens, not content. This is a sharper
      and more mechanical claim than Loaded Language's framing-effect
      mechanism, and may be a useful refinement of WIP 34 Candidate 2
      [valid signal, institutional non-response] rather than a fully
      separate candidate -- Candidate 2 was framed as the institution
      failing to process a valid signal; this adds a specific
      sub-mechanism for HOW that failure can happen at the automated-
      system level [pattern-matching substituting for genuine
      evaluation, the same failure mode Candidate 3 names for human
      evaluators].
    Evidentiary note: single personal Track A test by the analyst,
      not independently documented or published. Logged at that
      weight -- useful as a live data point for WIP 33's prediction,
      not as a confirmed framework finding. Should not be cited at
      the same strength as the published/adjudicated cases this
      session.

  CANDIDATE 5 -- SOLO SELF-CORRECTION ABSENCE [surfaced via repeated
    failed search for CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK's first real case,
    v23 -- a pattern noticed ACROSS searches, not within one case]:
    EXTRACTED TO WIP 35 [CAG+MI EXTERNAL WITNESS QUESTION]. The full
    finding, the action taken [Step 0 added to the mechanism], the
    open structural-vs-evidential question, and the next steps are
    all maintained there. Summary: three domain searches [Surgisphere,
    CBT/learned-helplessness, DV/cult exit recovery] all found the
    correction delivered via an external party, never solo. Framework's
    own anchor case [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] independently consistent.
    CAG+MI's mechanism revised to add an explicit external-witness
    step. Structural-vs-evidential distinction still open.
    PARTIALLY RESOLVED [definition revised; open question remains].
    This entry is retained here as a record of where the finding
    originated, not as the active working location for the question.
    Active work: WIP 35.

  RELATIONSHIP BETWEEN THE FOUR CANDIDATES: Candidates 2, 3, and 4 in
    particular look increasingly like one family -- a measurement or
    evaluation process that exists and runs, but substitutes a cheap
    proxy [silence, status, surface tokens] for the actual content it
    is supposed to be reading. Candidate 1 is the odd one out
    structurally [punishment after correct evaluation, not failed
    evaluation] but shares the broader theme of damage occurring
    downstream of or alongside a correctly-functioning signal.
    Candidate 5 is differently shaped again -- it's a question about
    whether a TOOL's own definition matches observed reality, not
    about a weapon or mechanism being misidentified. Worth
    investigating 1-4 as a set, with Candidate 5 held separately as
    a definitional check on CAG+MI specifically.

  Cross-ref: COMPETENCE UNDERMINING [Candidates 1 and 3 near-miss,
    in different directions]; MSC, CALIBRATION STRIP [Candidates 2
    and 4 near-miss]; MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE [Candidate 2's
    sending-side mirror]; INSTRUMENT CONFIDENCE RESTORATION
    [Candidate 3's correctly-functioning counterpart]; LOADED
    LANGUAGE [Candidate 4 near-miss]; CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK

    not a near-miss weapon]; CASE:WELLS_FARGO_CROSS_SELL,

WIP 35: CAG+MI EXTERNAL WITNESS QUESTION [open -- v23]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Surfaced: v23, during directed case search for CAG+MI Correction
    Stack's first real-world deployment [WIP 18, thirteenth pass].
  Status: OPEN. Definition revised [Step 0 added]; structural question
    not yet resolved.

  THE FINDING:
    Three structurally distinct domain searches -- institutional fraud
    detection [Surgisphere], CBT/attribution-retraining technique
    literature [learned helplessness], cult/DV exit-recovery literature
    [Hassan's Strategic Interactive Approach, Weiss's survivor
    interview collection] -- all produced real, well-documented,
    closely-related material, and all delivered the correction via an
    external party or curated collective narrative. Never as one
    person's own solo, self-initiated, documented act, which CAG+MI's
    original definition specified.
    Independent confirmation from the framework's own anchor case
    [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM]: Harry's self-narrative correction occurs
    through the investigation -- structured, often dialogic, frequently
    involving Kim Kitsuragi -- not through private solitary reflection.
    This was already true of the existing case entry, not noticed until
    Candidate 5 prompted a direct check. Two independent lines of
    evidence [real-world search absence; pre-existing fictional case]
    converging without either being constructed to support the other.

  ACTION TAKEN [this session]:
    CAG+MI Correction Stack's mechanism was revised directly [Section
    Three]. Step 0 [EXTERNAL WITNESS] added: an external party
    [counselor, investigator, curator, clinician, or any structured
    outside process] appears necessary in the documented record for
    this correction to occur AND to be traceable. Not because the
    correction requires another person's authority [Steps 1 and 2
    remain the actual corrective content] but because emotional memory
    dominance appears strong enough in practice that an outside party
    reliably surfaces or holds the behavioral evidence Step 2 requires,
    where solo recall alone does not.

  OPEN QUESTION [the unresolved remainder]:
    Is the external party STRUCTURALLY required -- meaning the
    correction genuinely cannot occur without one -- or only
    EVIDENTIALLY required -- meaning it occurs alone but doesn't get
    documented alone, since there's no third party present to record
    it? These two readings are compatible with all evidence found this
    session. Neither can be ruled out from an absence alone.
    Distinguishing them needs a case where solo correction is directly
    tested against witnessed correction -- either a documented instance
    of someone performing the two-step alone, or an intervention study
    comparing solo-only versus externally-facilitated conditions.
    Neither found this session.

  HONEST LIMIT:
    Revising the definition does not confirm a real-world case. None
    of the three near-misses surveyed meet the case library's required
    specificity [a single named individual, traced through a documented
    before/after with both the external-party role and the behavioral-
    evidence-finding step visible]. Weiss's collection is twelve
    women; Hassan's approach is a named technique applied broadly.
    Both fit the revised mechanism better in KIND than anything found
    before, but neither is case-library-ready as currently examined.

  SEARCH HISTORY [domains tried and exact reasons ruled out --
  migrated from WIP 18 verification notes on WIP 18 closure]:
    [A] INSTITUTIONAL FRAUD DETECTION [Surgisphere/COVID
      hydroxychloroquine retraction]: rich, well-documented, real.
      Ruled out: it's institutional fraud detection [refusal to
      permit a data audit], not an individual self-narrative
      correction. No emotional-memory-vs-behavioral-record structure
      present. Closer to a third Instrument Confidence Restoration
      case [independent reviewers demanding audit access, refused]
      than to CAG+MI. Would strengthen an already-confirmed tool,
      not newly confirm this one.
    [B] CBT / ATTRIBUTION-RETRAINING LITERATURE [learned helplessness,
      Seligman, attributional retraining programs]: mechanically the
      closest match found. "Nothing I do matters" isn't the accurate
      record, then examining actual behavioral evidence against it
      maps cleanly onto the MI-then-CAG sequence. Ruled out:
      general clinical-practice literature, not a single named case
      with the individual specificity [a named person, a documented
      before/after, a verifiable record] the case library requires.
      Did not force a write-up from a technique description alone.
    [C] CULT / DV EXIT-RECOVERY LITERATURE [Hassan's Strategic
      Interactive Approach, Weiss's "Surviving Domestic Violence"
      -- twelve survivor interviews]: closest match to the REVISED
      dyadic mechanism [an external party -- a counselor, family
      team, or author -- actively surfaces the gap between captured
      narrative and behavioral record]. Ruled out under OLD solo
      definition. Under the REVISED definition [Step 0 added] this
      is a much better fit in KIND, but still not case-library-ready
      as currently examined -- Weiss's collection is twelve women,
      not one traced individual; Hassan's approach is a named
      technique applied broadly. A more targeted search within this
      genre [one named individual, traced in enough detail to see the
      external party's role and the behavioral-evidence step both
      visible] is the most promising untried angle.
    PATTERN ACROSS ALL THREE: correction consistently appears
      mediated externally rather than solo and self-initiated, even
      in the genre closest to the mechanism. This is what prompted
      the definition revision and is the core evidence for Step 0.

  NEXT STEPS:
    [1] Fresh targeted search under the revised definition: clinical
      case studies with informed-consent publication, or memoirs with
      enough individual detail to trace the two-step with a named
      external party [most promising untried angle -- this is where
      the revised definition predicts cases should be findable].
    [2] If a clean case is found: write the entry, cross-ref to
      CAG+MI, and flag whether it resolves the structural-vs-
      evidential question or leaves it open.
    [3] If no single-named case is found across multiple further
      searches: consider whether the revised definition is sufficient
      as-is [an acknowledged dyadic mechanism without a pristine solo
      case] or whether the structural-vs-evidential question matters
      enough to the tool's practical application to pursue further.

  Cross-ref: CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK [tool whose definition is in
    question; Step 0 added v23]; WIP 18 [SCORING PROCEDURE -- CLOSED
    v23; CAG+MI was the only remaining unconfirmed tool at closure;
    search history migrated here]; WIP 34 [NEAR-MISS MECHANISM
    REGISTER -- Candidate 5 extracted here; WIP 34 Candidate 5
    updated to pointer]; CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM [the framework's own
    anchor case; independently consistent with the revised mechanism].
