WIP 19: THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL [v3 named; formalized v22]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[STATUS: OPEN -- model formalized v22 from case library reconstruction
  [v3 case readings as ground truth]; axis definitions reverse-engineered
  from confirmed case readings [Ranni, Bell Labs, Weimar, Catholic Church];
  operational procedures drafted; WIP 18 block LIFTED [WIP 18 closed v23];
  promotion pending: integration check with WIP 27 [three-layer extension]
  required before either promotes. Dedicated session needed for both.]

  PURPOSE:
    Restore comparative position output to framework readings. v3
    produced a coordinate per case; cases could be compared by
    position. v22 produces prose readings that are richer but not
    comparable in the same way. Three-axis model restores
    comparability without replacing prose depth.
    Complete reading output [target endpoint]:
      Named mechanism [v22 vocabulary] + score [WIP 18] +
      position [WIP 19]. All three together = framework as
      full measurement instrument.

  STRUCTURE:
    Center = functional coherence [unreachable structural constant;
      same as CENTER / point-truth in main theory]. All three axes
      orient toward center. Position reading = distance and direction
      from center on each axis. Trajectory reading = direction of
      movement on each axis across window.
    Axes are not binary poles -- they are directional vectors.
      A system is not "left" or "right" but at a position on the
      generation/extraction axis with a trajectory direction.
      Reading format: [AXIS1 position / AXIS2 position / AXIS3
      direction] for snapshot; add trajectory arrow for window read.

  THREE AXES [reconstructed from v3 case library -- v22]:

  AXIS 1 [horizontal]: GENERATION ↔ EXTRACTION
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    RIGHT = net generation. Surplus flows outward to defined
      population. GC completes, SP positive, CC functional.
    LEFT = net extraction. Surplus flows inward to terminal
      node. Cycle running but output captured before reaching
      population.
    CENTER = balanced [rare; structurally unstable; transition
      point between generation and extraction readings].
    Measurement: net transfer direction across defined window
      and population. Not intent -- output reading.
    Positions: right / right of center / center / left of
      center / left.

  AXIS 2 [vertical]: COHERENCE DEPTH
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    UP = higher level operational. L3-L4 functional. TS, SA,
      CAG running. Meta-correction present. Reflexivity possible.
    DOWN = lower level only. L1 functional at best. C2 and C3
      absent or collapsed. Cycle runs but cannot self-correct
      or transmit.
    CENTER [mid] = L2 functional, L3 partial. MI/CL/RI running;
      transmission present but not reliable.
    Measurement: highest confirmed operational level across
      defined window. Level must be behaviorally confirmed
      [outputs traceable], not claimed.
    Positions: up / upper-mid / mid / lower-mid / down.

  AXIS 3 [depth]: TRAJECTORY
  --------------------------------------------------------------------------
    FRONT = moving toward center. Correction active.
      Orientation improving. Gap between current position
      and center is closing.
    BACK = moving away from center. Degrading. Gap between
      current position and center is widening.
    STABLE = holding position. Not moving toward or away.
      Correction maintaining current state, not improving it.
    Measurement: position change across two time windows
      within the reading period. Requires minimum two
      snapshot readings to determine direction.
      Single snapshot = position only, no trajectory.
    Positions: front / stable / back.
    Note: front is relative to center, not to starting
      position. A system moving from left-extraction toward
      center is front even if still net-extractive. A system
      moving from right-generation away from center is back
      even if still net-generative.

  CONFIRMED CASE READINGS [v3 ground truth]:
    RANNI [Elden Ring -- completion]:
      Right [net generation -- Silent Alternative succeeds,
        surplus distributed to new order without extraction];
      Up [L3-L4 -- TS operational, SA transmitted, L4
        Reflexivity present in completing the plan across
        generations];
      Front [trajectory complete -- reached center-adjacent
        position at completion; correction arc finished].
      Coordinate: right / up / front.

    BELL LABS [1925-1984 peak window]:
      Right [net generation -- transistor, laser, Unix,
        information theory, cellular networks; measurable
        surplus outward];
      Up [L3-L4 -- transmission functional, correction
        capacity protected from market signals by AT&T
        insulation, meta-correction present];
      Front [trajectory toward center during peak -- AT&T
        funding insulation removing output pressure].
      Coordinate: right / up / front.
      Note: trajectory reverses to back at 1984 breakup.
        Transmission failure confirms axis was institutionally
        dependent. Post-1984: right of center / mid / back.

    WEIMAR REPUBLIC [full window]:
      Left of center [not extracting but not generating
        cleanly -- consumption-dominant without consistent
        surplus flow toward terminal node; Selection Pressure
        failure];
      Neutral to up [L2 functional, L3 partial -- Stresemann
        period correction attempted];
      Front [Stresemann period -- genuine correction
        initiation, insufficient substrate to hold].
      Coordinate: left of center / neutral to up / front
        [Stresemann window]; left of center / lower-mid /
        back [terminal window].

    CATHOLIC CHURCH [period readings]:
      Early period 33-313 CE: right / up / front.
      Constantine transition 313 CE: shifts toward center
        on Axis 1 [Legitimacy Transfer, Transmission Capture
        installed]; mid capture on Axis 2 [scale transition
        compromises depth]; trajectory becomes unstable.
      Reading confirms: three-axis position is window-
        dependent. Same institution reads differently across
        periods. Period specification mandatory before
        position declared.

  OPERATIONAL PROCEDURES [draft -- requires WIP 18 for
  full scoring]:

    STEP 1: DECLARE PARAMETERS
      Boundary, population, window. Same as standard
      framework reading. Position reading is meaningless
      without declared parameters -- same system reads
      differently at different windows and populations.

    STEP 2: AXIS 1 READING [generation/extraction]
      Run standard net transfer reading. Determine surplus
      flow direction across declared population and window.
      Locate on axis: right / right of center / center /
      left of center / left.

    STEP 3: AXIS 2 READING [coherence depth]
      Identify highest confirmed operational level.
      Behavioral confirmation required -- claimed level
      does not count. Run level tests [T1-T4] in sequence.
      Locate on axis: up / upper-mid / mid / lower-mid / down.

    STEP 4: AXIS 3 READING [trajectory]
      Requires minimum two time windows within reading period.
      Run Axis 1 and Axis 2 readings at both windows.
      Compare positions. Determine direction of movement
      relative to center.
      Single-window readings: declare position only [no
      trajectory]. Flag as snapshot, not trajectory read.

    STEP 5: COMPOSITE OUTPUT
      Format: [A1 position / A2 position / A3 direction]
      Example: [right / up / front] = high generation,
        deep coherence, improving trajectory.
      Example: [left / down / back] = net extraction,
        shallow coherence, degrading. Terminal candidate.
      Example: [right of center / mid / stable] = moderate
        generation, mid-level coherence, holding position.
        Correction maintaining but not improving.
      Add prose reading [v22 mechanism vocabulary] and
        score [WIP 18 when available] for complete output.

  INTEGRATION WITH EXISTING FRAMEWORK:
    CENTER maps directly to CENTER / point-truth [main theory].
      Unreachable; orientation toward it is the measure.
    AXIS 1 maps to GC/SP/CC [C1] + extraction definition.
      Right = C1 generative; left = C1 captured/extractive.
    AXIS 2 maps to level structure [L1-L4].
      Up = L3-L4; down = L1 only; mid = L2.
    AXIS 3 maps to CORRECTION CAPACITY trajectory.
      Front = CC firing and improving position; back = CC
      absent, captured, or insufficient.
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY reads on Axis 3:
      Extraction must stay back continuously; generation
      only has to reach front once and transmit.

  PENDING:
    WIP 18 scoring procedure required to attach numerical
      score to coordinate. Current output: coordinate +
      prose. Target output: coordinate + score + prose.
    Falsification test: case that produces coordinate
      reading inconsistent with confirmed prose reading
      from same case. If coordinate contradicts prose
      reading -- axis definitions require revision.
      Run against full case library when WIP 18 complete.

  Cross-ref: CENTER [point-truth -- geometric center of
    model]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Axis 3
    prediction]; L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION [Axis 2 + 3
    -- transmission tests]; CORRECTION CAPACITY [Axis 3
    primary]; WIP 18 [SCORING PROCEDURE -- required for
    complete output]; [CASE:BELL_LABS]; [CASE:RANNI];
    WEIMAR REPUBLIC; CATHOLIC CHURCH [confirmed readings
    reconstructed from v3].

  PHILOSOPHICAL VOCABULARY [candidate -- v22]:
    Greek/Roman terms map cleanly to geometric states.
    Candidates for formal adoption in geometric model:

    APEIRON [ἄπειρον -- Anaximander]: "the boundless /
      indefinite." Pre-axial substrate with no fixed form
      or limit. Maps to STRUCTURAL COHERENCE [sphere
      geometry] -- generative, no axis, no hole, no
      fixed orientation. Everything circulating; nothing
      held. The generative substrate before axis forms.
      Geometric state: sphere. Axis 2 position: down
      [no level structure yet]. Axis 1: center [neither
      generating toward a population nor extracting --
      cycling without direction].

    PANTA RHEI [πάντα ῥεῖ -- Heraclitus]: "everything
      flows." Full cycle operation, flux without
      solidification. Maps to healthy torus -- ouroboros
      closing, creation and destruction simultaneous,
      circuit not locked. The generative state the
      framework orients toward. Axis 3 position: front
      [trajectory toward center]. Not the center itself
      -- the closest operational state to it.

    TO ON [τὸ ὄν -- Parmenides]: "the being." Fully
      present, fully actual, no potential, no change.
      Maps to TWO failure modes simultaneously:
        [1] CENTER / TRUTH [absolute]: the unreachable
          Parmenidean solid that the flux orients toward.
          The axis threading all levels. Present as
          structural constant; inaccessible from inside
          any cycle. To on as the correct description
          of the truth axis -- fully IS, cannot be
          reached by anything that is still becoming.
        [2] FERTILE LOCK [failure]: fully determined
          transmission. No revision possible. The torus
          transmitting only locked geometry. Parmenidean
          solidity as failure mode -- nothing can change
          because everything IS exactly what it is.
          Extraction's preferred endpoint for methodology.
      Distinction: To on as CENTER is the correct
        orientation target. To on as FERTILE LOCK is
        what happens when a system mistakes its current
        state for the absolute -- stops becoming,
        declares itself arrived.

    PLENUM [Latin -- Parmenidean tradition]: fully filled
      space, no void, no flux. Related to to on but
      spatial rather than ontological. Maps to Axis 2
      [up -- fully coherent at all levels simultaneously]
      as the target state no real system achieves.
      Extraction architectures produce anti-plenum:
      apparent fullness concealing structural voids
      [substrate depleting, CC absent, MI captured].

    SHAPE MODEL VOCABULARY [proposed]:
      Apeiron state [sphere]: pre-axial, generative,
        undirected. No three-axis reading possible.
      Panta rhei state [healthy torus]: full cycle,
        flux, circuit closing. Three-axis reading:
        right / up-to-mid / front [toward center].
      To on trap [locked torus]: Fertile Lock,
        Parmenidean capture. Three-axis reading:
        [varies by axis 1] / [varies by axis 2] /
        STABLE or BACK [not moving toward center --
        the lock IS the position].
      To on correct [center]: unreachable. Orientation
        target only. Three-axis reading: [0,0,0] --
        pure center, never achieved.

    STATUS: candidate vocabulary for WIP 19 formal
      geometry. Requires integration check against
      existing WIP 23 pre-axial categories [apeiron
      maps to Structural Coherence / Nascent; to on
      maps to both CENTER and Fertile Lock -- the
      ambiguity is philosophically load-bearing, not
      a conflict]. Adopt in next geometric model pass.


COLLAPSED / STUB ENTRIES
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  CORRECTION LANDING / INTEGRATION [collapsed into CORRECTION
    CAPACITY]
     Correction Landing and Correction Integration confirmed
     as named properties of CORRECTION CAPACITY, not
     standalone qualities. Fires; Landing = whether firing
     changes target; Integration = whether change holds. All
     three downstream of same mechanism.
     Failure modes retained as sub-properties:
       Correction Bounce [fires, target absorbs without
         change]
       Correction Reversion [change lands, reverts under
         pressure]

  DIRECTIONAL SUSTAINABILITY [stub -- v11; Section Two-B
    speculative]:
    Extension of COHERENCE AS BIOLOGICAL BASELINE.
    Up [True Coherence trajectory]: self-sustaining.
      Circuit closes; substrate regenerates; transmission
      crosses generational boundary.
    Down [extraction trajectory]: self-terminating.
      Substrate consumed; circuit shortens; endpoint is
      substrate exhaustion. Known endpoint confirmed --
      no extraction architecture in case library achieved
      stability at civilizational scale.
    Lateral [Mixed Coherence holding]: decays.
      Not stable indefinitely -- Mixed Coherence requires
      active maintenance against extraction pressure.
      Holding is not ascending. Substrate degrades slowly
      unless actively regenerated.
    Status: speculative extension of confirmed finding.
      Do not use for case scoring. Confirmed component
      [extraction is self-terminating] lives in EXTRACTION
      CYCLE [Section Five]. Speculative component [up is
      self-sustaining in strict sense] requires L4.5
      confirmation -- WIP 14.
    Cross-ref: COHERENCE AS BIOLOGICAL BASELINE [Section

      MIXED COHERENCE [lateral trajectory]; WIP 14
      [L4.5 -- confirms or falsifies the self-sustaining

TOWER ORDER [primary organizing structure]

  FOUNDATION LAYER [enable all levels]:
  GENUINE AXIS             axis must exist before transmitting
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       defines who measurement applies to
  SUBSTRATE REGENERATION   substrate must exist for cycle
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      meta -- holds all levels in shape
  TENSION DISSOLUTION      meta -- TENSION MAINTENANCE
                             opposite

  CONSTANTS [required from L1 upward]:
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      CONSTANT + L1 tension unit
  BOUNDARY INTEGRITY       CONSTANT
  TENSION MAINTENANCE      CONSTANT [meta]

  LEVEL 1 [dynamic -- generative]:
  GENERATIVE CYCLE         TENSION UNIT -- core
  SURPLUS PRODUCTION       TENSION UNIT
  CORRECTION CAPACITY      TENSION UNIT [also constant]
  Tests: T1.1-T1.5 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 2 [dynamic -- epistemic]:
  MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY    TENSION UNIT -- core
  CAUSAL LEGIBILITY        TENSION UNIT
  REFERENCE INTEGRITY      TENSION UNIT
  Tests: T2.1-T2.3 [see tower block above]

  LEVEL 3 [dynamic -- temporal]:
  TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY TENSION UNIT -- core
  SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY       TENSION UNIT
  CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING    TENSION UNIT [also foundation]
  Tests: T3.1-T3.3 [see tower block above]

ANTI-COHERENCE TOWER ATTACK [probability gradient -- not
  necessary sequence]
  L3 attack: Fertile Lock -> corrupt transmission
  L2 attack: Measurement destruction -> surface captured
  L2 attack: Boundary manipulation -> measurement population
    shifted
  L2 attack: Causal orphaning -> chain severed
  L1 attack: Axis imposition -> direction captured
  L1 attack: Correction capture -> fires toward monopolist
  L1 attack: Consumption dominance -> cycle costs exceed
    output
  L1 attack: Cycle collapse -> generative loop fails
  Tower reading: attacks typically start at L3 [cheaper,
  less visible] and cascade downward. L1 attack is most
  catastrophic but most costly to execute. TRANSMISSION
  SELECTIVITY failure runs parallel not sequential. Each
  earlier step reduces resistance cost of later steps -- not
  a required sequence. Any step can fire first [Enron: L2
  without prior L3 failure; Room 101: L1 attacked directly].
  Read as: typical deployment order under resource
  constraints.

[HDR:VERSION_CHANGE_CONVENTION__V9_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
VERSION CHANGE CONVENTION [v9]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

VERSION INCREMENT [vN -> vN+1] when:
  - New quality added to stack [quality stack level change]
  - New section added or sections restructured
  - Category renamed across full document [Structural
    Coherence rename]
  - New formal category added [Intrinsic, Nascent, Seeded
    Nascent]
  - New failure state added to measurement procedure
  - New honest limit category added [T2 Permanent]
  - New formal designation added [Evil/Good, Mixed Coherence]
  - New measurement track added [Track A-E]
  - Weapons index restructured or major weapon formally defined

[HDR:MERGE___REMOVE_LOGIC__EXECUTE_]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
MERGE / REMOVE LOGIC [execute at version change]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------

Run this checklist before finalizing any new version file.

REMOVE:
  - WIP items marked RESOLVED or CLOSED -- delete entirely
  - "deferred to vN" references where N <= current version
  - "Move to Pending" markers -- item should already be there
  - Duplicate section content [same definition in two
    sections]
  - Stale transcript-pending notes where content now confirmed
  - Orphan separator lines [=== or --- with no adjacent
    content]

MERGE:
  - Standalone findings that duplicate Theory section content
  - Psychology parallel + Fiction/Real table [same purpose]
  - Pending items that are fully complete -- remove from
    Pending
  - WIP sub-items that belong under a parent item

COMPRESS:
  - Version convention lists > 10 items -- summarize by
    category
  - Remove RESOLVED items from active list when next version
    is cut
  - Pending list items marked complete in all sub-points --
    remove

FORMAT:
  - Max 2 consecutive blank lines
  - No blank line immediately after === separator
  - No double === separators [run dedup pass]
  - No duplicate subsection headers within 10 lines of each
    other

AUDIT COMMANDS [run before saving]:
  Double separators:  count(===\n===) == 0
  Triple blanks:      count(\n\n\n) == 0
  Stale deferred:     count('deferred to v[N-1 or earlier]')
    == 0
  Orphan RESOLVED:    count('RESOLVED [v') in Active List
    == 0

QUALITY USAGE AUDIT [mandatory on version increment]:
  Run when: any quality added, removed, renumbered, or
  renamed.
  1. MAPPING TABLE: produce old->new quality name table
     before any changes. Archive in WIP Active List under
     version item.
  2. PROOF LABEL SCAN: grep all 'Proof:' and 'Proof for:'
     lines. Verify each quality name matches current schema.
     Dirty range = any Q that changed number or name.
  3. DEFINITION BLOCK SCAN: check internal cross-references
     within quality definitions [e.g. 'Distinct from
     MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY']. Update any reference into
     dirty range.
  4. WEAPONS INDEX SCAN: verify each [HDR:QN_WEAPONS] tag
     matches the 'QN WEAPONS' content label directly below
     it.
  5. STRUCTURAL ORDER TABLE: update to match new
     assignments. This table is authoritative -- resolve
     conflicts against it.
  6. BODY TEXT: do NOT bulk-substitute in body text. Update
     only where meaning breaks. Quality names in context are
     robust; quality names in proof labels are not.
  7. CONFIRM: grep dirty-range quality names in proof labels
     after pass. Count must be zero before version is saved.
  If audit deferred: log as WIP item with mapping table
  inline. Undocumented renumber = document internally
  inconsistent.

FORMAT RULE: all lines <= 108 chars. Separators exempt.


==========================================================================
SECTION THREE -- MECHANISMS
[SEC:MECHANISMS]
==========================================================================
Tools index [correction mechanisms] and weapons index [damage mechanisms]. Low to high damage.

SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [v22 -- applies everywhere]:
  SEVEN-SECTION ARCHITECTURE [v22]:
    Content belongs in the section that matches its TEST
    [see SECTION BOUNDARY PROTECTION in Standing Rules].
    Wrong section = [BOUNDARY VIOLATION] flag. Move next
    version increment.

  GENERAL ORDER PRINCIPLE [applies throughout]:
    Simple to complex. Within any section, division, or block:
    foundational content before derived content; definitions
    before findings; general before specific; confirmed before
    candidate; lower level before higher level; preconditions
    before what they enable.
    SPECIFIC ORDERING [overrides where declared]:
    Some sections have a natural ordering that is not simple-
    to-complex. That ordering is declared in INSERTION POSITION
    RULE and takes precedence within that section:
      Section Three [MECHANISMS]: tools before weapons;
        within weapons: high damage before low damage.
      Section Six [POLITICS IN ACTION]: by scale [individual
        → organizational → institutional → national →
        civilizational].
      Section Seven [SCIENCE]: CONFIRMATION before ILLUSTRATION.
      Cases: by quality level; within level by S-rating.
    If no specific ordering declared: simple to complex.
    New entries slot at the correct position in the ordering
    at insertion time. Do not append to end and reorder later.

  RECOVERY SESSION FLAG [declare at session start]:
    If session involves restoring content from older versions,
    declare [RECOVERY SESSION]. When active:
      MERGE IS SUSPENDED. Flag [MERGE CANDIDATE] only.
      REMOVAL IS SUSPENDED. Flag [RECOVERY -- verify before
        removing]. Never delete during recovery.

  RULE 1 -- PUT SIMILAR CONTENT CLOSE:
    Similar entries belong adjacent in the file regardless
    of whether they merge. Proximity is the first action.
    Same section: slot near related entries [INSERTION
      POSITION RULE applies].
    Different section: cross-reference both directions;
      physical proximity not required across section boundary.
    After any merge: keep merged entry near related entries.
      Content does not drift away from its neighbors after merge.

  RULE 2 -- NO COMPRESS:
    Compression removes content. Content cannot be recovered
    once compressed. Do not compress.
    If an entry seems redundant: cross-reference it, place
      it adjacent, or merge it [if nine tests pass].
    Never reduce to a stub. Never replace content with a pointer.

  RULE 3 -- CROSS-REFERENCE IF SIMILAR BUT NOT SAME:
    Two entries that address the same phenomenon but differ
    in mechanism, quality level, epistemic status, or section:
    cross-reference both directions. Place adjacent where
    possible within section.
    Cross-reference when:
      Different sections [always -- never merge across boundaries].
      Different quality levels or epistemic status.
      Same subject, different mechanism.
      Similar but distinct content -- either would lose something
        in a merge.
    Format: [CROSS-REF: SECTION X -- ENTRY NAME / relationship].
    Both directions. One-line relationship description each.
    Cross-reference is always reversible. Merge is not.

  RULE 4 -- MERGE ONLY IF NINE TESTS PASS [all required]:
    Fail any one test: no merge. Cross-reference instead.

    [T1] SAME SECTION. Never merge across section boundaries.
    [T2] SAME QUALITY LEVEL. Same S-rating, T-level, epistemic
         status. Never merge confirmed with candidate.
    [T3] SAME MECHANISM. Same causal chain, same evidence type,
         same quality target. Same subject is not sufficient.
    [T4] SAME EPISTEMIC STATUS. Both confirmed, both candidate,
         or both speculative. Never mix.
    [T5] SUBSET TEST. One entry is a proper subset of the other.
         OR: both are sequential argument splits forming one
         argument when adjacent. Neither is sufficient alone
         to justify merge -- must combine with T6.
    [T6] ZERO UNIQUE CONTENT. The absorbed entry has no content
         the primary lacks. Zero. Not mostly. If anything would
         be lost: cross-reference instead, place adjacent.
    [T7] ZERO LOAD-BEARING LOSS. Merged entry retains every
         falsification condition, mechanism trace, epistemic
         flag, case reference from both originals.
    [T8] CLEANER OUTPUT. Merged entry is more useful than two
         adjacent entries. Not just shorter -- better.
         If adjacent is the better answer: don't merge.
    [T9] POST-MERGE STUB. Both originals retained as stubs
         adjacent to merged entry until next version audit
         confirms nothing lost. Stub format:
           [MERGED vN into PRIMARY ENTRY NAME]
           [Retained until vN+1 audit. Remove when confirmed.]
         If originals cannot be retained: do not merge.

    ALL NINE PASS: flag [MERGE CANDIDATE -- T1-T9 passed].
    Execute at version change, not mid-session.
    After merge: update all cross-refs pointing to absorbed
      entry. Log in version header.
    MERGE IS SUSPENDED during recovery sessions.

  ADD NEW ENTRY:
    Entry has distinct mechanism, distinct quality target, AND
    no existing entry covers it in its section home.
    Otherwise: add as subsection or extend nearest related entry.
    If unclear: WIP staging until section assignment confirmed.
    Slot near related entries [INSERTION POSITION RULE].

  SECTION-SPECIFIC PLACEMENT:
    SECTION TWO [FINDINGS]: foundational before level-specific
      before variant subtypes before candidates.
    SECTION THREE [MECHANISMS]: tools before weapons. Within
      weapons: high damage before low damage. Similar mechanisms
      adjacent within damage tier.
    SECTION SIX [POLITICS IN ACTION]: each entry declares
      boundary, analyst prior, T3 status. Missing any:
      flag [INCOMPLETE ENTRY -- missing: X].

    CASES: three-level order: category → source → entries.
      Category is primary sort [REAL / WARHAMMER / SCP /
      ANIME / MANGA_MANHWA / GAMES / LITERATURE_FILM].
      Source is secondary sort [same game / show / band
      adjacent within category].
      Entries alphabetical within source; primary case
      before sub-cases regardless of alpha.
      New case: find category → find source cluster →
      insert adjacent. See INSERTION POSITION RULE [Cases]
      for full procedure.

  REMOVE [last resort -- harder than merge]:
    Merge removes one entry. Remove removes both. Higher bar.
    Remove only when ALL of the following:
      [R1] Content is confirmed present elsewhere in full.
          Not summarized. Not cross-referenced. Actually present.
      [R2] Entry has been in the file long enough to have been
          read and verified in at least one session after addition.
          No same-session removal.
      [R3] No unique content: zero falsification conditions,
          mechanism traces, epistemic flags, or case references
          that do not exist in the surviving location.
      [R4] Content is not a sole surviving instance of any
          finding, case, or mechanism from any version.
      [R5] Removal is not during a recovery session.
    Fail any: do not remove. Cross-reference or archive instead.

    CLOSED WIP STUB PROCEDURE:
      When a WIP is closed or promoted: reduce staging entry
        to a stub immediately. Do not leave full content.
      Stub format:
        WIP N: [NAME] [CLOSED / PROMOTED vX]
        [STUB -- VERIFY AND REMOVE vN+1]
        [Canonical in: SECTION X / ENTRY NAME. Cross-refs
          confirmed. No unique content remaining here.
          Remove this stub after next session confirms clean.]
      Next session: verify stub. Run R1-R4 against stub.
        If all pass: remove stub entirely.
        If any fail [unique content found]: restore from stub;
          do not remove.
      Stub is the safety mechanism between closure and removal.
      Do not skip stub phase and remove immediately.

    VERIFICATION WORK GOES IN NEW, NOT OLD [load-bearing]:
      When checking whether a WIP is stub-safe and the check
        is inconclusive [unique content possibly present;
        taxonomy framing possibly load-bearing; promotion
        conditions partially met]: write the verification
        finding as a NEW note. Do not edit the OLD staging
        block in place to add the finding.
      Why: the old staging block is the audit record of what
        existed before the check. Editing it in place to add
        "verify next session" language mixes the original
        content with the verification metadata, making it
        unclear later what was original vs what was added
        during the check. A future session [or future Claude
        instance] reading the block cannot tell whether the
        caution flag was always there or was added after an
        inconclusive check.
      Correct pattern: leave the old staging block completely
        untouched. Add a NEW short entry directly above or
        below it: "[VERIFICATION NOTE vN -- date/session]:
        checked R1-R4 against this entry; inconclusive because
        X; before stubbing, confirm Y." The new note is dated
        to its own session and clearly separated from the
        original content.
      This applies symmetrically to promotion: if a WIP's
        promotion conditions are partially met, write a NEW
        note stating what was confirmed this session, rather
        than rewriting the original problem statement to
        reflect partial progress. The original problem
        statement stays intact until full promotion, at which
        point the whole entry is replaced by the stub.
      Failure mode this corrects: incremental in-place edits
        to WIP staging entries across multiple sessions
        produce a block that is part-original, part-v21-edit,
        part-v22-edit with no record of which session changed
        what. The next session cannot do a clean R1-R4 check
        because it cannot tell what is canonical-original
        content versus accumulated session notes.

    ARCHIVE vs REMOVE:
      If uncertain whether R1-R4 pass: archive, do not remove.
      Archive = move to ARCHIVE section [SEC:ARCHIVE].
      Archive is recoverable. Removal is not.
      Orphan separators [blank === lines] are the one case
        where removal is safe without running R1-R4.

    MERGE IS HALF-REMOVE [same bar applies]:
      The absorbed entry disappears from its location after merge.
      T1-T9 merge tests are the equivalent of R1-R4 for the
      absorbed entry. If T6 [zero unique content] cannot be
      confirmed: do not merge. The absorbed entry has content
      that would be lost. Cross-reference instead.
      After merge: absorbed entry becomes a stub adjacent to
      primary [T9 requirement]. The stub is the safety mechanism.
      Stub removal follows R1-R4 after audit confirms clean.

    WIP CLOSED → reduce active list entry to one-line closed
      note. Full staging entry reduced to stub immediately.
      Stub removed after next session R1-R4 verification.

  AUDIT [run before saving any version]:
    Double separators == 0
    Quad blanks == 0
    [BOUNDARY VIOLATION] flags == 0
    [MERGE CANDIDATE] flags: T1-T9 verified before executing
    [RECOVERY -- verify] flags: all resolved
    [MERGED vN] stubs: audit confirms clean → move to ARCHIVE
    [ARCHIVE CANDIDATE] flags: moved to ARCHIVE section
    [CLOSED vN] WIPs: staging entry reduced to stub
    [STUB -- VERIFY AND REMOVE vN+1]: run R1-R4 each session
      Pass all → remove stub
      Fail any → restore from stub; do not remove

INSERTION POSITION RULE [v22 -- within-section ordering]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  SECTION ONE [FOUNDATION]:
    Foundation preconditions → Level 1 → Level 2 → Level 3
    → Nested Cycle → CENTER/TRUTH/OUROBOROS → EVIL/GOOD.
    New entry: slot at the level where it applies.

  SECTION TWO [FINDINGS]:
    Confirmed [S3+] before candidate before stubs.
    Within confirmed: by level [L1 before L2 before L3 before L4].
    Within level: general finding before named subtype.
    New confirmed: after last confirmed at same or lower level.
    New candidate: after all confirmed, by evidence proximity.

  SECTION THREE [MECHANISMS]:
    Tools before weapons. Tools alphabetical within type.
    Weapons by damage tier [EXTREME → HIGH → MID-HIGH → MID → LOW].
    Within tier: alphabetical.
    New tool: alphabetical in tools block.
    New weapon: correct damage tier.

  SECTION FOUR [PROCEDURE]:
    Epistemic rules → scoring → reading → output format.
    New entry: at the procedural step it belongs to.

  SECTION FIVE [POLITICAL THEORY]:
    Definitions → rubrics → framework prior → structural
    findings [general before specific] → edge cases.

  SECTION SIX [POLITICS IN ACTION]:
    By scale [individual → organizational → institutional
    → national → civilizational].
    Within scale: by recency [older first; newer appended].

  SECTION SEVEN [SCIENCE]:
    CONFIRMATION before ILLUSTRATION.
    Within type: alphabetical by subject.

  CASES:
    THREE-LEVEL ORDER: category → source → entries.

    LEVEL 1 -- CATEGORY [primary sort]:
      Cases are grouped by source category. Category order:
        REAL [real-world -- people, events, institutions,
          science, music, history]
        WARHAMMER [40K and Fantasy -- large enough for own
          category]
        SCP [SCP Foundation wiki fiction]
        ANIME [Japanese animation]
        MANGA_MANHWA [manga and manhwa]
        GAMES [video games -- all platforms and genres]
        LITERATURE_FILM [books, film, television]
      New categories added when a source cluster reaches
        ~5 or more cases and has a clear distinct identity.
      CATEGORY header format:
        [CATEGORY:NAME]
        Brief description
      New case: identify correct category first.
        If no clear category: add to nearest existing
        category and flag [CATEGORY UNVERIFIED].

    LEVEL 2 -- SOURCE [secondary sort within category]:
      [STATUS v22: GAMES category source-grouped this session --
        115 true game cases organized into 16 source groups
        [BIOSHOCK, CLAIR_OBSCUR, DARK_SOULS, DEUS_EX, DISCO_ELYSIUM,
        ELDEN_RING, ELDER_SCROLLS, FALLOUT_NEW_VEGAS, FINAL_FANTASY_6,
        FINAL_FANTASY_7, FINAL_FANTASY_10, FINAL_FANTASY_14,
        LEGACY_OF_KAIN, NIER, SOMA, XENOBLADE]
        with [SOURCE:NAME] headers; single-case and two-case sources
        [Armoured Core, Devil May Cry, Gnosia, etc.] sit adjacent to
        their nearest thematic neighbor without a header per the
        3+ threshold. 16 miscategorized cases also corrected during
        this pass: Wheel of Time cluster [WHEEL_OF_TIME, ISHAMAEL,
        RAND_DRAGONMOUNT, AIEL_REVELATION], 1984 cluster
        [WINSTON_SMITH, JULIA_1984, OBRIEN, OCEANIA_PARTY], and
        standalone film/TV cases [2001_FILM, HAL_9000, WESTWORLD,
        MR_ROBOT, DOCTOR_MANHATTAN, OZYMANDIAS, HYDRA, PALPATINE]
        moved from GAMES to LITERATURE_FILM; THE_PROTOMEN [music]
        moved from GAMES to REAL. CORRECTION [same session]: WORLD
        AFTER THE FALL [5 cases: AWAKENING_SYSTEM, BIG_BROTHER_WATF,
        DREAM_SEEDS, MULAK, TOWERS_WATF] is a webtoon/manhwa source,
        not a video game -- moved from GAMES to MANGA_MANHWA as
        [SOURCE:WORLD_AFTER_THE_FALL]. MANGA_MANHWA now 16 cases,
        GAMES now 115. REAL, WARHAMMER, ANIME, MANGA_MANHWA,
        LITERATURE_FILM source-grouping still pending -- smaller
        categories, lower priority, do next when time allows.]
      Within each category, cases from the same source
      [same game, same show, same author, same band]
      are grouped together in close proximity.
      Source header format [when 3+ cases from one source]:
        [SOURCE:NAME]
      Cases from same source should be adjacent even when
      the source header is absent. PROXIMITY RULE applies:
      put similar content close. Same source = similar.
      Source tagging is a dedicated pass -- do not
      reorder single cases mid-session to achieve source
      proximity. Flag [SOURCE UNVERIFIED] if source is
      unclear.

    LEVEL 3 -- ENTRIES [within source]:
      Within each source group: alphabetical by case name.
      Exception: main case [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME] before
        sub-cases [CASE:ISHAMAEL, CASE:RAND_DRAGONMOUNT]
        regardless of alphabetical order.
      Small entries [sub-cases with cross-refs to a
        primary case] slot immediately after primary case.

    ADDING A NEW CASE:
      [1] Identify category [REAL / WARHAMMER / SCP /
          ANIME / MANGA_MANHWA / GAMES / LITERATURE_FILM].
      [2] Identify source [what game / show / band / event].
      [3] Find existing cases from same source. Insert
          adjacent. For GAMES specifically: check if a
          [SOURCE:NAME] header already exists for that game
          [16 exist as of v22] and insert within it
          alphabetically; if the source has fewer than 3
          cases and no header, insert near other cases from
          that source without creating a header until the
          3-case threshold is reached.
      [4] If first case from that source: insert at end
          of correct category section. Source cluster
          will form naturally as more cases are added.
      [5] If source unclear: insert at end of category
          with [SOURCE UNVERIFIED] flag.
      [6] Update BY_ALPHABET and BY_SOURCE indexes.

    INDEX MAINTENANCE:
      BY_QUALITY [existing]: update when new case added.
      BY_ALPHABET [existing]: add new case in alpha order.
      BY_SOURCE [pending -- dedicated session]: standalone
        lookup index not yet built; the [SOURCE:NAME] headers
        now embedded in GAMES category [v22] serve the same
        navigational purpose in the meantime. Build standalone
        BY_SOURCE index once remaining categories [REAL,
        WARHAMMER, ANIME, MANGA_MANHWA, LITERATURE_FILM] are
        also source-grouped.

  GENERAL: uncertain slot → end of correct section with
    [POSITION UNVERIFIED] flag. Do not disrupt existing order.

NAME COMPRESSION POLICY:
  Abbreviations in use [used throughout for readability]:
    GA  = GENUINE AXIS
    BI  = BOUNDARY INTEGRITY
    SR  = SUBSTRATE REGENERATION
    TM  = TENSION MAINTENANCE
    TD  = TENSION DISSOLUTION
    GC  = GENERATIVE CYCLE
    SP  = SURPLUS PRODUCTION
    CC  = CORRECTION CAPACITY
    SD  = SURPLUS DISTRIBUTION
    MI  = MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY
    CL  = CAUSAL LEGIBILITY
    RI  = REFERENCE INTEGRITY
    TS  = TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
    SA  = SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
    CAG = CAUSAL AXIS GROUNDING
    FC  = FORMATION CAPTURE
    MSC = MEASUREMENT SURFACE CONTROL
    ECC = EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION
    ICD = IDLE CYCLE DEPRIVATION
    ECD = ENGINEERED CAUSAL DIFFUSION
    CT  = CORRECTION THEATRE

WEAPON STACK DETECTION PROTOCOL [main theory -- v17,
  promoted from Section Two]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Promoted from Section Two: stacks are the primary
  deployment pattern in confirmed political and institutional
  cases. Single-weapon analysis misses load-bearing
  interaction effects. Stack detection changes both reading
  and intervention priority.

  5-STEP PROCEDURE:
    1. List all active weapons from case reading.
    2. Check for confirmed stack patterns [below].
    3. Identify interaction effects -- co-deployed weapons
       amplify each other's damage and reduce counter cost.
    4. Identify highest-damage weapon in stack [weapons index
       damage range] -- this is intervention priority, not the
       most visible weapon.
    5. Confirm stack is load-bearing: remove one weapon from
       the model and check if the reading changes. If yes --
       it's a stack. If no -- coincidental co-deployment.

  CONFIRMED STACKS [interaction effects]:
    C2+C1 PRESSURE:
      MI degradation makes C1 invisible. System generates
      at full rate; surface cannot read depletion.
      Extraction appears healthy until substrate hits floor.
      Intervention on C2 alone triggers Diagnostic Collapse
      if C1 already terminal. Highest-risk stack: C2
      restoration is the standard intervention and it fires
      correctly into terminal C1. Pre-intervention: C1
      substrate audit mandatory. Do not open C2 on unaudited
      C1 when this stack is active.

    FC+ECC:
      Formation Capture installs axis; Exit Cost Construction
      makes revision structurally unavailable. FC alone =
      captured axis, revision possible at cost. ECC alone =
      high revision cost, axis still examinable. Stack =
      revision both psychologically blocked and structurally
      unavailable. Breaks the standard post-formation
      revision path [requires C1 starved + enabling moment
      + L3.5 matched contact -- ECC eliminates enabling
      moment by making exit too costly to reach substrate
      depletion].

    MSC+CT:
      Measurement Surface Control makes deviation unreadable;
      Correction Theatre makes invisible non-correction look
      like active correction. Stack = system appears to
      correct while surface is captured. External observer
      sees correction activity; no target changes. Standard
      tell: Correction Theatre firing at high volume
      alongside MSC = stack confirmed. Counter: measure
      outputs not correction events. If correction fires
      frequently but targets never change, MSC+CT stack is
      operating.

    ICD+FC:
      Idle Cycle Deprivation prevents axis formation;
      Formation Capture fills the gap with external content.
      ICD alone = axis doesn't form, gap present. FC alone
      = external content available, idle cycles required to
      install it. Stack = ICD creates the vacuum; FC fills
      it before internal generation can fire. No deployer
      coordination required -- structural co-deployment
      under attention architecture. Most common spontaneous
      stack in current population-scale conditions [social
      media + educational Formation Capture].

    CULT STACK [named six-phase sequential stack -- v22]:
      The complete high-control group deployment pattern.
      Six phases in load-bearing sequence -- each phase
      removes one counter-capacity before the next deploys.
      Psychological confirmation: Lifton [thought reform],
      Singer [cult psychology], Herman [trauma bonding],
      Cialdini [influence and commitment]. Independent
      convergence across four researchers on same sequence.

      PHASE 1 -- LOVE BOMBING:
        Relational substrate constructed at high density
        before target knows what the relationship is.
        Function: installs substrate dependency; lowers
        skepticism; makes the group feel like the highest-
        value relational environment available.
        Counter-capacity removed: pattern recognition
        [target is in peak positive affect; skepticism
        reads as ingratitude].

      PHASE 2 -- FORMATION CAPTURE:
        Axis installation while substrate is high and
        counter-capacity is down. Love Bombing provides
        the conditions FC requires: idle cycles filled
        with group content; emotional salience high;
        external reference comparison actively suppressed
        by positive affect.
        Counter-capacity removed: examined axis [new axis
        installs as identity before examination can fire].

      PHASE 3 -- ISOLATION CONSTRUCTION:
        Alternative substrate sources removed. Outside
        relationships reframed as threats, low-quality,
        or spiritually/ideologically contaminated. Exit
        cost begins rising as alternative substrate
        disappears.
        Counter-capacity removed: external relational
        substrate [the resource that would enable exit
        and provide alternative axis contact].

      PHASE 4 -- MEASUREMENT SURFACE CONTROL:
        External information sources captured or blocked.
        Group is the only valid source of truth about
        the group. Independent verification structurally
        unavailable from inside boundary.
        Counter-capacity removed: external reference
        standard [the tool required to counter MSC is
        now inaccessible from inside].

      PHASE 5 -- IDLE CYCLE DEPRIVATION:
        Unstructured processing time eliminated. Schedule
        filled with group activities, rituals, work,
        study. No idle cycles available to examine the
        installed axis or process doubts.
        Counter-capacity removed: internal generation
        capacity [the idle cycle requirement for axis
        self-examination cannot fire].

      PHASE 6 -- EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION:
        Housing, income, identity, community, family
        relationships -- all locked to group membership.
        Exit = total substrate loss. CC present [target
        may know something is wrong] but behaviorally
        blocked [exit costs too high to act on].
        Counter-capacity removed: behavioral CC [the
        final correction mechanism is present but
        structurally inaccessible].

      WHY SEQUENCE IS LOAD-BEARING:
        Each phase uses the counter-capacity removed by
        the prior phase. FC requires the substrate
        dependency LB constructed. Isolation requires
        the axis FC installed [makes outside contact
        feel threatening]. MSC requires Isolation
        [removes the alternative surfaces Isolation
        cut off]. ICD requires MSC [group schedule fills
        cycles that would otherwise generate doubt].
        ECC requires all prior phases [exit cost is
        only total because the prior phases eliminated
        all alternative substrates simultaneously].
        Reorder the phases and the stack partially fails.
        Phase 1 must precede Phase 2; Phase 3 must
        precede Phase 4. The sequence is the weapon.

      INTERACTION EFFECTS [amplification]:
        FC + MSC: axis reads group content as truth;
          group content IS the measurement surface.
          Circular confirmation. Each loop increases
          axis capture depth.
        ICD + FC: no idle cycles to examine installed
          axis; installed axis fills the space that
          examination would require.
        ECC + Isolation: exit costs total AND
          alternative substrate absent. Not additive
          -- multiplicative. ECC without Isolation:
          exit costs high but outside support possible.
          Isolation without ECC: outside contact
          possible but exit costs high. Stack: outside
          support unavailable AND exit costs total.

      COUNTER-STACK [phase-matched -- pre-emptive]:
        Counter must fire before corresponding phase
        completes. Post-completion counters require
        the tool one level harder.

        PRE-PHASE 1 [before Love Bombing completes]:
          TOOL: CYCLE NAMING [pattern recognition].
          Identify Love Bombing pattern before substrate
          dependency installs. Tell: intensity of positive
          attention disproportionate to relationship
          depth; group presents as uniquely able to
          meet all needs; rapid intimacy construction.
          Window: before substrate dependency makes
          skepticism feel like loss.

        PRE-PHASE 2 [before FC completes]:
          TOOL: EXTERNAL AXIS CONTACT [L3.5 matched].
          Maintain contact with someone outside the
          group who shares the primary axis. Not
          argument -- contact on matched axis. The
          external axis contact provides comparison
          that slows FC installation.
          Window: before new axis installs as identity.
          After: CAG + MI stack [post-event FC counter]
          -- higher cost, partial result.

        PRE-PHASE 3 [before Isolation completes]:
          TOOL: ALTERNATIVE SUBSTRATE MAINTENANCE.
          Keep outside relationships active. Not as
          backup -- as primary. Group relationship is
          one substrate source; outside relationships
          remain load-bearing. Isolation cannot
          complete if outside substrate stays load-
          bearing.
          Window: before outside contact is reframed
          as threat. After: rebuilding outside substrate
          requires exit, which Phase 6 blocks.

        PRE-PHASE 4 [before MSC completes]:
          TOOL: EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD.
          Maintain one independent information source
          about the group that the group does not
          control. Can be a person, institution, or
          documented prior belief. The source must
          pre-date group membership.
          Window: before group becomes sole valid
          source. After: external reference must be
          smuggled in through cracks in the boundary
          -- possible but high-cost.

        PRE-PHASE 5 [before ICD completes]:
          TOOL: PROTECTED IDLE CYCLES.
          Maintain unstructured time that is not
          filled with group content. Journaling,
          walking, unstructured solitude. The content
          of idle cycles is less important than their
          existence. Internal generation requires them.
          Window: before schedule fills completely.
          After: environmental redesign required --
          structural not individual intervention.

        PRE-PHASE 6 [before ECC completes]:
          TOOL: EXIT RESOURCE CONSTRUCTION.
          Build one exit resource that is not group-
          controlled before exit costs lock. Financial
          resource, outside housing contact, documented
          skill. Does not require planning to leave --
          requires that leaving is structurally possible.
          Window: before all substrate locked to
          membership. After: external intervention only
          [exit programs, family/friend outside support,
          structured exit support].

        POST-FULL-STACK [all six phases complete]:
          Counter requires dismantling all six phases
          simultaneously -- which is why cult exit is
          structurally difficult even when the target
          knows they are in a cult. Knowing does not
          produce exit when ECC is complete. The
          target needs:
            [1] External contact maintained or rebuilt
            [2] Substitute substrate for everything
              the group controls
            [3] Safe exit pathway [housing, income,
              community outside group]
            [4] Post-exit axis reconstruction [the
              installed axis continues operating after
              exit; CAG + MI stack required]
            [5] ICD recovery [idle cycle restoration;
              environmental redesign]
          All five simultaneously. External support
          organization is the only reliable delivery
          mechanism at full stack deployment.

        POST-CULT EPISTEMIC PATTERN [named -- v22;
        analyst primary case]:
          Common adaptation pattern in post-cult
          population. Not damage -- structurally
          correct response to formation under capture
          pressure. Five components, often arrived
          at independently without framework vocabulary:

          [1] EMOTIONAL REDUCTION:
            Reduce authority of emotional signal over
            conclusions. Not eliminate [that destroys
            somatic axis]; reduce weight. The cult
            deployed emotional channel as primary
            installation mechanism [Love Bombing =
            peak positive affect; FC installs through
            emotional salience]. Correct counter:
            emotional signal is data, not instruction.
            Somatic data still arrives; does not
            automatically determine output.

          [2] LIMITED TRUST IN EMOTIONS:
            Emotional reads require external
            verification before being acted on.
            Not because emotions are wrong -- because
            the verification layer was compromised
            during formation. The instrument that
            would verify "this feeling is genuine"
            is the same instrument that was under
            capture pressure. External anchor required.

          [3] AXIS OF SOME KIND OF TRUTH:
            Genuine axis forming under capture
            pressure as alternative orientation.
            Pre-verbal, pre-framework. The axis
            preceded its own vocabulary. "Some kind
            of truth" is the correct epistemic framing
            for pressure-formed GA that hasn't yet
            named itself -- operating correctly before
            it was identified.

          [4] SOMATIC USE BEFORE UNDERSTANDING IT:
            Pre-theoretical instrument registration.
            Somatic channel functioning as CC
            mechanism before framework vocabulary
            existed to name it. LOOK_OUTSIDE pattern:
            pre-theoretical registration producing
            framework findings before the framework.
            The instrument was reading correctly;
            the analyst didn't yet have words for
            what it was doing.

          [5] TEST IDEAS AGAINST REALITY:
            AXIS GROUNDING [Track C] arrived at
            independently. External behavioral record
            as verification surface. The instrument
            discovered the correct counter to its
            own verification layer compromise without
            being told what the counter was. This
            is the genuine axis operating correctly
            under adverse conditions -- producing
            the right tool before the tool was named.

          COST OF THIS PATTERN:
            Irrational behavior filtered out of
            analysis along with installed emotional
            content. The rational-over-emotional
            filter that correctly countered FC
            installation also filtered genuine
            somatic and intuitive data. Over-
            filtering. The resolution: irrational
            is logic + emotional multiplier =
            predictable [see IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED
            LOGIC]. Once that read is available,
            the filtered category becomes readable
            without restoring the cult's emotional
            installation channel.

          OUTCOME:
            Genuine axis intact. Verification layer
            compromised; external grounding required.
            Some installed content still running;
            ongoing CAG + MI work to distinguish.
            This is the expected output of a
            pressure-formed GA that exited a high-
            control environment and adapted correctly.
            Not damaged -- differently calibrated.
            The calibration is appropriate to the
            formation history.

          Cross-ref: PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS
            [the formation pattern]; CAG + MI
            CORRECTION STACK [the ongoing work];
            IRRATIONAL AS WEIGHTED LOGIC [the
            resolution to the over-filtering cost];
            AXIS GROUNDING [Track C] [the tool
            arrived at independently]; ANALYST AXIS
            DECLARED PRIOR [this pattern as the
            analyst's declared formation context].

      PARTIAL STACKS [same sequence, fewer phases]:
        Two-phase [LB + FC only]: high-control
          relationship without institutional structure.
          Standard abusive relationship pattern.
          Counter: external axis contact + alternative
          substrate. More accessible than full stack
          counter because Phases 3-6 not yet deployed.
        Four-phase [through MSC]: high-control group
          without full economic capture. Exit possible
          but psychologically very costly. Counter:
          external reference standard + alternative
          substrate [both required].
        Full six-phase: named cult deployment pattern.
          External intervention required.

      Psychological confirmation [S4]:
        E4 [real-world multi-domain -- clinical,
          sociological, survivor testimony, experimental];
        M4 [mechanism confirmed across independent
          research traditions: Lifton's eight criteria,
          Singer's six conditions, BITE model [Steven
          Hassan], Herman's trauma bonding -- four
          independent frameworks arriving at same
          six-phase sequence];
        F3 [falsification: high-control group producing
          no axis capture, maintained external reference,
          or low exit costs -- would falsify stack
          as named pattern; not observed].
        S4: highest rating in index. Cult stack is
          the most thoroughly confirmed weapon stack
          in the framework.

      Cross-ref: FORMATION CAPTURE [Phase 2 --
        primary axis weapon]; LOVE BOMBING THEN
        WITHDRAWAL [Phase 1; withdrawal fires when
        compliance fails]; ISOLATION CONSTRUCTION
        [Phase 3]; MSC [Phase 4]; ICD [Phase 5];
        ECC [Phase 6]; FC+ECC STACK [two-phase
        subset]; CAG + MI CORRECTION STACK [tools
        -- post-event FC counter required after
        exit]; ALTERNATIVE SUBSTRATE CONSTRUCTION
        [tools -- counter to Phases 3 and 6];
        EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD [tools --
        counter to Phase 4]; ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN
        [tools -- counter to Phase 5]; RELATIONAL
        STATES [cooperation → extraction transition
        -- cult entry is cooperation reading that
        converts to extraction after Phase 2].

  INTERVENTION PRIORITY RULE:
    In a confirmed stack, always counter the highest-cost-
    asymmetry extraction mechanism first, not the most visible extraction mechanism.
    The most visible extraction mechanism is usually deployed to draw
    counter-attention away from the load-bearing extraction mechanism.
    Examples:
      C2+C1: C2 capture is visible; C1 depletion is not.
        Counter C1 depletion first; then restore C2.
      FC+ECC: ECC is visible [exit is blocked]; FC is not
        [axis capture is invisible from inside]. Reduce ECC
        to enable enabling moment; FC revision can then
        fire.
      MSC+CT: CT is visible; MSC is not [surface appears
        intact]. Identify independent MI surface before
        attacking CT -- CT without independent MI gives
        the extraction actor a new surface to capture.
      ICD+FC: ICD is structural [attention environment];
        FC is content [what fills the gap]. Restore idle
        cycles first; FC content loses delivery mechanism.

  CROSS-REFERENCE: case library political entries apply
    stack detection at Step 8 of Political Diagnostic
    Protocol. Stack findings feed back to intervention
    priority.

CASE STRENGTH RATING [v17 -- cross-library standard]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Every case carries a composite strength rating [S1-S4]
  derived from three axes. Rating is independent of T3
  [analyst prior] -- T3 applies to political cases only.
  S-rating applies to all case types including fictional.

  AXIS 1 -- EVIDENCE TYPE [E]:
    E1: single fictional/illustrative source [structural
      illustration only; no independent confirmation
      possible by definition]
    E2: fictional + framework-consistent cross-reference
      [multiple fictional sources confirming same mechanism;
      still no real-world anchor]
    E3: real-world single domain [historical or empirical,
      one evidence type or one domain]
    E4: real-world multi-domain OR cross-domain independent
      confirmation [two or more independent evidence types
      or domains arriving at same finding without shared
      assumption]

  AXIS 2 -- MECHANISM CONFIRMATION [M]:
    M1: mechanism inferred from outcome only [no pathway
      described]
    M2: mechanism described + plausible pathway [internally
      consistent but no independent confirmation]
    M3: mechanism confirmed by independent parallel
      [psychology research / biological confirmation /
      historical analog in different domain]
    M4: mechanism confirmed by controlled comparison or
      near-control condition [same system, same conditions,
      one variable changed; or paired comparison with
      matched structure]

  AXIS 3 -- FALSIFIABILITY [F]:
    F1: no falsification criteria stated or structurally
      possible
    F2: falsification criteria stated, prediction made,
      not yet tested
    F3: falsification criteria stated, tested against one
      independent case, not falsified
    F4: falsification criteria stated, tested across
      multiple independent conditions in different domains,
      not falsified

  COMPOSITE [S-RATING]:
    S1 [ILLUSTRATION]: E1-2, any M, F1.
      Structural illustration -- valid for concept clarity
      and case presentation. Not confirmation. Do not cite
      as evidence for framework claims. Fictional cases
      are S1 unless cross-confirmed by E3+ finding.
    S2 [WEAK THEORY]: mixed scores with at least one axis
      at E3 or M3. Suggestive, not confirmed. One strong
      falsification would retire the finding to historical
      note. Use as directional indicator only.
    S3 [CONFIRMED]: E3+ AND M3+ AND F2+.
      Independent real-world evidence plus mechanism
      pathway plus stated falsification. Framework standard
      for a confirmed finding. Promote provisional findings
      to S3 when all three axes reached.
    S4 [STRONG]: E4 AND M4 AND F3+.
      Multi-domain independent confirmation plus controlled
      comparison plus tested falsification. Highest library
      confidence. Use as anchor for derived claims.

  FICTIONAL CASE CEILING:
    Fictional cases cap at S2 on E axis [E2 maximum].
    They cannot reach S3 without a real-world anchor
    confirming the same mechanism independently.
    Fictional cases are S1 [illustration] until a real-
    world parallel is found, then S2 [weak theory
    contribution to the cross-domain finding].
    A fictional case that is the sole proof of a framework
    claim = that claim is S1. Add E3+ evidence to promote.

  CURRENT HIGH-CONFIDENCE CASES [S4]:
    DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE: E4 [Glasnost + Libya/Tunisia
      controlled comparison, two independent real-world
      domains], M4 [mechanism confirmed by differential --
      same C2 opening, different C1 states, different
      outcomes], F3 [falsification tested against two cases
      + controlled comparison]. S4.
    L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION: E4 [Cistercian + Jesuit
      [two independent institutional domains] + Linux
      kernel [fast-cycle third domain]], M3 [mechanism
      confirmed by Protestant Reformation failure and Bell
      Labs partial failure], F3 [tested across three active
      cases, not falsified]. S4.

  S3 CONFIRMED FINDINGS [borderline S4]:
    C1 STARVED RECOVERY REQUIRES L3.5 CONTACT: E3 [five
      cases -- two real-world, three fictional; real-world
      anchor present], M3 [mechanism described + biological
      parallel confirmed], F2 [stated, no counterexample].
      S3. Promote to S4 when: independent institutional
      case with near-control condition confirmed.
    AXIS-MATCHING REQUIREMENT: E3 [five individual cases
      + China 1978 split], M4 [China 1978 split-axis
      differential = controlled comparison], F2 [stated,
      no falsification]. S3 borderline S4; promote when
      second split-axis institutional case confirmed.

  APPLICATION RULE:
    Assign E/M/F scores; derive S from composite. S-rating
    lives in case header alongside T3 where applicable.
    Provisional main theory findings require minimum S2 to
    remain in main theory. S1 entries move to Illustration
    Annex or annotated [ILLUSTRATION ONLY]. Any claim
    citing only S1 cases as support is flagged [STRUCTURAL
    READING -- EVIDENCE PENDING].

QUALITY ADDITION / MERGE LOGIC [v14 -- applies to quality stack]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Applies specifically to adding or removing named qualities from
  the quality stack. Distinct from SECTION AND MERGE LOGIC [which
  governs section content] -- this governs the quality stack itself.

  TOWER CODEPENDENCE TEST [apply first]:
    Before adding, identify where the candidate sits in the tower:
      FOUNDATION [GA / BI / SR / TM]: preconditions enabling all
        levels. Add here only if quality enables tower formation.
      LEVEL 1 [generative]: GC / SP / CC and extensions.
      LEVEL 2 [epistemic]: MI / CL / RI and foundations.
      LEVEL 3 [temporal]: TS / SA / CAG and foundations.
      CROSS-LEVEL: quality active at multiple levels simultaneously.
    If candidate cannot be placed: route to alternatives below.

  FOUR ADDITION TESTS [pass all four or route to alternatives]:
    [1] TOWER PLACEMENT: identify which level and role [pair /
        third point / foundation / support]. If no clear placement,
        route to named failure mode.
    [2] TENSION UNIT: can this quality stand alone and produce
        meaningful reading? If yes, add solo. If only meaningful
        in tension with others, identify the tension unit [3
        qualities] first. Incomplete tension units held until all
        three identified.
    [3] GAP CHECK: does an existing quality + modifier already
        cover this mechanism? If >50% covered, collapse into that
        quality as named failure mode or weapon. If genuinely new,
        add.
    [4] PRACTICAL TEST: measurable from Track C without Track B?
        If not, flag T3 candidate only.

  MERGE LOGIC [when to collapse into existing quality]:
    - Mechanism is a named sub-property of existing quality.
    - Middle zone property of a tension unit level.
    - Weapon variant with no generative counterpart.
    - Downstream of existing quality [fires after, not alongside].

  KEEP AS STANDALONE [when not to collapse]:
    - Distinct tower placement from nearest quality.
    - Weapon exists specifically targeting this quality and not
      the nearest existing one.
    - Biological or case confirmation exists independently.

  ROUTE MAP [candidate fails tests]:
    → Named failure mode of nearest quality.
    → Weapon variant in weapons index.
    → Middle zone property of existing level pair.
    → T3 structural limit [unmeasurable].
    → Candidate held for tower placement identification.

CASE LIBRARY ADDITION LOGIC [v13 -- permanent filter]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Apply before adding any case entry. Purpose: keep library
  instrument-quality high. Every weak entry raises MI burden
  without increasing proof coverage. Quantity is not quality.

  ENTRY FILTER [apply first]:
    Case must be STRANGE -- if it confirms what the framework
    already knows without adding texture, do not add. Familiar
    mechanism, familiar scale, no surprises = no entry.
    Test: does this case add anything the library cannot
    already read from existing entries?

  STANDARD ENTRY [pass any one]:
    Two or more qualities active and distinguishable.
    Multiple weapons active simultaneously [weapon stack cases].
    Unusual scale, boundary, or domain for a known mechanism.

  PRIMARY CASE [stricter -- pass one]:
    Sole proof of type [unique mechanism or failure state].
    Single quality but highest-clarity or most-unusual scale --
    only if it is the cleanest proof of that quality in library.
    Replace existing if new case is cleaner. Do not add if
    existing is equivalent or stronger.

  SIMILAR MECHANISM to existing entry:
    Compress and cross-reference. Do not add as standalone.

  REJECTION THRESHOLD:
    If entry would not survive any of the above tests: do not
    add. Library maintenance cost scales with size. Every
    redundant entry reduces reading speed without adding proof
    coverage.

  S-RATING REQUIREMENT:
    All entries require E/M/F scores and composite S-rating
    [see CASE STRENGTH RATING above] before finalization.
    Unrated entries are provisional and flagged [S-RATING
    PENDING].

GIFT INDEX [v15 -- positive transfer mechanisms]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Inverse of weapons. Increase qualities in recipient without
  depleting them in source. Requires genuine SP in giver --
  redistributed extracted surplus does not qualify.

  FORMAL DEFINITION:
    A gift gives coherence. It increases quality capacity in the
    recipient while leaving source qualities intact or increased.
    Source not depleted; recipient more coherent after transfer.
    A weapon takes coherence away. It reduces quality capacity in
    the target while the deploying system extracts or maintains at
    the target's expense. Target less coherent after contact.
    Distinguished by direction of quality-effect: gifts flow outward
    from surplus; weapons extract inward from target. Same mechanism
    can be either depending on direction and intent.
  Neuroscience basis: prosociality hardwired in mammals [oxytocin
    dual pathway confirmed]; cooperation emerges as evolutionary
    emergent property from recognition + memory alone. Gift
    architecture is biological baseline, not cultural construction.
    Weapon deployment requires overriding baseline -- additional
    mechanism always required to suppress default cooperation.

  STRUCTURAL CONDITIONS [all required]:
    Source has genuine SP [output exceeds cost of transfer].
    Recipient quality increases without corresponding source depletion.
    Transfer not contingent on recipient dependency [else Dependency
      Construction disguised as gift].
    Correctable by recipient [else Fertile Lock disguised as gift].

  FAILURE MODES [gift degrading into weapon]:
    Surplus absent → redistribution weapon [extracted surplus
      redistributed; source depleted elsewhere].
    Dependency created → GC weapon [Dependency Construction].
    Correction blocked → TS weapon [Fertile Lock].
    Axis transmitted not method → GA weapon [Formation Capture].

  GIFT TYPES [coherence type determines gift structure]:
    SCIENCE type: method released; recipients can extend and falsify.
      Primary: Bell Labs [1925-1984], Berners-Lee/WWW [1991].
    RELATIONAL type: connection infrastructure released; surplus
      grows with contribution. Primary: Linux kernel, Wikipedia.
    ANTI-CAPTURE type: legal structure transmitted preserving SA.
      Primary: GNU/GPL, Creative Commons, RFC/IETF.
    INTRINSIC LOOSE type: method transmitted without institutional
      capture mechanism. Primary: Quaker network, Darwin, Spinoza.
    MATH subtype: proof transmits correctable method; falsification
      non-negotiable. Primary: Euler, Grothendieck, Scholze/Clausen.

  GIFT VS REDISTRIBUTION [critical distinction]:
    True gift: surplus generated, not extracted. Source capacity
      intact or growing. Recipient capacity increases.
    Redistribution: surplus extracted elsewhere, moved here. Source
      somewhere depleted. Net quality unchanged or negative.
    Redistribution disguised as gift: political mechanism claiming
      gift architecture while running extraction + redistribution.
      Tell: trace the source. If SP is negative at origin, it is
      redistribution not gift regardless of stated intent.

  Cross-ref: WEAPONS INDEX [below -- inverse]; GIFT QUALITY
    MINIMUM STACK [Section Two -- five conditions required];
    ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed -- why extraction
    loses long; asymmetry with gift architecture]; CASE LIBRARY
    [gift cases: Bell Labs, GNU/GPL, Wikipedia, Euler, etc.].

TOOLS INDEX [v22]
==========================================================================
Correction-oriented mechanisms. Deployer-neutral -- tools can be used
by any system with genuine axis and correction intent.

TWO-DIMENSION TAG FORMAT [on each entry]:
  Cost: LOW / MED / HIGH [resource + coordination cost to deploy]
  Effectiveness vs: [weapon name] at [LOW/MED/HIGH effectiveness]

EFFECTIVENESS NOTE: HIGH = primary counter [weapon substantially
  reduced or neutralized]. MED = partial counter [weapon impact
  reduced; structural change also required]. LOW = useful but
  insufficient alone.

PRE-EMPTIVE vs REACTIVE:
  PRE-EMPTIVE tools must deploy before weapon completes:
    External Reference Standard; Alternative Substrate Construction;
    Environmental Redesign.
  REACTIVE tools deploy after weapon is running:
    Axis Grounding; CAG + MI Correction Stack; Output Verification;
    Boundary Declaration; Causal Reconstruction; Instrument Confidence.

FRAMEWORK QUALITY STACK [primary tools -- always available]:
  GC [GENERATIVE CYCLE]: surplus production mechanism.
    Restores when: cycle broken. Counter to: ICD, Rhythm Disruption,
    ECC at MED effectiveness each.
  CC [CORRECTION CAPACITY]: deviation detection and adjustment.
    Counter to: Correction Theatre, MSC, Calibration Strip, ECD,
    Competence Undermining at LOW-MED effectiveness [CC often
    captured before it can fire; named tools required].
  TS [TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY]: correctable method transmitted.
    Counter to: Fertile Lock, Propagation Mimicry, Churn Normalization
    at MED effectiveness.
  SA [SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY]: successor capacity to revise.
    Counter to: Intercession Monopoly, Formation Capture [post-window],
    Long Lock-In at LOW-MED effectiveness.
  MI [MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY]: honest instrument, declared blind spots.
    Counter to: Loaded Language, Calibration Strip, MSC at MED
    effectiveness [MI often the target; external standard required].
  SP [SURPLUS PRODUCTION]: output exceeds input.
    Counter to: ECC [demonstrates exit viability], ICD [restores SR]
    at MED effectiveness.

NAMED COUNTER-TOOLS:

AXIS GROUNDING [Track C]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
[CALIBRATION: EMPIRICALLY CONFIRMED v22 -- WIP 18. 11 case-library
  deployments [CAT_SCIENTIST, DEVELOPMENTAL_ASYMMETRY_FAMILY,
  IGNATIUS_LOYOLA, ROBERT_MCNAMARA, BLACK_TEMPLARS, MECHANICUS_
  MACHINE_SPIRIT, ROBOUTE_GUILLIMAN, PHOSPHOPHYLLITE, DISCO_ELYSIUM,
  NOITA_WAND, RAND_DRAGONMOUNT] confirm effectiveness ratings below
  with no contradicting case. First tool promoted from framework-
  derived to empirically calibrated.]
  Cost: LOW | Effectiveness: Sincere Delusion [HIGH], FC post-event [MED], Competence Undermining [MED]
  Anchor claims to behavioral outputs, not to social consensus or
  emotional intensity. The instrument reads what the axis actually
  produced, not what it felt like to produce it.
  Mechanism: locate behavioral record; compare to claimed self-reading;
    correct the narrative to match the output not the emotional memory.
  Damage: none. Calibrating friction only [self-critical pressure].
  Fires against: Sincere Delusion Framing [primary]; Competence
    Undermining [axis confidence restoration]; Formation Capture
    post-event persistence [behavioral record vs emotional narrative];
    any weapon targeting CAG or self-reading accuracy.
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS; CAG; CORRECTION CAPACITY.

EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cost: HIGH [pre-emptive; must build before capture completes]
  Effectiveness: MSC [HIGH -- only counter], Calibration Strip [HIGH], ECD [MED]
  Note: hardest to deploy -- weapon purpose is to eliminate this tool.
  Independent measurement surface not owned or controlled by the actor
  whose outputs are being measured. The instrument producing the reading
  must be outside the boundary being read.
  Mechanism: identify measurement surface; verify it is not controlled
    by the deployer of the relevant weapon; obtain reading from outside
    the capture architecture.
  Damage: none. May produce discomfort when reading contradicts
    captured surface [calibrating friction].
  Fires against: Measurement Surface Control [primary -- external
    instrument is the only counter]; Calibration Strip [requires
    independent reference before calibration point]; ECD [independent
    causal reconstruction from outside diffusion architecture];
    Consultancy Shield [independent commissioning is the test].
  Note: hardest tool to deploy against MSC and Calibration Strip --
    the weapon's purpose is to eliminate independent reference surfaces.
    Building this tool requires action before capture completes.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; MSC; CALIBRATION STRIP.

ALTERNATIVE SUBSTRATE CONSTRUCTION
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cost: HIGH [pre-emptive; resource-intensive]
  Effectiveness: ECC [HIGH], Isolation Construction [HIGH], ICD [MED], Rhythm Disruption [MED]
  Build parallel generative substrate outside the captured dependency
  architecture before exit. Exit becomes viable when alternative
  substrate is operational.
  Mechanism: identify what substrate the weapon controls; build
    alternative source of same substrate [relational, economic,
    informational, institutional]; reduce dependency incrementally;
    exit when alternative is load-bearing.
  Damage: none. Resource cost to build [not damage -- investment].
  Fires against: Exit Cost Construction [primary -- alternative
    substrate reduces exit cost]; Isolation Construction [alternative
    relational substrate]; Rhythm Disruption [stable scheduling
    outside precarity architecture]; ECC [substrate independence
    restores exit viability].
  Cross-ref: GENERATIVE CYCLE; EXIT COST CONSTRUCTION; T1.4;
    [CASE:IPV_ECONOMIC_EMPOWERMENT_RCT] [CONFIRMED v23 under revised
    one-real-case bar -- first real-world functional match, a true
    randomized controlled trial; fires against Exit Cost Construction
    specifically via financial substrate, the weapon's primary
    target].

ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Cost: HIGH [structural; requires coordination]
  Effectiveness: ICD [HIGH -- primary counter], Rhythm Disruption [HIGH], FC [MED -- pre-window only]
