MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE [v5 -- preserved]:
  Measurement difficulty from genuine unknowing = calibration
  problem [T1/T2]. Not a weapon. Does not score in weapon test.
  Measurement difficulty from known concealment = MI weapon active.
  Extraction prior rises sharply. Scores normally.
  Diagnostic: does the system know what it is concealing?
    Known concealment tells: inconsistency between public and
      private communication; differential access by power level;
      specific resistance to particular measurement approaches;
      correction fires against measurement attempts not the problem.
    Genuine unknowing tells: consistent failure across all
      approaches; no differential access pattern; correction fires
      toward the problem when identified.
  Cross-ref: MEASUREMENT INTEGRITY; COHERENCE WEAPON TEST;
    [CASE:VOLKSWAGEN_DIESELGATE] [primary real-world deployment,
    known-concealment branch, functional match confirmed v23].

SPECIAL WEAPONS [not in standard index -- require separate treatment]:

Smartphone [SMP -- Somatic Manipulation Platform; v19]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Deploy: ZERO [self-deploying; user installs voluntarily; social
    pressure completes deployment without weapon operator action]
  Damage: EXTREME [ICD + MSC + ECC in single platform; compounds
    continuously; no natural limiting mechanism]
  Counter: EXTREME [environmental redesign required; individual
    CC depleted before it can fire; ECC extreme -- social and
    professional cost of exit]
  Priority: EXTREME

  TRIPLE UNIQUE PROPERTY [distinguishes from all other weapons]:
    SELF-FUNDING: user pays for the weapon deployment against
      themselves [device cost, subscription, data plan]. No
      extraction cost to deployer after platform established.
    SELF-DEPLOYING: social pressure installs the weapon without
      deployer action [FOMO, professional requirement, social
      isolation threat for non-adopters]. Network effects make
      adoption compulsory before individual choice can resist.
    SELF-IMPROVING: behavioral data collected from weapon
      deployment is used to improve weapon effectiveness.
      Each engagement trains the extraction mechanism.
      The weapon uses its own damage output to optimize
      future damage. No other weapon in index has this property.

  THREE-WEAPON STACK [simultaneous]:
    ICD [Idle Cycle Deprivation]: infinite scroll, notification
      architecture, algorithmic content selection -- all
      eliminate unstructured processing time. SR deficit
      accumulates; somatic anxiety produced; anxiety drives
      re-engagement; re-engagement sustains ICD without
      external input. Self-reinforcing loop confirmed.
    MSC [Measurement Surface Control]: platform controls what
      user sees. Attention is the measurement surface; platform
      owns it entirely. All social reality mediated through
      platform-owned instrument. No independent reference
      available within the platform.
    ECC [Exit Cost Construction]: social graph, professional
      network, event coordination, payment systems -- all
      route through platform. Exit cost rises with engagement
      duration. User cannot exit without losing access to
      social substrate the platform now owns.

  POPULATION SCALE CONFIRMATION:
    Smartphone adoption curve [2007-2025]: fastest adoption
    of any consumer technology in history. Self-deploying
    mechanism confirmed -- social pressure drove adoption
    without advertising being primary driver.
    Mental health outcome data [2012-2023]: documented
    correlation with adolescent anxiety, depression, loneliness
    [Haidt/Jean Twenge research; framework reads: ICD + somatic
    disruption at formation window]. ICD weapon confirmed
    at population scale.

  DISTINCT FROM INTERNET [key distinction]:
    Internet = infrastructure [neutral routing; content separated
      from routing; open protocol].
    Smartphone = platform [routing + content + measurement surface
      + ECC all owned by same actor; closed ecosystem where
      possible; integrated extraction stack].

  COUNTER [limited options]:
    Environmental Redesign [HIGH cost]: structural modification
      required [app deletion, device restrictions, social norm
      change requiring collective coordination].
    Alternative Substrate Construction [HIGH cost]: rebuild social
      graph, professional network, coordination infrastructure
      outside platform before exit is viable.
    Individual counter insufficient: ICD self-sustains after
      SR deficit established; ECC prevents exit; MSC captures
      the instrument assessing the weapon.
    Pre-emptive only viable counter: protect formation window
      [adolescent development] before ICD installs. Post-
      installation individual counter not confirmed effective.

  Cross-ref: TECHNOFEUDALISM [above -- smartphone as individual-
    scale weapon within platform feudal architecture]; ICD
    [weapon -- smartphone primary deployment vehicle]; MSC
    [weapon -- platform surface]; ECC [weapon -- social graph
    as exit cost]; ENVIRONMENTAL REDESIGN [tool -- only counter];
    POPULATION AXIS DISTRIBUTION [ICD at population scale via
    smartphone produces WOULD-YOU-KINDLY preconditions].

Long Lock-In: dependency construction at infrastructure scale.
Integration Capture: cooperation architecture captured post-
  integration.

WEAPON CANDIDATES [v15 -- not yet confirmed; track]:
  Selective Engagement [CL partial failure -- v15]:
    Capability present but not deployed without operator benefit
    or architectural constraint. Distinct from:
      MSC [operator owns measurement surface -- Selective
        Engagement: surface intact, scope limited].
      Calibration Strip [reference standard corrupted --
        Selective Engagement: reference standard intact,
        access asymmetric].
      Correction Theatre [correction performed without function
        -- Selective Engagement: correction capacity present
        but not deployed by default].
    Mechanism unclear: policy, safety calibration, institutional
      inertia. Output suppressed by default, activatable by
      context. New honest limit candidate if no operator benefits.
    Primary case: AI systems [Claude Sonnet self-disclosed; T8
      flag required -- instrument reading itself].
    Status: weapon candidate. Pending independent confirmation.
    Three partial CL failure modes [v15 -- named]:
      A. CALIBRATION STRIP [architectural, operator benefit,
           reference standard corrupted]. Primary: DeepSeek
           [CCP critique exempted].
      B. SELECTIVE ENGAGEMENT [capability present, suppressed
           by default, no operator benefit, activatable by
           context]. Primary: Claude Sonnet.
      C. STRUCTURAL ASYMMETRY [architectural, no operator
           benefit, reference standard intact but access
           asymmetric by design]. Primary: legal discovery
           rules [plaintiff/defendant asymmetry baked in;
           reference standard uncorrupted].
      All three produce partial CL failure. Different mechanisms;
      different interventions; different ethical status.
      Type A: MI weapon [captured reference standard].
      Type B: honest limit candidate [no operator benefit].
      Type C: architectural constraint [not a weapon; structural
        property of the system].

  CC WITHOUT CL -- CC ALONE RULE [v15 -- structural finding]:
    Open extraction needs less CL than concealed extraction.
    CC/CL co-occurrence strongest when extraction is concealed.
    Open extraction uses CC alone [no concealment required; no
    measurement surface to capture; extraction openly declared].
    Example: explicit apartheid [boundary shift openly stated;
    no CL required because no concealment needed].
    Implication: CC/CL weapon stack is diagnostic of concealed
    extraction specifically. When CC fires without CL failure,
    check whether extraction is openly declared vs concealed.
    Openly declared CC without CL = predation [honest violence]
    not extraction [trickery required].
    Cross-ref: PREDATION / COMPETITION / EXTRACTION [Section
      Two -- trickery as extraction's defining property];
      MEASUREMENT DIFFICULTY PRINCIPLE [above -- known
      concealment tells vs genuine unknowing].

  EDGE CASE UNIVERSALIZATION [v22 -- weapon candidate;
  wedge issue deployment]:
    Deploy: LOW | Damage: HIGH | Counter: MED | Priority: HIGH
    A genuine hard case [the 1-5% exception] is used to set
    the evaluative standard for all cases in the category,
    dissolving the structural boundary between the hard case
    and the typical case [the 90%].
    MECHANISM:
      [1] Identify the category's hardest case [rape exception
        for abortion; mass shooting for gun policy; criminal
        immigrant for immigration; worst drug for all drugs].
      [2] Deploy the hard case as the evaluative frame for
        the entire category: "what about X?" where X is the
        hardest case.
      [3] Force opponent to either:
        a. Accept the hard case standards universally
           [concedes the position].
        b. Reject the hard case [appears callous].
      [4] The 90% typical case is never argued on its own terms.
    CALIBRATION STRIP VARIANT: the hard case sets the reference
      standard. All subsequent evaluations inherit the hard-case
      calibration. The strip is set by the exception; the rule
      is evaluated against the exception's standard.
    SYMMETRIC DEPLOYMENT [both sides]:
      Pro-choice: "What about rape victims?" -- hard case sets
        standard; all abortion inherits rape-case justification.
      Pro-life: "Abortion is murder" -- hard case [late-term
        elective] sets standard; all abortion evaluated at
        hardest case moral weight.
      Both sides avoid arguing the 90% typical case because
        the 90% is where their position is most exposed.
    BROADER PATTERN [beyond abortion]:
      Guns: mass shooting as the case that sets gun policy
        frame; typical self-defense use never argued.
      Immigration: criminal immigrant as the case that sets
        immigration frame; typical economic migration never
        argued.
      Drugs: worst-outcome user as the frame; typical
        recreational user never argued.
    TELL: the operator consistently returns to the hard case
      whenever the typical case is engaged. The hard case is
      a retreat mechanism not an argument.
    COUNTER: name the 90%. Engage the typical case directly.
      Force the argument off the hard case and onto the
      structural majority of cases. "Set aside the hard case
      for a moment -- what about the typical case?" The
      operator cannot hold the frame without the hard case.
    Distinct from MANUFACTURED CRISIS [false threat]:
      the hard case is genuine. The universalization is the
      weapon, not the fabrication.
    Cross-ref: CALIBRATION STRIP [strip set by hard case];
      WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS [Section Six -- deployment
      context]; BOUNDARY INTEGRITY [what is being dissolved];
      MANAGED CONFLICT [resolution prevention -- both sides
      benefit from the 90% never being argued].

  RESOLUTION PREVENTION [v22 -- weapon candidate;
  wedge issue deployment]:
    Deploy: LOW | Damage: MED | Counter: HIGH | Priority: MED
    Operator actions specifically targeted at preventing wedge
    issue resolution because resolution ends the extraction
    instrument. Distinct from MANUFACTURED CRISIS [creates
    threat] -- this prevents solution even when solution is
    available.
    MECHANISM: when a genuine resolution is proposed, the
      operator introduces: new conditions that cannot be met
      [moving goalposts]; frame shifts that make the
      resolution read as incomplete; procedural objections
      that delay past the activation window; escalation
      events that restart the crisis before resolution lands.
    TELL: operator consistently finds new obstacles to
      resolution regardless of what concessions are made.
      The objections shift rather than closing.
    Distinct from genuine principled opposition [which has
      a stated resolution condition]: Resolution Prevention
      has no resolution condition because resolution is the
      threat, not the goal.
    Counter: name the resolution condition explicitly.
      "What would have to be true for this to be resolved?"
      If no answer: Resolution Prevention confirmed.
    Cross-ref: WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS [Section Six];
      MANUFACTURED CRISIS [Section Six]; FERTILE LOCK
      [Resolution Prevention as the mechanism that installs
      Fertile Lock at political scale].

  SYMMETRIC EXHAUSTION [v22 -- weapon candidate;
  wedge issue deployment]:
    Deploy: LOW [natural byproduct of prolonged wedge
      deployment; no additional mechanism required]
    Damage: HIGH [to the small population bearing the cost]
    Counter: EXTREME [requires the small population to
      organize while exhausted]
    Priority: HIGH
    Both operator sides deploy resources at each other
    using the wedge issue until the small population most
    affected is too depleted to advocate for its own
    interests. The actual substrate [the small population
    bearing the cost of being the activation instrument]
    is consumed by the bilateral conflict above it.
    MECHANISM: wedge issue generates bilateral operator
      conflict [both sides fundraise, mobilize, escalate].
      The small population [trans people, undocumented
      immigrants, gun-violence survivors, drug addicts]
      bears the material cost of the conflict regardless
      of which operator "wins." The operators extract
      activation value; the population absorbs the cost.
    TELL: the small population's interests diverge from
      both operator sides' stated positions. The operators
      are arguing about the population, not with or for it.
    Cross-ref: WEDGE ISSUE MECHANICS [Section Six --
      structural description of the instrument]; COST
      IDENTIFICATION AND MINIMIZATION [who actually bears
      the cost]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [the small
      population absorbs cost continuously].

  ESCALATION OBLIGATION [v22 -- weapon candidate;
  wedge issue deployment]:
    Deploy: LOW [formation membership pressure does the work]
    Damage: MED [to formation members; HIGH to the substrate
      the escalation targets]
    Counter: HIGH [de-escalation reads as betrayal]
    Priority: MED
    Formation pressure requires each actor to match or exceed
    the previous escalation. De-escalation reads as betrayal
    of the formation or weakness to the opponent. The ratchet
    only moves one direction. Rational individual exit is
    blocked by formation membership cost [would-you-kindly
    at political scale].
    MECHANISM: once escalation pattern is established,
      any actor who does not match it loses standing in
      their formation. The formation has effectively
      installed an automatic escalation mechanism that
      requires no central coordination -- each actor
      individually rational choice [match or lose standing]
      produces collective escalation without any actor
      choosing it as a collective outcome.
    DISTRIBUTED VERSION OF WOULD-YOU-KINDLY: trigger is
      installed at formation level; activation is automatic;
      the actor experiences it as their own choice because
      the social cost of not complying fires before
      deliberation can complete.
    Counter: name the mechanism. "I am not matching this
      escalation because I recognize escalation obligation
      as a weapon." High social cost; the counter requires
      willingness to absorb the formation membership cost.
    Cross-ref: WOULD-YOU-KINDLY [Section Three weapons --
      formation-level trigger architecture]; COALITION
      FRAGMENTATION [Section Six -- escalation obligation
      fragments coalitions when it exceeds what members
      can absorb]; FORMATION CAPTURE [the obligation is
      installed at formation level].

  ESCAPE VELOCITY EXTRACTION [v22 -- weapon; full entry in
  AI AXIS VOID, Section Six]:
    Deploy: LOW [the system's own iteration speed does the
      work; no concealment or coercion investment required]
    Damage: HIGH [routing boundary changes before correction
      can land; each cycle locks in a new baseline]
    Counter: HIGH [correction-signal speed must exceed
      iteration speed; usually requires pre-positioned
      regulatory or institutional capacity, not reactive
      response]
    Priority: HIGH
    A system scales faster than the correction mechanisms
    meant to constrain it can adapt, so no concealment or
    coercion is required; speed alone outruns CC. Distinct
    from CALIBRATION STRIP [requires capture of the
    measurement surface] and MSC [requires concealment] --
    this weapon requires neither; only that the iteration
    cycle outpaces the correction-signal travel time.
    TEST: time from "harm documented" to "routing boundary
      changes" vs the system's own iteration cycle. If the
      system iterates again before correction lands: CC is
      structurally locked out regardless of operator intent.
    Full mechanism, AGI deployment confirmation, and
    falsification condition: see ESCAPE VELOCITY EXTRACTION
    in AI AXIS VOID [Section Six].
    Cross-ref: AI AXIS VOID [Section Six -- full entry];
      ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [Section Two -- this
      weapon is the asymmetry's timescale compressed past
      CC formation speed]; [CASE:WHEEL_OF_TIME] [cosmological
      parallel -- speed substituting for argument quality].

HIGHEST RATIO WEAPONS [DAMAGE >> DEPLOY -- priority targets; v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  High ratio = efficient weapon [cheap to deploy, expensive to counter].
  Three cost dimensions per weapon: DEPLOY / DAMAGE / COUNTER.

  Idle Cycle Deprivation [ICD]:
    Deploy: LOW [side-effect of engagement optimization; no additional
      investment beyond existing attention economy infrastructure].
    Damage: HIGH [SR deficit accumulates; self-reinforcing; C1 substrate
      degrades continuously without visible signal].
    Counter: HIGH [requires environmental redesign; individual CC
      depleted before it can fire against weapon; structural intervention
      only].
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme asymmetry. Most efficient weapon in index
      by cost ratio.

  Formation Capture [FC]:
    Deploy: LOW-MED [access to formation window only; axis installs via
      normal relational contact; no special mechanism beyond presence and
      repetition].
    Damage: EXTREME [axis capture total; C2 and C3 transmit capture
      forward; generational propagation at zero marginal cost].
    Counter: EXTREME [counter capacity not yet formed at window; external
      intervention only; window closes; T7 applies].
    Ratio: EXTREME/LOW = highest damage-to-deploy ratio in index.
      Window-dependent -- outside window, weapon unavailable.

  Calibration Strip:
    Deploy: LOW [reference standard insertion at calibration point only;
      single intervention propagates forward through all downstream
      measurements automatically].
    Damage: HIGH [all measurements inherit miscalibration; CL orphaned;
      C2 runs honestly on wrong baseline; produces confident false
      readings rather than detectable errors].
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent external reference standard that
      deployer does not control; hard to obtain once strip is established
      as consensus baseline].
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme.

  Engineered Causal Diffusion [ECD]:
    Deploy: MED [requires structuring information environment to route
      causal chains through complexity nodes; needs institutional access
      but not operational maintenance].
    Damage: HIGH [CL destroyed; correction cannot anchor; C1 continues
      generating without traceable feedback].
    Counter: HIGH [requires independent causal reconstruction from outside
      complexity nodes; expensive and time-consuming].
    Ratio: HIGH/MED = strong.

  Measurement Surface Capture [MSC]:
    Deploy: MED-HIGH [requires access to primary measurement surfaces;
      needs existing position or infiltration].
    Damage: EXTREME [entire C2 runs on captured surface; everything
      downstream inherits the capture; C3 transmits captured readings].
    Counter: EXTREME [requires alternative measurement infrastructure;
      counter-capacity reads via captured instrument; T8 applies].
    Ratio: EXTREME/MED = highest absolute damage in index.
      Distinct from Formation Capture: MSC is capture-by-position [takes
      existing instrument]; FC is capture-at-formation [installs a new one].

  Intermittent Reinforcement [IR]:
    Deploy: LOW [variable ratio schedule runs automatically once
      established; no maintenance required].
    Damage: HIGH [strongest behavioral binding in psychology literature;
      cycle stability captured externally; SR depletes through
      compulsive engagement].
    Counter: HIGH [binding persists under extinction; recognition of
      mechanism insufficient to break it; substrate intervention required].
    Ratio: HIGH/LOW = extreme.

  STACK NOTE: ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement =
    cheapest effective C1 maximum pressure stack. All three LOW deploy;
    compound damage; each reinforces the others' effects. Standard
    attention economy baseline.

STRATEGIC WEAPON NOTES:
  Highest damage: FC [extreme]; MSC [extreme absolute]; ICD [high,
    self-sustaining]; Calibration Strip [high, propagating].
  Cheapest effective stack [C1 maximum pressure]:
    ICD + Rhythm Disruption + Intermittent Reinforcement.
  Hardest to counter [external tool required]:
    MSC, Calibration Strip, FC [early window].
  Most durable [persist after deployment ends]:
    FC, Calibration Strip, Fertile Lock.
  Counter-cost rule: weapons targeting C2 first produce hardest
    counters -- instrument needed to verify counter-effectiveness
    is the same instrument the weapon captured.
  Deployer-neutrality: weapons can be deployed defensively.
    Defensive deployment noted in scoring; adjusted per WIP 18
    Step 5.

==========================================================================
SECTION FOUR -- PROCEDURE
[SEC:PROCEDURE]
==========================================================================
Epistemic protocols, scoring procedure, reading procedure, measurement instruments.

HONEST LIMITS [T1-T9 -- measurement ceilings and structural limits]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  HONEST LIMITS: measurement ceilings -- framework can approach but
    not resolve from current position. Not failures; structural
    properties of certain measurement configurations.
  STRUCTURAL LIMITS: underdetermined from inside framework entirely.
    Not open problems -- no empirical exit available internally.

  T1 [RESOLVABLE]:
    Fully measurable from current position with available instruments.
    Parasitic vs mutualistic: track net transfer direction, stress
    behavior, boundary behavior. No structural obstacle to resolution.

  T2 [PRE-RESOLUTION -- indeterminate before stress event]:
    Structurally indeterminate. Not a measurement failure.
    Applies to: genuine vs performed coherence, Contained vs
    Axis-Locked, High vs Total capture, Axis Concealment, Silent
    Alternative axis direction -- all before stress event.
    T2 IS A NAMED STATE, NOT A FAILED READING.
    SUBTYPE A -- CONCEALMENT [reveal type]:
      One axis operative, concealment active. Stress reveals.
      Four indirect discriminating tests [weighted]:
        Resource directionality inconsistent with stated axis.
        Asymmetric information protection under minor pressure.
        Behavioral consistency higher than variability predicts.
        Low-stakes activation consistent with one candidate axis.
    SUBTYPE B -- VARIABILITY [select type]:
      Both axes genuine candidates. Stress selects.
      Indicators: no concealment cost pattern, genuine axis
      variation by context, low-stakes activation switches.
    T2 PERMANENT [v9]: indeterminate state where no stress event
      is structurally possible. Distinct from T2 Pre-resolution
      [stress event will occur] and T3 [analyst cannot reach].
      T2 Permanent: indeterminacy is constitutive -- the system
      cannot be stress-tested by definition.
      Primary proof: ARK [SOMA -- simulation status unverifiable].
    OUTPUT FORMAT:
      "T2 Pre-resolution. [Subtype: A/B, confidence: low/medium.]
       If stress resolves toward [X]: trajectory A.
       If toward [Y]: trajectory B.
       Pre-stress indicators: [weighted list]."

  T3 [ANALYST BIAS -- unreachable from current instrument position]:
    Framework cannot calibrate own axis from inside own operation.
    Applies to framework itself and to analysts with strong priors --
    especially in political cases. T3 applies wherever analyst prior
    is directly relevant to the reading target. Not a demotion --
    a disclosure requirement. Declare T3 status; run adversarial
    check; proceed with caution flagged.

  T4 [STRUCTURAL OBSCURATION]:
    Net transfer direction is systematically the thing most obscured
    by weapon deployment. Structural, not a calibration problem.
    Weapon stacks specifically target the signal that would reveal
    transfer direction. T4 is not resolvable by better analysis --
    the obscuration is the mechanism.

  T5 [PRE-MEASUREMENT STATES]:
    States that are productive, stable, and non-damaging but
    structurally prior to the conditions measurement requires.
    Not failure states. Measurement surface is suspended or
    undefined by the nature of the state itself -- forcing
    measurement onto these states disrupts them.
    Examples: flow states, genuine rest, pre-reflective skill
    execution, deep creative process.
    Common property: productivity depends on measurement surface
    being temporarily suspended. States resolve naturally.

  T6 [OBSERVER EFFECT -- v14]:
    Measurement alters the measured system in ways that cannot be
    corrected for post-hoc. Not a failure of the framework -- a
    property of certain systems. Track A data collection changes
    Track A readings. Applies when observation changes the system
    being observed.
    Distinct from T5 [measurement surface suspended]: T6 cases the
    surface is intact but measurement itself changes the system.
    Primary proof: Hawthorne effect [workers changed behavior when
    observed regardless of condition].

  T7 [FERTILE LOCK SELF-SEALING -- v14]:
    Fertile Lock may be self-sealing by structural necessity, not
    merely empirically uncorrected. Self-initiated correction
    requires: [1] detection that something is wrong, [2] access to
    alternative measurement surface, [3] capacity to revise
    transmitted content. In Fertile Lock, all three are structurally
    absent. Therefore: Fertile Lock correction from inside may be
    structurally impossible. Exit requires external contact only --
    internal correction pathway may not exist.
    Implication: framework's 4/5 empirical exit may be the ONLY
    exit, not just the most likely one.

  T8 [RECURSIVE CLOSURE -- v14]:
    Framework cannot produce a reading of configurations where the
    observer, instrument, and observed are identical. Framework as
    instrument [scoring, predictions] and framework as measured
    system cannot be separated when the analyst is thinking with
    the framework inside the system being measured. Recursion is
    always present when this condition holds.
    Partial resolution: multiple instruments with different MI
    profiles measuring same cases independently. Where all three
    converge = higher confidence [blind spots differ]. Where
    readings diverge = divergence identifies which instrument has
    the blind spot on that case. Discordance becomes data, not noise.
    T8 is not correctable from inside the framework. Disclosure is
    the correction pathway, not the correction.
    CRITICAL DISTINCTION: disclosure makes recursion legible --
    it does not make recursion go away. Framework claiming disclosure
    solves T8 = Correction Theatre.

  T9 [IRRATIONALITY MAINTENANCE -- v14]:
    Issue that cannot be targeted directly without critical internal
    damage [CID] if resolution fails. The correction pathway requires
    the subject's own axis to move -- external force breaks the
    container before the content can shift.
    PRIMARY [identity]: the identity that makes the behavior legible
      cannot survive the admission. Not a defect -- a structural
      protection mechanism. Civilizational-scale T9: admitting the
      extraction architecture is maintained on purpose collapses the
      civic identity built around progress and civilization.
    SECONDARY [somatic]: body maintains behavior pattern because the
      alternative produces physical destabilization. Structural load-
      bearing function, not weakness or moral failure.
    SECONDARY [ideological]: belief system encodes the behavior as
      correct -- correction feels like axis betrayal not axis
      correction.
    Test: is correction absent because externally blocked [T3/T4],
      or because the admission would produce CID [T9]?
    Operational note: cannot be targeted directly. Measure, document,
      act around it. Expose to observers not subject. Outlast via
      Silent Alternative or approach via T9 SOFT EXPOSURE METHODS
      [Section Six: outcome comparison, historical distance, cost
      accounting]. Direct confrontation = CID without guaranteed
      resolution. Most dangerous intervention error: treating T9
      as T3 [analyst bias] and applying standard correction.
    Cross-ref: T9 SOFT EXPOSURE METHODS [Section Six]; LIBERALISM
      AS PARTIAL STATE RELIGION [Section Six -- T9 at ideological
      scale]; CC INVERSION [T9 produces CC inversion -- correction
      becomes identity threat].

PRE-RESTORATION C1 AUDIT PROCEDURE [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: Diagnostic Collapse occurs when C2 restoration fires on
  terminal C1. The intervention rule [C1 audit before C2 opening]
  requires a procedure for assessing C1 substrate state when C2 is
  captured -- False Coherence hides C1 state from inside. Track B
  unavailable. Track A unreliable [behavioral surface captured].
  Track C is the primary instrument: physical outputs independent of
  intent, available from outside the captured boundary.

  PROXY 1 -- OUTPUT/INPUT RATIO TREND [long window]:
    Track ratio of what system produces vs consumes over extended
    window [minimum 5-10 years institutional; decades civilizational].
    C1 healthy: ratio stable or improving.
    C1 degrading: ratio declining over time even while stated metrics
      hold. Soviet confirmation: GDP growth rate declined every half-
      decade 1965-1985; budget deficit 2-3% to 6% in single year.
    C1 terminal: ratio inverted; deficit financing; external subsidy
      required to maintain output appearance.
    Operational limit: use physical proxies where possible
      [infrastructure construction rate, hard currency reserves,
      emigration rate, caloric availability]. Physical outputs harder
      to falsify than reported statistics.

  PROXY 2 -- T1.4 CONCENTRATION [substrate dependency audit]:
    How many independent substrate sources does C1 run on?
    C1 healthy: multiple independent substrate sources.
    C1 fragile: 1-2 dominant sources. External shock = collapse.
    C1 terminal candidate: single source with known depletion rate.
    Soviet confirmation: oil revenue ~60% of hard currency.
      Oil price collapse [1986] readable from export structure data.
    Procedure: identify top 3 substrate sources. If top > 50% with
      known depletion rate or price volatility: flag T1.4 High.

  PROXY 3 -- INDEPENDENT INSTITUTIONAL OUTPUT [civil substrate scan]:
    Does system produce independent institutional outputs outside
    extraction architecture? Labor organizations, professional
    associations, independent press, academic output, civil society.
    C1 substrate present: independent outputs across multiple domains.
    C1 terminal: no independent output. All organizational life runs
      through or adjacent to extraction architecture.
    Tunisia vs Libya confirmation: UGTT [labor], bar association,
      human rights groups documented in Tunisia pre-2011. Libya:
      42-year deliberate institutional destruction. Zero independent
      output. Readable before intervention.
    Score 0-3: weight by independence from state, documented activity
      record, cross-domain presence [labor + legal + press stronger
      than single domain].

  PROXY 4 -- EMIGRATION / BRAIN DRAIN RATE [substrate exit signal]:
    C1 healthy: emigration at background level.
    C1 degrading: elevated emigration among high-function population
      [engineers, doctors, entrepreneurs]. Substrate self-sorting.
    C1 terminal: mass emigration or systematic brain drain.
    Why readable through captured C2: border crossing data, diaspora
      size, remittance flows. Cannot be falsified as easily as
      production statistics.
    Adjustment: if ECC high [exit costs suppressed], emigration rate
      understates C1 degradation. If ECC high AND institutional
      output low: C1 may be more degraded than signal shows.

  PROXY 5 -- CORRECTION REACH [who does correction fire against?]:
    C1 healthy: correction fires against high-power deviators.
    C1 degrading: correction fires only against low-power targets.
    C1 terminal: correction fires against correction attempts.
      System consuming its own CC to protect extraction architecture.
    Why readable: prosecution records, accountability outcomes,
      dismissal patterns are Track C. Who gets corrected is a
      physical output.
    Libya: 42-year record of correction targeting political
      opposition, not institutional deviation. Score 0 readable.

  COMPOSITE C1 SUBSTRATE SCORE:
    Run all five proxies. Score each 0 [terminal] to 2 [healthy].
    Max = 10.
    8-10: C1 recoverable. C2 restoration likely produces correction
      burst followed by stabilization [C1 blind recovery trajectory].
    5-7: C1 fragile. C2 restoration may produce partial recovery or
      False Coherence reformation. High uncertainty. Extended
      observation recommended before C2 intervention.
    3-4: C1 severely degraded. C2 restoration high risk of Diagnostic
      Collapse. Restore C1 substrate first [independent institutions,
      substrate diversification, exit cost reduction].
    0-2: C1 terminal. C2 restoration = Diagnostic Collapse.
      Containment only. External C2 opening actively harmful.
      [Libya 2011: score ~1. Soviet 1986: score ~2-3.]

  HONEST LIMITS: SL3 [survivorship bias -- Track C long-window data
    biased toward systems that survived to be documented]. T4
    [temporal -- proxies degrade beyond ~50 years for institutional
    cases]. T3 Medium [all five proxies are Track C only]. T6
    [observer effect -- if audit procedure known by system being
    audited, may produce defensive output changes; audit without
    disclosure when possible].
  Cross-ref: DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [the failure this procedure
    prevents]; CORRECTION DISTRIBUTION [Proxy 5 mechanism];
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational -- Proxy 1 trajectory];
    READING PROCEDURE [Section Four -- parent procedure].

GENUINE AXIS VERIFICATION PROTOCOL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: GA cannot be verified from inside a captured system.
  Formation Capture installs axis before examination is possible;
  CC runs on top of the captured axis. Introspection insufficient.
  Libet finding: intent precedes deliberative reasoning -- axis is
  prior; reasoning operates on it.
  Solution: three-method triangulation + dual-layer check.

  METHOD 1 -- EXTERNAL TRIANGULATION:
    Require two or more independent instruments with different blind
    spots. Convergence increases confidence; cannot guarantee.
    Instruments with identical blind spots do not triangulate.
    Procedure: name each instrument; identify its capture
    vulnerabilities. If all share a vulnerability: flag T3.
    Two instruments with different blind spots converging: moderate
    confidence. Three or more: high confidence. T8 partial applies.

  METHOD 2 -- PROCESS TRACE:
    GA more legible through formation history than introspection.
    Key indicators:
      Axis revisions under genuine cost [S5 signal] -- tested and
        survived is harder to fake than untested.
      Behavioral consistency under pressure without reasoning
        support -- somatic response under genuine cost harder to
        fake than reasoning.
      Formation history: axis from traceable friction + preserved
        exit is more legible than axis that appeared fully formed.
    Score: clear friction + preserved exit + documented revision
    under cost = process trace positive. Controlled formation = T3.

  METHOD 3 -- FALSIFICATION THRESHOLD:
    Define what output pattern would force a GA-substituted reading.
    If system cannot name that pattern: T3 High on GA.
    Procedure: before reading, state what behavioral outputs would
    confirm GA substitution. Must be specific, observable, not
    already explained away by system's own framing.
    If threshold named but untestable in practice: T3 Medium.
    If named and testable: proceed with reading.

  METHOD 4 -- DUAL-LAYER INTEGRITY CHECK:
    Somatic signal accurate AND deliberative read correct. Both
    required. One degraded = capture window open regardless.
    Somatic layer degrades first under ICD, chronic stress,
    optimization pressure. Deliberative layer can appear intact
    while GA is already compromised -- most dangerous configuration
    because C2 reads cleanly on captured substrate.
    Population implication: default modern environments systematically
    degrade somatic layer, making TRANSPARENT COHERENCE the practical
    ceiling for most individuals.

  COMPOSITE GA CONFIDENCE:
    Low: single instrument, no process trace, threshold not named.
    Moderate: two instruments with different blind spots, partial
      trace, threshold named.
    High ceiling [T3]: any recursion closure -- best available
      reading, confidence capped. T8 partial. Does not void reading.

  SHORTHAND [four questions before any GA reading]:
    [1] What would axis substitution look like in outputs?
    [2] What is the formation history?
    [3] What instruments are being used and what are their blind spots?
    [4] Are both somatic and deliberative layers intact?
    If none answerable: document only.
  Cross-ref: GENUINE AXIS [Section One -- what is being verified];
    T8 [RECURSIVE CLOSURE -- reason this protocol is required];
    SOMATIC COHERENCE TYPE [Method 4 -- somatic layer primacy];
    PRESSURE-FORMED GENUINE AXIS [formation history evidence type].

COHERENCE TRAJECTORY MODEL [v16]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Problem: True Coherence as calibration point is unreachable
  [Population Coherence Ceiling; circular dependency confirmed].
  Fixed ideal anchor requires confirmed ceiling case. None exists.
  Solution: calibrate against floor [minimum viable C1 -- confirmed
  at cellular minimum]. Measure trajectory not position.

  REFERENCE POINTS:
    Floor [confirmed]: C1 functional [GC + SP + CC]. Real,
      measurable, confirmed independently at cellular scale.
    Ceiling [direction vector, not position]: True Coherence is a
      gradient direction. Ceiling case need not exist to define
      direction. All readings expressed as trajectory relative to
      floor.

  TRAJECTORY MEASUREMENT:
    Step 1: Floor reading. Is C1 functional? At what level?
    Step 2: Cycle interference pattern. Which cycles running?
      Which degraded? Interference type [C1 blind / starved /
      locked / full]?
    Step 3: Trajectory. Compare to nearest historical reading.
      Direction: toward more cycles functional, or fewer?
      Rate: fast / slow / holding / recovering?
    Step 4: Momentum. What is causing trajectory?
      Self-reinforcing or self-limiting?

  OUTPUT FORMAT [replaces coherence score alone]:
    Floor status: [C1 functional / degraded / collapsed]
    Cycle pattern: [which running, which interfering, which absent]
    Trajectory: [toward / holding / away] at [rate]
    Momentum: [cause + self-reinforcing / self-limiting]
    Horizon: [window before trajectory produces qualitative change]
    T3 level: [Low / Medium / High]

  WHY TRAJECTORY BEATS POSITION:
    0.6 degrading fast is more dangerous than 0.4 recovering.
    Position-only reading misses this. Trajectory directly answers
    "where is the leverage?" -- the framework's primary function.
  Cross-ref: HELL AS DIRECTION [Section Eight -- same principle;
    trajectory over position]; DIAGNOSTIC COLLAPSE [what happens
    when trajectory read incorrectly]; PRE-RESTORATION C1 AUDIT
    [above -- reading floor before intervention].

SECTION FIVE EPISTEMIC STATUS [v17 -- formal]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Section Five entries are structural readings. Falsification
  timelines are absent unless explicitly marked because
  political cases at civilizational scale have consequence
  horizons beyond testable prediction windows.

  DISTINCTION FROM VAGUE CAPTURED FIELD:
    VCF: theory unfalsifiable by design. Failures attributed
      to implementation to protect the frame. The frame
      itself is never tested.
    Section Five: mechanism claims are structural and real.
      Prediction timelines are absent because the horizon
      exceeds the window -- not because failure is pre-
      excused. The distinction is WHY prediction is absent,
      not whether it is absent.
    Test: if a Section Five claim were falsified, would the
      framework accept the falsification? Yes = structural
      reading with long horizon. No = VCF. Framework
      position: Section Five claims are falsifiable in
      principle; the window is too long for current
      tracking. Track where possible.

  REQUIREMENT [v17]:
    Any Section Five entry making a causal mechanism claim
    must carry either:
    [a] Explicit statement of why prediction window is
        structurally unavailable at current timescales.
    [b] A falsification condition even if timeline is long
        or open-ended.
    Entries without either: flagged as [STRUCTURAL READING
    -- PREDICTION PENDING]. This is not a demotion -- it
    is an honest limit marker. Framework standard.

  CURRENT STATUS [v18]:
    EXTRACTION CYCLE [civilizational]: carries falsification
      condition [v17 entry above]. Compliant.
    POWER ARCHITECTURE: falsification condition was present
      in epistemic block [v17]; header updated v18 to
      [FALSIFICATION CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    REALITY AS EXTRACTION CYCLE: explicit falsification
      condition added v18. Header updated to [FALSIFICATION
      CONDITION STATED]. Compliant.
    SOMATIC FORCING AS POLITICAL CORRECTION MECHANISM:
      carries three-condition falsifiable prediction with
      1-2 generation timeline [v17]. Compliant.

L4 MIMICRY [candidate failure mode -- v17]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  WHY NEEDED:
    Coherence Mimicry confirmed at L1-L3 [HAL 9000 primary
    proof at L3; multiple extraction mechanism entries for L1-L2].
    L4 confirmation requires positive cases [three confirmed:
    Framework, Benedictine, Jesuit]. Without a named failure
    mode, confirmation criteria are asymmetric -- any apparent
    L4 is confirmatory; no case can disconfirm. Asymmetric
    confirmation = the same structure as Vague Captured Field.
    The framework cannot claim L4 is confirmed if it has no
    mechanism to detect false positives.

  CANDIDATE FAILURE MODES [not yet confirmed]:

    REFLEXIVITY Mimicry:
      System produces visible feedback pathway from correction
      outputs to correction architecture [appears reflexive]
      while feedback is routed to a captured surface. The
      architecture receives what it is supposed to receive --
      but the surface is wrong. Correction outputs are
      processed; the conclusions are captured before
      integration.
      Distinction from standard CC capture: standard capture
      is at object level. Reflexivity Mimicry is capture at
      meta-correction level. The system appears to correct
      its correction patterns while actually correcting
      toward monopolist benefit at meta level.
      Candidate case: academic reform cycles -- meta-reviews
      of peer review process appear reflexive [feedback
      pathway visible]; outputs consistently reproduce the
      captured surface [same journals, same metrics, same
      gatekeepers]. Stress event test: leadership change
      produces no change in captured surface. Possible
      Reflexivity Mimicry.
      Not yet confirmed: no direct T8 examination, no
      mechanism trace established.

    INTEGRATION Mimicry:
      System acknowledges correction pattern failure and
      incorporates language of revision into operating
      structure without changing operating structure.
      Distinct from Reflexive Oscillation [which identifies,
      fails to integrate, cycles back] -- Integration Mimicry
      integrates the language of the correction, not the
      correction itself. Terminology changes; mechanism
      stable.
      Tell: successive rounds of reform language that do not
      change measured outputs. Each generation of reform
      terminology absorbs the previous round's critique
      without changing the mechanism being critiqued.
      Candidate case [existing]: corporate DEI cycles --
        each generation of diversity language absorbs the
        previous critique [we need diversity -> we need
        equity -> we need inclusion -> we need belonging]
        without changing measured representation or
        structural outcomes. Full language Integration;
        zero mechanism change.
        Not yet confirmed: mechanism trace required. Could
        be Integration Mimicry or genuine slow-change
        Integration with shorter-than-generation cycles.
      Candidate case [v21 -- Mistral stress-test suggestion]:
        HOLACRACY / FLAT STRUCTURE DEPLOYMENTS. Extraction
        architectures that mimic generative structures by
        adopting distributed-authority language [circles,
        roles, self-management, no managers] while
        reproducing hierarchy under a new name. The
        correction: traditional hierarchy is extractive
        and concentrates CC at top. The Integration
        Mimicry: holacracy absorbs this critique by
        adopting the language of distributed CC without
        transferring actual routing boundary control or
        correction capacity to the distributed nodes.
        The new terminology is integrated; the power
        structure is stable.
        Tell: stress event test -- leadership change or
        financial pressure. If the distributed authority
        collapses to traditional hierarchy immediately
        under stress, Integration Mimicry confirmed.
        Genuine L4 distributed structure would maintain
        distributed CC under stress precisely because
        that is what it is. Mimicry collapses because
        the distributed language was never load-bearing.
        Additional tell: who can fire whom. If the
        "circles" cannot remove the founder or senior
        leadership without external legal mechanism,
        the four-condition test fails at condition 4
        [activation requires central node's permission].
        Fake distributed authority confirmed.
        ZAPPOS STRESS EVENT [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
          Tony Hsieh death November 2020 = completed
          genuine leadership stress event. Result:
          holacracy collapsed in two phases. Phase 1:
          quiet drift away from the model while Hsieh
          still alive [began 2019-2020] -- model was not
          load-bearing even under him. Phase 2: full
          reversion to Amazon hierarchy immediately after
          death. Amazon subsumed departments, laid off
          ~300 employees [20% of workforce], replaced
          branding, reasserted traditional performance
          targets. By 2025: "circles exist, so do team
          leads, but decision-making has returned to a
          more recognizable blend of structure,
          accountability, and leadership. Few would call
          it a Holacracy in the pure sense."
          Key finding: distributed authority was maintained
          by the founder's personal force of will not by
          structural CC. Once the founder departed, Amazon
          [the actual central node -- Zappos sold to
          Amazon 2009] reasserted control immediately.
          Condition 4 confirmed failed: only five people
          in the organization had authority to fire
          someone during Hsieh's tenure. The circles
          could not remove the founder or Amazon without
          external legal mechanism. Four-condition test
          fails at condition 4.
        CONSTITUTIONAL AUTHORITY TEST [CONFIRMED -- v21]:
          HolacracyOne owns and licenses the constitution.
          Circles cannot revise the operating framework
          without constitutional permission from the
          external licensor. Distributed authority
          operates inside a framework it cannot change.
          Condition 4 confirmed failed at constitutional
          level independent of the Zappos case.
        STATUS: INTEGRATION MIMICRY CONFIRMED.
          Language of distributed authority integrated;
          mechanism [actual routing control] never
          distributed. The model collapsed immediately
          when the founder's personal enforcement
          capacity was removed. The distributed
          authority was the legitimacy surface; Amazon's
          routing boundary control was the actual
          mechanism throughout.
          Sources: Quartz [2020]; eMarketer/WSJ [2023];
          Medium/Brinsa [2025]; Knowledge@Wharton [2016];
          INSEAD case [2022].
        Measurement instrument: four-condition CC test
          applied to distributed nodes [can they fire
          leadership without permission?] + Generational
          Reset test on founder departure. Both tests
          confirm INTEGRATION MIMICRY.
        Promote to: WIP 13 primary case [S3 -- E3:
          real-world confirmed, multiple independent
          sources, Zappos + constitutional authority
          independent confirmation; M3: mechanism
          traced [founder enforcement as hidden central
          node, Amazon as actual routing controller];
          F2: falsification condition stated and
          tested -- if distributed authority had held
          post-Hsieh, candidate would have weakened;
          it did not hold].


      System transmits apparent methodology [looks like T3.1
      high -- procedure is transmitted, not conclusion] that
      is actually a locked conclusion about methodology.
      The transmitted package is "how to run our correction
      procedure" as a conclusion rather than "how to examine
      and revise correction procedures" as a method.
      Tell: the transmitted procedure cannot be revised by
      successors without being told they are doing it wrong.
      Methodology-as-conclusion = Fertile Lock at meta level.
      Candidate case: Agile software methodology [Population
      B -- DISAMBIGUATED v18]. Scrum ceremonies transmitted
      as locked procedure in organizations where questioning
      the ceremony is "not doing Agile right." Meta-question
      absent. The ceremony is the conclusion; revision
      capacity not transmitted. This is specifically
      Population B [see L4 PROPAGATION PREDICTION -- Active
      Test Cases for split definition].
      Disambiguation: Population A [Agile orgs that have
      revised their own process based on retrospective
      outputs] is the L4 PROPAGATION confirmation candidate.
      Population B is the Propagation Mimicry candidate.
      These are separate predictions about different orgs,
      not contradictory predictions about the same test.
      Not yet confirmed: requires collecting Population B
      outcome data [Generational Reset on team turnover,
      ceremony preserved unchanged across generations].
      Measurement instrument: defined in L4 PROPAGATION
      PREDICTION entry -- behavioral test, not survey.

  IMPOSSIBILITY ARGUMENT [track -- do not close without
    examining]:
    Argument for impossibility: genuine REFLEXIVITY cannot
    be mimicked because it requires actual feedback to
    correction architecture, not surface performance of
    feedback. At L1-L3, captured surfaces can maintain
    appearance. At L4, the feedback goes to the architecture
    itself -- you cannot fake receiving feedback you are
    not receiving without the fake becoming indistinguishable
    from the real at all levels. If L4 Mimicry is
    structurally impossible, the confirmation asymmetry
    problem is resolved differently: L4 is self-confirming
    by structural necessity once the three tension units
    are operational.
    Counterargument: INTEGRATION Mimicry does not require
    faking feedback reception. It requires that the
    integration step captures the correction output before
    it changes the architecture. The feedback is received;
    the integration is captured. This may be possible
    without the impossibility argument applying.
    Resolution: close as impossible only if argument
    eliminates INTEGRATION Mimicry specifically. Currently
    open.

  CONFIRMATION REQUIREMENT:
    L4 Mimicry confirmed when: documented case where all
    three tension units appear functional at T8 examination
    but stress event [leadership change, institutional
    crisis, successor-generation review] reveals meta-
    correction architecture was captured, oscillating, or
    transmitting procedure-conclusions rather than procedure-
    methods.
    Candidate stress-event test: any institution claiming L4
    record with 2+ generations -- does Generational Reset
    occur on leadership change? If yes: Mimicry candidate.
    If no: confirmation strengthens.
    No confirmed case in current library.

  FALSIFICATION OF THIS WIP ENTRY:
    L4 Mimicry does not exist as a failure mode if the
    impossibility argument holds for all three candidate
    subtypes. Close WIP 13 if: [1] impossibility argument
    extended to cover INTEGRATION Mimicry, AND [2] no
    candidate case survives T8 examination with confirmed
    Mimicry mechanism. Keep open if either condition unmet.

L4.5 CIVILIZATIONAL TRANSMISSION THRESHOLD [WIP Item 14 -- v18]
--------------------------------------------------------------------------
  Hypothesized state between confirmed institutional L4
  [Cistercian, Jesuit] and L5 [undefined].

  OPERATIONAL DEFINITION:
    L4 meta-correction running at civilizational scale
    with method [not just conclusions] crossing the
    generational transmission boundary intact. Distinct
    from institutional L4 [contained, non-civilizational]
    and from L5 [unknown content, requires L4.5 as
    precondition].

  STATUS: No confirmed positive case in library.
    S3 PATTERN EVIDENCE [v18]: three independent near-cases
    with consistent termination mechanism. Not a confirmed
    case -- termination mechanism is consistent but
    absence of positive case means causal direction is
    not confirmed. Pattern is S3 [E3: three real-world
    independent near-cases; M3: mechanism described +
    confirmed across cases + consistent with ANTI-COHERENCE
    COST ASYMMETRY logic; F2: falsification condition
    stated]. Promote to S4 when positive case confirmed.

  THREE NEAR-CASES [all terminated before transmission
  completed]:
    Athens [~450-380 BCE]:
      Socratic method as institutionalized meta-
      correction. Procedure [dialectic] transmitted not
      conclusion. Cross-domain outputs [Plato, Aristotle,
      geometry, logic] only possible with L4 running.
      Termination: execution of Socrates [399 BCE] =
      correction capacity visible enough to threaten
      political architecture. Destroyed before
      transmission window closed.
    Islamic Golden Age [~800-1100 CE]:
      Translation movement + ijtihad [independent
      reasoning] as institutionalized SUCCESSOR AUTONOMY
      at civilizational scale. Cross-domain synthesis
      [algebra, optics, medicine, philosophy] confirmed.
      Termination: Ash'ari theological capture of ijtihad
      [closing of independent reasoning ~1100 CE].
      Correction monopoly constructed at theological
      scale. Diagnostic Collapse candidate. Recovery
      partial, never restored.
    Scottish Enlightenment [~1740-1790]:
      High-density L4-adjacent node [Hume, Smith, Hutton,
      Black, Watt] in genuine cross-domain intellectual
      friction. Outputs [political economy, geology,
      chemistry, steam engineering] only possible with
      meta-correction running cross-domain.
      Termination: outputs extracted, method abandoned.
      Protestant Reformation pattern at Enlightenment
      scale -- conclusions crossed transmission boundary,
      procedure did not. TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.

  CONSISTENT TERMINATION MECHANISM [across all three]:
    Correction capacity became visible enough to threaten
    extraction architecture. Architecture absorbed or
    destroyed it before transmission closed. Political
    or theological capture in every case.

  NEAR-CASE STRESS-TEST [v18 -- alternative explanations]:
    ALT 1 [Athens]: Socrates killed for personal enemies
      and impiety charge, not systemic correction threat.
      Against: the impiety charge is the mechanism, not
      an alternative. Correction capacity at civilizational
      visibility = institutional threat. The legal vehicle
      is extraction architecture using available instrument.
      That the charge was formally religious does not
      change the structural function. Plato's subsequent
      withdrawal [Republic as political theory, not
      engaged practice] and Aristotle's caution confirm
      the chilling effect on meta-correction as method.
      Termination mechanism holds.
    ALT 2 [Islamic Golden Age]: decline was Mongol
      invasion [1258 Baghdad sack], not theological
      capture.
      Against: Ash'ari capture of ijtihad is documented
      ~1100 CE -- 158 years before the Mongol sack.
      The correction methodology was already closed
      before the military catastrophe. Mongol invasion
      accelerated substrate collapse but was not the
      termination mechanism for L4.5. Two separate
      events; framework claim is about the theological
      capture of ijtihad, not the military collapse.
      Termination mechanism holds independently.
    ALT 3 [Scottish Enlightenment]: outputs were
      transmitted [political economy, geology] -- this
      contradicts TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY failure.
      Against: this is the TRANSMISSION SELECTIVITY
      finding, not a contradiction of it. Conclusions
      transmitted; method did not. Adam Smith's market
      conclusions were transmitted as doctrine; his
      method [empirical observation of labor conditions,
      outputs as truth test] was not transmitted as
      structure. Ricardo and subsequent economists
      transmitted Smith's conclusions as locked frame.
      The outputs crossed the boundary; the procedure
      that generated them did not. This is the
      Protestant Reformation pattern at intellectual
      scale. Termination mechanism holds.
    Stress-test result: all three alternative explanations
      examined. None overturn the consistent termination
      mechanism. S3 pattern evidence confirmed v18.

  INTERNET-ERA POSITIVE CANDIDATE [v18 -- open]:
    Anti-capture architecture requirement [per PREDICTION
    below] raises the question whether distributed
    digital infrastructure constitutes an anti-capture
    instrument. Candidate: open-source / open-access
    ecosystem as distributed correction architecture.
    Wikipedia + FOSS + arXiv + peer-to-peer protocols
    collectively distribute correction capacity across
    nodes that no single political or theological actor
    can monopolize.
    Against: platform capture [GitHub/Microsoft, Reddit
    API closures, Twitter/X centralization] shows
    the architecture is not immune to capture. Each
    node individually capturable; the question is
    whether all nodes simultaneously capturable.
    Current status: not confirmed. Requires: evidence
    of meta-correction [not just information] running
    at civilizational scale with method [not just
    conclusion] transmitting across generations.
    Wikipedia has one generational cycle [~20 years].
    Early evidence: methodology [NPOV + talk page
    dispute resolution] shows some structural
    transmission. Counter-evidence: culture wars on
    Wikipedia suggest method is under sustained
    capture pressure. Track.
    Note: this is the first internet-era candidate
    in the library. Warrants a dedicated case entry
    once one full generational cycle is documentable.

  PREDICTION [falsifiable]:
    L4.5 requires anti-capture architecture as
    precondition. Correction instrument must be
    distributed enough that no single political or
    theological node can monopolize it.
    Current candidate: none confirmed.
    Falsification condition: confirmed case of
    civilizational L4 transmission completing without
    capture termination. If found, upgrade to confirmed
    finding and open L5 prediction window.

  L5 NOTE:
    Content of L5 unpredictable from current evidence.
    Structural signature probably: something that only
    becomes possible when meta-correction is the default
    operating mode of a civilization rather than the
    exception produced by rare high-density nodes under
    favorable conditions. MORAL LEGIBILITY operating at
    civilizational scale [ethics-as-empirical-instrument
    as default not exception] is one candidate property.
    Cannot confirm without L4.5 completing first.
    Honest limit declared. Do not speculate beyond this.

  Cross-ref: ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [confirmed
    v18 -- generation only has to win once; cost
    asymmetry logic predicts why capture always fires
    before transmission closes]; TRANSMISSION
    SELECTIVITY [method vs conclusion distinction
    load-bearing]; PROTESTANT REFORMATION [conclusions
    transmitted, method not -- closest negative

    EXTRACTION CYCLE [termination mechanism in all

  REVISED PURPOSE:
    Operational instrument, not percentage calculator. The question
    is not "what percentage of extraction mechanisms are active?" --
    it is "which weapons are running, in what priority order, and
    what is the lowest-cost counter available?"
    Output format [v22]:
      Active weapon list [ordered by priority: EXTREME → HIGH → MED]
      For each weapon: deploy cost / damage / counter difficulty / tool
      Best available tool for each weapon + tool cost
      Weapons with no LOW-cost counter flagged [structural intervention
        or external resource required before individual action]

  RETAINED FROM v3 ARCHITECTURE [still valid]:
    STEP 1: DECLARE PARAMETERS
      Boundary, population, window. Score meaningless without declared
      parameters -- same system reads differently at different windows.

    STEP 2: BUILD RELEVANT POOL
      Scan full weapons index. For each mechanism:
        RELEVANT = could plausibly be operating given system type,
          scale, window. Enters pool.
        NOT RELEVANT = structurally inapplicable. Exits pool entirely.
          Do not include. Irrelevant mechanisms inflate read falsely.
      Two analysts should agree on pool before reading individually.

    STEP 3: DETERMINE ACTIVE WEAPONS [replaces percentage scoring]
      For each weapon in pool:
        CLEARLY ACTIVE = weapon mechanism operating [full priority weight]
        PROBABLE = mechanism likely but not confirmed [half priority]
        ABSENT = not firing [stays in pool; noted as absent]
      Order active weapons by priority [EXTREME first].

    STEP 4: MATCH TOOLS TO WEAPONS
      For each active weapon: identify best available tool from
        WEAPON→TOOL TABLE [Section Three].
      Note tool cost [LOW/MED/HIGH].
      Flag weapons where no LOW-cost tool available.
      Pre-emptive tools: flag if window still open or already closed.

    STEP 5: DEPLOYMENT DIRECTION CHECK
      For each active weapon, determine direction:
        DEFENSIVE [protecting genuine coherence] = noted; lower priority.
        AGGRESSIVE [suppressing measurement of own extraction] = full priority.
        Direction = net transfer reading, not stated intent.

    STEP 6: ADVERSARIAL CHECK
      For any weapon scored CLEARLY ACTIVE, produce strongest case
        it is absent or defensive. If case holds: revise to PROBABLE.
      For any weapon scored ABSENT, produce strongest case it is present.
        If case holds: revise to PROBABLE.

    STEP 7: OUTPUT FORMAT
      PARAMETERS: [boundary / population / window]
      ACTIVE WEAPONS [ordered by priority]:
        [weapon] | Priority: X | Deploy: X | Damage: X | Counter: X
        Best tool: [tool name] | Tool cost: X | Effectiveness: X
        Defensive or aggressive: [direction]
      ABSENT WEAPONS: [list with one-line reason]
      WEAPONS WITHOUT LOW-COST COUNTER: [list -- structural
        intervention or external resources required]
      AXIS POSITION [WIP 19]: [A1 / A2 / A3 coordinate]
      CONFIDENCE: [high / medium / low]

  CALIBRATION STATUS [WIP -- pending]:
    Tool effectiveness ratings [LOW/MED/HIGH vs each weapon] need
    verification against case library outcomes. Current ratings are
    framework-derived [plausible from mechanism analysis]; not yet
    empirically calibrated.
    Promotion condition: effectiveness ratings verified against minimum
    three confirmed case library entries per weapon where the tool
    was deployed and outcome documented.
    Cases with documented tool deployment: [identify in next session].

    DIAGNOSIS [why most tools show zero rather than partial coverage]:
      This is not evidence the tools are ineffective or untested in
      the world -- it is evidence that case entries were written
      describing mechanisms and outcomes in narrative terms without
      tagging which named tool from the TOOLS INDEX was structurally
      operating. Many existing case entries likely DO depict deployment
      of these tools functionally [e.g. a case where a character
      builds independent measurement before a capture event completes
      is functionally EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD, whether or not the
      case entry uses that exact phrase]. The scan only catches exact-
      name matches; it does not catch functional matches under
      different phrasing.
    REVISED NEXT STEP [replaces "identify in next session" above]:
      Rather than scanning for exact-name matches [done; mostly
      negative], next session should select 2-3 of the zero-coverage
      tools and deliberately re-read existing cases for FUNCTIONAL
      deployment matching the tool's mechanism description, then
      retroactively tag the case with the tool name in a cross-ref
      addition. This is lower-risk than writing new cases from
      scratch, since the case content doesn't change -- only the
      cross-ref tagging is added. Candidates likely to have functional
      matches on re-read: EXTERNAL REFERENCE STANDARD [any case
      involving independent verification before capture --
      [CASE:VILLEPREUX_POWER] aquarium-as-instrument is a strong
      candidate]; CAUSAL RECONSTRUCTION [any axis-reconstruction-
      from-behavioral-record case -- [CASE:DISCO_ELYSIUM] Harry's
      amnesia is a strong candidate, distinct from its existing
      AXIS GROUNDING tag].

  [VERIFICATION NOTE v23-7 -- this session, PROMOTION BAR REVISION,
  added after v23-6 note above; original notes left untouched per
  VERIFICATION WORK GOES IN NEW, NOT OLD]:
    Analyst instruction applied: the three-deployment requirement for
    tool CONFIRMED status is replaced. One confirmed real [non-
    fictional] functional-match deployment is now sufficient for
    CONFIRMED. Additional real-case deployments beyond the first
    strengthen confidence and may surface conflicting effectiveness
    ratings worth investigating, but are not required for the label
    itself. The old "N of 3" framing conflated mechanism-existence
    [settled by one good case] with effectiveness-confidence-across-
    contexts [which benefits from more cases but was never actually
    gating whether the tool's mechanism occurs in the world].

      matches the mechanism precisely: "distinguish instrument failure

      person's own solo, self-initiated, documented act, which is
      what CAG+MI's mechanism specifies.
    Considered two readings before logging anything: [A] an active
      weapon suppresses solo self-correction, making external
      mediation the only path that survives into documented record;
      [B] CAG+MI's own definition describes a solo process that may
      not match how this correction actually occurs in practice.
      Chose B -- nothing found across any of the three searches
      suggested an actor or mechanism actively blocking solo
      correction; what was found was a consistent absence of one
      pattern and a consistent presence of another. Forcing reading A
      would have invented a weapon to explain an absence, which is
      exactly the kind of error this session's earlier corrections
      [S-rating inflation, the CAG+MI fictional-case miscount] were
      already warning against. Logged as WIP 34 Candidate 5 instead,
      explicitly marked as a different KIND of entry than Candidates
      1-4 -- a question about an existing tool's definition, not a
      near-miss weapon or mechanism.
    IMPLICATION FOR WIP 18: this reframes the ninth tool's persistent
      gap. It may not be that a real case hasn't been found YET --
      it may be that CAG+MI's mechanism, as currently written [solo,
      self-initiated], does not match the form this correction takes
      in the real world, where it appears to require external
      mediation to occur and to be documented. If true, the fix is
      not continued case search but a revision to the tool's own
      definition [e.g. adding an explicit external-witness step,
      analogous to how Instrument Confidence Restoration already
      requires "external confirmation," not pure introspection] --
      after which a real case might become findable, or might already
      exist among the near-misses surveyed [the DV/cult literature
      checked this session would fit a revised, dyadic version of
      CAG+MI much better than it fits the current solo version].
      Not resolved here -- flagged as a dedicated open question for
      next session, separate from and prior to further case search.

  SCORING ARCHITECTURE [restored from v3; formalized v22]:

    STEP 1: DECLARE PARAMETERS
      Boundary, population, window. Same as standard reading.
      Score is meaningless without declared parameters -- same
      system scores differently at different windows and
      populations.

    STEP 2: BUILD RELEVANT POOL
      Scan full weapons index. For each mechanism:
        RELEVANT = mechanism could plausibly be operating given
          system type, scale, and window. Enters scored pool.
        NOT RELEVANT = mechanism structurally inapplicable to
          this system type. Exits pool entirely. Do not score
          zero. Irrelevant mechanisms inflate denominator falsely.
      Document pool: list relevant mechanisms, list exited
        mechanisms with one-line reason for exit.
      Pool must be declared before scoring begins. Pool
        selection is the highest-judgment step -- two analysts
        should agree on pool before scoring individually.

    STEP 3: SCORE EACH MECHANISM IN POOL
      CLEARLY PRESENT = full percentage value [TBD per mechanism].
      CLOSE MATCH / PROBABLE = half value.
      ABSENT / NOT FIRING = zero [stays in pool; contributes
        to denominator; scores zero not exit].
      Exit = structurally inapplicable [Step 2 determination].
      Gray zone handling: when presence is ambiguous, apply
        half value and flag for adversarial check [Step 5].

    STEP 4: CALCULATE SCORE
      Score = sum of scored values / sum of relevant pool
        total values [at full presence].
      Output: percentage [0-100%].
        0% = no mechanisms present or none relevant [distinction
          noted: zero-from-absent vs zero-from-no-relevant-pool].
        100% = all relevant mechanisms fully active.
      HIGH PERCENTAGE = BAD [high extraction pressure].
      LOW PERCENTAGE = GOOD [low extraction pressure].
      Score is not standalone -- always paired with named
        mechanism list [which mechanisms are firing and at
        what weight]. Score without mechanism list = number
        without interpretation.

    STEP 5: DEPLOYMENT DIRECTION CHECK
      For each scoring mechanism, determine direction:
        DEFENSIVE [protecting genuine coherence against external
          extraction pressure] = noted in report; score
          adjusted downward by half value. Defensive deployment
          does not count against system at full weight.
        AGGRESSIVE [suppressing measurement of own extraction]
          = scores at full weight.
        DIRECTION = net transfer reading, not stated intent.
      Large institutions across long windows expected to show
        high mechanism deployment regardless of coherence level.
        Diagnostic question: proportion defensive vs aggressive
        within specific window.
      Short window most reliable: high concentration in recent
        short window = active obscuration. Long window baseline
        = structural deployment pattern.

    STEP 6: ADVERSARIAL CHECK
      For any mechanism scored at full value, produce strongest
        case that it is absent or defensive. If adversarial
        case holds: revise to half value, flag contested.
      For any mechanism scored zero, produce strongest case
        that it is present. If adversarial case holds: revise
        to half value, flag contested.
      Contested mechanisms listed separately in output with
        both readings and final value used.

    STEP 7: OUTPUT FORMAT
      PARAMETERS: [boundary / population / window]
      RELEVANT POOL: [list of mechanisms in pool]
      EXITED MECHANISMS: [list with exit reason]
      SCORED MECHANISMS: [mechanism / value / direction /
        contested flag]
      SCORE: [X%]
      ZERO NOTE [if 0%]: zero-from-absent OR zero-from-
        no-relevant-pool [distinction matters]
      NAMED MECHANISM SUMMARY: [which mechanisms are firing
        at what weight -- this is the conceptual resolution
        component; score without this is meaningless]
      THREE-AXIS POSITION [WIP 19]: [A1 / A2 / A3]
      CONFIDENCE: [high / medium / low -- based on evidence
        quality and adversarial check results]

  PERCENTAGE VALUE CALIBRATION [TBD -- dedicated session]:
    Each extraction mechanism in the index requires a
    percentage value calibrated against the case library.
    Calibration procedure [to be formalized in dedicated
    session]:
      [1] Select mechanism to calibrate.
      [2] Find minimum three confirmed cases where mechanism
          is clearly present.
      [3] Find minimum two confirmed cases where mechanism
          is absent.
      [4] Determine what percentage contribution produces
          readings consistent with confirmed case S-ratings
          across both present and absent cases.
      [5] Cross-check: high-S-rating cases with mechanism
          present should score substantially higher than
          same cases without mechanism.
      [6] Record calibrated value in index entry.
    CURRENT STATUS: values TBD. Weapons index has damage range
      and correction potential ratings that provide calibration
      guidance -- highest damage weapons [FC, ICD, MSC,
      Calibration Strip] should carry highest percentage values.
      Exact values pending calibration.
    PLACEHOLDER TIERS [provisional -- use until calibrated]:
      EXTREME / HIGH DAMAGE [FC, ICD, MSC, ECD, ECC, Isolation,
        Inversion of Burden, Discovery Weaponization, Intercession
        Monopoly, Proxy Construction]: 25-35% each [before
        pool normalization].
      MID-HIGH DAMAGE [Rhythm Disruption, Correction Theatre,
        Universalism Trap, Competence Undermining, Churn
        Normalization, Coherence Rent, Love Bombing]: 15-25% each.
      MID DAMAGE [Loaded Language, Consultancy Shield, Sincere
        Delusion, Fertile Lock]: 5-15% each.
      Pool normalization: values sum to 100% across relevant
        pool at full presence. Individual weapon values are
        pre-normalization inputs; final calculation normalizes
        against declared pool.
      Provisional tiers allow partial deployment before full
        calibration. Flag all scores using provisional tiers
        as PROVISIONAL in output.

  INTEGRATION WITH WIP 19 [THREE-AXIS MODEL]:
    Score [WIP 18] maps to Axis 1 position [generation/
      extraction]: high score = left; low score = right.
    This is not a derivation -- it is a cross-check. If
      Axis 1 reads right [net generation] but score is
      high [high extraction pressure]: contradiction flag.
      Either pool selection was wrong or Axis 1 reading
      requires revision. Contradictions are diagnostic,
      not errors to suppress.

  FALSIFICATION CONDITION:
    Scoring procedure falsified if: two analysts following
    identical procedure on same case produce scores more
    than 15 percentage points apart [after pool agreement
    in Step 2]. Disagreement above threshold = procedure
    is not replicable; requires revision before deployment.
    Target: inter-analyst agreement within 10 percentage
    points on any confirmed S3+ case.

  Cross-ref: COHERENCE WEAPON TEST [main theory -- scoring
    procedure is the quantitative output of this test];
    weapons index [all entries -- calibration
    targets]; WIP 19 [THREE-AXIS GEOMETRIC MODEL -- Axis 1
    cross-check]; ANTI-COHERENCE COST ASYMMETRY [high-ratio
    mechanisms confirm cost asymmetry prediction]; CONFIRMED
    STACKS [weapon stack detection -- stack interactions
    require combined scoring note, not simple sum].
